### [1] [The Gribov problem on the horizon: A short guide to the Gribov-Zwanziger framework and its refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10303)
*Antonio D. Pereira*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文综述了Yang-Mills理论中Gribov问题的起源、Gribov-Zwanziger作用量的构造及其含维度二凝聚态的改进，分析了红外传播子与格点模拟的符合性，并讨论了BRST对称性及若干开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gauge fixing在非微扰区域失效（存在Gribov拷贝），导致Faddeev-Popov方法全局不成立，需从规范场空间几何出发理解红外动力学。

Method: 基于Euclidean Yang-Mills理论，通过限制Faddeev-Popov泛函至Gribov区域（首阶Gribov条件），引入辅助场构造Gribov-Zwanziger作用量；进一步加入局域维度二胶子-鬼子凝聚项以恢复对称性并改善红外行为。

Result: 获得红外压低的胶子传播子和增强的鬼子传播子，与格点QCD模拟定性一致；BRST对称性被软破坏但可恢复为形变BRST；预言胶子质量尺度与凝聚能标。

Conclusion: Gribov-Zwanziger框架为理解Yang-Mills红外非微扰结构提供了自洽有效理论基础，但仍面临如物理算符定义、重整化群一致性及与 confinement 机制的严格关联等未解问题。

Abstract: Gauge fixing in Yang-Mills theories encounters a non-perturbative obstruction: distinct gauge-equivalent configurations can satisfy the same gauge condition. These are the so-called Gribov copies and their existence challenges the global validity of the Faddeev-Popov prescription. Addressing this ambiguity motivates a framework that connects the geometry of gauge-field space to infrared dynamics. This mini-review introduces the Gribov problem in Euclidean Yang-Mills theories and traces the construction of the Gribov-Zwanziger action and its refinement through the introduction of dimension-two condensates. We discuss the resulting infrared propagators, their comparison to lattice results, and the role of BRST symmetry, before surveying selected applications and open questions.

</details>


### [2] [Matrix-vector models for the 3D Ising CFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12071)
*Kaichi Mizohata,Tatsuya Seko,Asato Tsuchiya*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类基于球面上最低朗道能级相互作用费米子的三维共形场论（3D CFT）构造方法，特别针对3D Ising CFT，通过引入角动量依赖的相互作用阻尼函数定义变形哈密顿量；证明其可等价表述为基底无关的矩阵–矢量量子力学模型，并进一步积掉费米矢量得到纯矩阵模型；选取高斯型阻尼时，玻色矩阵获得热核传播子，参数ξ控制其宽度；数值上对至多16个费米子系统进行精确对角化，发现临界点处低能谱与3D Ising CFT一致，且该矩阵模型不表现出传统模糊球理论中的UV/IR混合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服传统模糊球场论中UV/IR混合问题，探索更可靠的3D CFT（特别是3D Ising）的非微扰、离散化实现；利用最低朗道能级费米子体系在球面上天然具备共形对称性破缺与临界行为的良好控制特性。

Method: 构建一类由角动量依赖阻尼函数调控的变形哈密顿量；通过正则变换将系统重写为基底无关的矩阵–矢量模型；对费米矢量路径积分得到有效玻色矩阵模型；对高斯型阻尼情形导出热核传播子；采用精确对角化（ED）方法，在N_f ≤ 16的有限尺寸系统中提取临界低能谱。

Result: 证实该类变形模型在临界点处低能激发谱与3D Ising CFT高度吻合；获得具热核传播子结构的矩阵模型实现；明确显示低能谱中无UV/IR混合迹象，区别于标准模糊球CFT构造。

Conclusion: 基于最低朗道能级费米子的矩阵模型提供了一种新型、可控且无UV/IR混合的3D Ising CFT非微扰实现；高斯阻尼参数ξ构成一个可调的共形窗口，支持该模型作为普适临界理论的有效离散化近似。

Abstract: The construction of three-dimensional conformal field theories (3D CFTs) using interacting fermions in the lowest Landau level on a sphere has recently proven remarkably efficient, while it differs from that of conventional field theories on the fuzzy sphere. Focusing on the 3D Ising CFT, we consider a class of models that are defined by deformed Hamiltonians specified by a function which controls how the interaction is damped at high angular momenta, and this class includes the original model. We show that each model in the class can be reformulated in a basis-independent manner as a matrix--vector quantum-mechanical model, and, upon integrating out the fermionic vectors, as a matrix model. We then focus on a Gaussian choice, for which the bosonic matrices acquire a heat-kernel propagator whose width is controlled by a parameter $\xi$. Through exact diagonalization with the number of fermions up to $16$, we find that the low-energy spectra of the deformed Hamiltonians at criticality agree with those in the 3D Ising CFT for a range of $\xi$. We thus obtain matrix-model realizations of the 3D Ising CFT that, in contrast to conventional fuzzy-sphere field theories, show no sign of UV/IR mixing in the low-lying spectrum.

</details>


### [3] [A Free-Field Realization of Boundary Symmetries in Discrete Jackiw--Teitelboim Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10074)
*H.T. Özer,Aytül Filiz*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了sl(2,R) Jackiw–Teitelboim引力中Virasoro–dilaton扇区的一个有限周期性Miura自由场实现，基于单个对数Cartan场，通过离散Miura映射与自由场Poisson括号导出格点自旋-2流和周期性复合dilaton场，自然诱导混合Poisson括号，并验证其与连续Virasoro–dilaton对称性的兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为JT引力边界对称性提供可计算的自由场实现，弥合离散Miura构造与连续共形对称（特别是dilaton共形权-1）之间的鸿沟。

Method: 采用离散Miura映射与自由场Poisson括号，从单一对数Cartan场出发构造格点Virasoro流；dilaton由相同Cartan变量的周期性复合给出，由非齐次差分方程及单值性条件确定；导出诱导混合Poisson括号，分析转移问题、二次伴随不变量及有限傅里叶代数。

Result: 获得显式、自洽的有限周期性自由场实现，其中dilaton自动具有共形权-1，且边界变换律再现JT理论特征；但该Miura Poisson结构未被证明等价于BF型边界电荷代数。

Conclusion: 该构造为JT引力提供了首个基于Miura映射的、具明确Poisson几何结构的离散自由场模型，揭示了dilaton作为Cartan变量周期复合的内在几何起源，但其与规范理论边界代数的对应仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: We investigate a finite-periodic Miura free-field realization of the classical Virasoro--dilaton sector relevant to sl(2,R) Jackiw--Teitelboim (JT) gravity. Starting from a single logarithmic Cartan field, we use the established discrete Miura map and its free-field Poisson bracket to construct the lattice spin-two current. The dilaton is then realized as a periodic composite of the same Cartan variables, selected by an inhomogeneous difference equation and its monodromy condition. This construction yields an explicit induced mixed Poisson bracket without postulating an independent dilaton bracket. We examine the associated transfer problem and quadratic adjoint invariant, and test the finite Fourier algebra against the continuous Virasoro--dilaton transformation, in which the dilaton has conformal weight minus one. The construction supplies a free-field counterpart of the JT boundary transformation laws; it does not by itself identify the Miura Poisson structure with the canonical BF boundary-charge algebra.

</details>


### [4] [Integrable AdS$_2$ branes in mixed flux AdS$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10120)
*Davide Bonomi,Volker Schomerus*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展Dekel–Oz可积D-膜至含Wess–Zumino项的混合通量背景，并将此前仅适用于纯玻色子情形的混合通量结果推广至完整超弦框架；统一理解电-磁对偶、边界可积性与全息界面之间的关系。

Method: 基于D3-膜Dirac–Born–Infeld + Chern–Simons作用量分析世界体积电/磁场稳定性；利用费米边界项抵消要求引入特定闭合2-形式以修正边界条件；构造场依赖的反射矩阵实现Lax联络的边界胶合；构建双行单值性矩阵并验证其泊松对易性以确立无穷守恒荷。

Result: 证实AdS₂×S²包裹D3-膜在AdS₃×S³×T⁴混合通量下构成超coset σ模型的可积边界；世界体积场由SL(2,ℝ)电-磁对偶旋转从WZW模型结果导出；边界作用量中闭2-形式贡献精确复现该场构型；获得满足相容性条件的场依赖反射矩阵；双行单值性矩阵生成无穷对易守恒量，确立经典可积性；给出AdS₃/CFT₂中对应共形界面的全息解释。

Conclusion: 混合NSNS/RR通量下的半BPS D3-膜在AdS₃×S³×T⁴中诱导可积开弦动力学，其边界结构由电-磁对偶与Lax可积性共同约束；该结果为AdS₃/CFT₂中带界面的二维共形场论提供了严格可积模型基础。

Abstract: We show that half-BPS D3-branes wrapping AdS$_2\times$S$^2$ in AdS$_3\times$S$^3\times$T$^4$ with mixed NSNS and RR flux define integrable boundary conditions for the supercoset sigma model of the Green--Schwarz superstring. This extends the integrable D-branes of Dekel and Oz to backgrounds with a Wess--Zumino term, and previous bosonic results for mixed flux to the full superstring. Using the D3-brane action, we find that the electric and magnetic worldvolume fields that stabilise the branes at generic positions are obtained from those of the AdS$_2\times$S$^2$ branes of the WZW model by the same $SL(2,\mathbb{R})$ rotation that maps the pure NSNS background to the mixed flux one, acting on the D3-brane as electric--magnetic duality. On the worldsheet, cancelling the fermionic boundary terms requires introducing a specific closed two-form in the boundary action, and its contribution to the bosonic boundary conditions reproduces precisely these worldvolume fields. We then construct field-dependent reflection matrices that lift the boundary conditions to gluing conditions for the Cagnazzo--Zarembo Lax connection and describe displacements along geodesics normal to the brane. The resulting double-row monodromy matrix generates infinitely many conserved charges, which establishes the classical integrability of the open superstring for any mixture of fluxes. In AdS$_3$/CFT$_2$, these branes are expected to be dual to conformal interfaces in a deformation of the symmetric product orbifold of T$^4$.

</details>


### [5] [Liberation in the Heavy Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11594)
*Sridip Pal,Gordon Rogelberg*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了共形场论（CFT）中相同标量原初算符的四点函数在原初算符维数Δ增大时的行为，定义了‘解放态’（liberated）——即其增长速率与广义自由场（GFF）四点函数指数一致；证明了在交叉对称点处‘解放’等价于轻谱稀疏，并进一步给出不同稀疏性条件（如低扭度态稀疏、主扭度间隙大于√2Δ等）下解放区域的精确刻画，同时指出二维情形下Virasoro原初算符解放需中心荷c比Δ更快增长，并由此强化了大c环面配分函数的普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解大维数极限下CFT关联函数的渐近行为，特别是与AdS中重粒子沿测地线传播的对应关系，以及稀疏谱对全息对偶中信息恢复和纠缠熵结构的影响。

Method: 基于交叉对称性、OPE展开与扭度谱分析，结合共形块渐近行为（尤其在z, z̄→0或z+z̄→1极限）、鞍点分析及二维Virasoro代数表示理论，系统刻画四点函数在Δ→∞下的指数增长律及其对谱密度的依赖。

Result: 1) 四点函数在z=z̄=1/2处解放 ⇔ 轻谱稀疏；2) 若低扭度态亦稀疏，则解放区域为z+z̄<1−ε∪z+z̄>1+ε（显式带隙）；3) 若主扭度间隙>√2Δ，则处处解放；4) 扭度截断<Δ时，自旋GFF双复合态在双轻锥极限附近不解放；5) 2D中Virasoro原初算符解放要求c≫Δ；6) 此框架导出大c环面配分函数的更强普适性。

Conclusion: CFT四点函数的大维数渐近行为由低能谱的稀疏性严格控制，‘解放’是谱稀疏性的可测信号，为全息对偶中AdS几何重建与量子引力信息论提供了精细判据；二维结果凸显了Virasoro代数对大Δ物理的刚性约束。

Abstract: We study CFT four-point functions of identical scalar primaries as their dimension $\Delta$ grows for fixed $0<z, \bar z<1$, and call them liberated if they grow at the same exponential rate as those of a generalized free field (GFF), as is the case for heavy free particles propagating along geodesics in AdS. We show that the correlator is liberated at the crossing symmetric point if and only if the light spectrum is sparse, and that liberation then holds wherever $z$ and $\bar z$ lie on the same side of $1/2$. If the low twist states are also sparse, liberation holds everywhere except in an explicit band around $z+\bar z=1$. In particular, if the primary twist gap lies above $\sqrt{2}\Delta$, then the correlator is liberated everywhere. For twist cutoffs below $\Delta$, composites of two spinning generalized free fields fail to be liberated in a region that fills the band near the double lightcone limit. In two dimensions, Virasoro primaries cannot be liberated unless the central charge grows faster than $\Delta$. The same framework sharpens the universality of large-$c$ torus partition functions of two-dimensional CFTs.

</details>


### [6] [Semiclassical Asymptotics of Entanglement in Torus-Link States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11961)
*Vikash Ganwariya,Simran Sain,Siddharth Dwivedi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了(2+1)维SU(2) Chern–Simons理论中Td,dn环面链对应的拓扑量子态的纠缠熵，在k→∞半经典极限下，通过分析约化密度矩阵矩的渐近行为，发现主导贡献来自左右边界区域，体部贡献被指数压制；结果以Hurwitz zeta函数表示，并依赖k mod n的剩余类；大d极限下熵退化为由左右边界归一化权重决定的二元熵；且k→∞与d→∞两个迭代极限可交换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建多体拓扑量子场论中多部分纠缠的自然模型，利用环面链补空间态研究Chern–Simons理论中拓扑纠缠的结构性质及其半经典极限行为。

Method: 将可积表示空间分解为左边缘、右边缘和体部区域，运用驻相法计算各区域对约化密度矩阵矩的渐近贡献，并提取纠缠熵的主导阶行为。

Result: 纠缠熵在k→∞极限下由左右边缘主导，表达式含Hurwitz zeta函数，显含k mod n依赖；大d极限下收敛于二元熵；k→∞与d→∞极限可交换。

Conclusion: 拓扑纠缠熵的半经典结构由边界自由度主导，体部不贡献主导项；其模n周期性揭示了量子群表示论中根单位的深层作用；多体极限与大k极限的可交换性表明该纠缠结构具有稳健的渐近一致性。

Abstract: We investigate the entanglement entropy of topological quantum states associated with $T_{d,\,dn}$ torus links in (2+1)-dimensional SU(2) Chern--Simons theory at level $k$. These links consist of $d$ components with pairwise linking number $n$, and the corresponding link-complement states provide a natural setting to study multipartite entanglement in topological quantum field theories. We analyze the semiclassical limit $k\to\infty$ of the entanglement entropy by deriving the asymptotic behavior of the moments of the reduced density matrix. To achieve this, we decompose the integrable-representation space into left-edge, right-edge, and bulk regions and employ the stationary-phase method to evaluate their respective contributions. We find that the leading asymptotic behavior is governed entirely by the two edge regions, while the bulk contribution is asymptotically suppressed. The resulting expression for the limiting entanglement entropy is given in terms of Hurwitz zeta functions and exhibits a dependence on the residue class of $k$ modulo $n$. We further investigate the dependence of the limiting entanglement entropy on the winding parameter $n$ and the number of link components $d$. In the large-$d$ limit, the entropy reduces to a binary entropy determined by two normalized weights associated with the left and right edges. Finally, we show that the two iterated limits, $k\to\infty$ and $d\to\infty$, commute, reproducing the result obtained previously by taking the large-party limit first.

</details>


### [7] [Kasner Exponents from the Bouncing Singularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31699)
*Hyunwoo Oh*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究全息CFT热两点函数如何探测黑洞奇点，发现其在临界时间t_c处的奇异性指数b_t由奇点附近的Kasner指数决定，并给出了适用于AdS_{d+1}平面黑膜的显式公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解黑洞内部奇点对边界CFT可观测量（如热两点函数）的影响，特别是通过全息对偶将奇点几何性质编码到边界关联函数的奇异性中。

Method: 利用全息原理，分析从奇点反射的高能波对大频率推迟关联函数的贡献；结合Kasner奇点几何与测地线反弹行为，在1/N领头阶下推导两点函数在t→t_c时的奇异性指数b_t。

Result: 得到普适公式b_t=(d−1)max((p_i−p_t)/(1−p_t),1/2)，适用于AdS_{d+1}平面黑膜；该公式复现了Čeplak–Valach对特殊黑膜提出的经验关系，并显著优于其推广形式，与多数数值数据高度吻合。

Conclusion: 奇点处的各向异性（由Kasner指数刻画）直接决定边界热关联函数的奇异性结构，证实了全息对偶中时空奇点与量子场论奇异行为之间的深刻对应。

Abstract: Thermal two-point functions of holographic CFTs can probe the black hole singularity. When a bouncing geodesic exists, the correlator at leading order in $1/N$ develops a singularity $G(t, \vec{x}=0) \propto (t_c-t)^{-2\Delta+b_t}$, where $\Delta$ is the scaling dimension of the operator. We show that the additional exponent $b_t$ is determined by the Kasner exponents near the singularity. From the wave reflected off the singularity, which gives an exponentially small term in the large-frequency retarded correlator, we derive the formula $b_t=(d-1) \max \left( \frac{p_i-p_t}{1-p_t}, \frac{1}{2} \right)$ for planar black branes in AdS$_{d+1}$, where $p_t$ and $p_i$ are the Kasner exponents of the time and spatial directions. We compare our formula with the numerical values of Čeplak and Valach. It reproduces the relation that they proposed for a special class of black branes. For more general black branes, where no formula had been found, our formula agrees with most of their numerical data and fits them much better than the extension of their relation. We also give predictions for $b_t$ in theories where the Kasner exponents are known, but $b_t$ has not been computed.

</details>


### [8] [Baby Universes Nucleate by Hiding Observers from the Boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10112)
*Sergio E. Aguilar-Gutierrez,Takato Mori*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究双尺度Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev（SYK）模型耦合系统，引入含观测者（表征为重粒子）的态；发现当粒子落入虫洞时，虫洞持续增长，而哈密顿量弦的稀有产物可抵消粒子弦，导致闭合婴儿宇宙携载观测者分离，空虫洞恢复有限离散能级；该一阶相变中空虫洞获得的能量补偿观测者熵，潜热等于温度乘以该熵；Krylov复杂度（表征虫洞长度）在相变处下降，从而在边界上探测婴儿宇宙诞生；同时边界丢失关于观测者微观态的Holevo信息，该信息由婴儿宇宙携带。


<details>

Abstract: Stanley Deser was Ancell Professor Emeritus at Brandeis University and Visiting Professor at the California Institute of Technology. He was a world-renowned theoretical physicist whose accomplishments include the Arnowitt-Deser-Misner (ADM) formulation of general relativity, supergravity with Zumino, the dynamics of flat space with Jackiw and 't Hooft and many other contributions to the quantum theory of gravity. In the 1980s he was instrumental in securing National Science Foundation approval of the LIGO gravitational wave experiment against much opposition. He was awarded the Einstein Medal in 2015 for important contributions to general relativity, in particular the development of the ADM formalism. Other awards include: The Dannie Heineman Prize 1994; Fellow of the National Academy of Sciences 1994; Honorary Member of the Torino Academy of Sciences 2001; Member of the American Academy of Arts and Sciences 1979, Fellow of the American Institute of Physics.

A key technical point is that the uniform asymptotic expansion of the mode generating function cannot be used in full; a part must be retained unexpanded, and an integral representation of the digamma function is used to carry out the analytic continuation. This limit shows that a finite, physically meaningful renormalized vacuum energy can be defined for a massive scalar field in the background of a Coulomb source. For $m\to\infty$ it vanishes in accordance with the normalization condition, while for $m\to0$ it diverges, so that the present calculation does not apply to a massless field, pointing to the need of another vacuum state in that case.

Abstract: Rotating black holes can amplify waves that scatter off them, a process known as superradiance that fluid analogues bring into the laboratory. Separating the horizon's role from dissipation in the surrounding fluid remains difficult. Here we realise a rotating analogue black hole in a lattice fluid: a shallow-water vortex whose horizon is set by a topographic sill, so that the only sink lies behind a supercritical layer that lets nothing out. We measure superradiance as energy extraction: the flux at the horizon is outward for co-rotating waves whose pattern rotates more slowly than the horizon, and inward for faster waves, counter-rotating waves and a non-rotating hole. A scan over three lattice viscosities separates the extraction at the horizon, which is inviscid, from that of the viscous fluid inside the corotation radius: well below the band edge the horizon is the absorber; near the edge the viscous share is half at the operating viscosity and vanishes at the lowest. A drain-anchored horizon, as in water-tank experiments, gives the same signs. Our analysis gives insight into the mechanism of superradiance and offers a cheap, flexible platform for designing analogue-gravity experiments.

Abstract: In conventional quantum mechanics, the Hamiltonian is required to be Hermitian, ensuring real energy eigenvalues. Certain non-Hermitian Hamiltonians can also possess entirely real spectra when they are invariant under the combined action of parity ($\mathcal P$) and time reversal ($\mathcal T$). Such Hamiltonians are called unbroken parity--time ($\mathcal{PT}$)-symmetric. We conjecture that, in a string-inspired scenario, a repulsive phase of gravity may be due to a $\mathcal{PT}$-symmetric gravitational theory, characterised by anomalous Chern-Simons terms, coupled to axion fields. The conjecture is based on the behaviour of the chiral anomalous couplings of these axions to electromagnetic potentials. Within the Exact Renormalisation Group (ERG) framework in the modified local potential approximation, we argue that singularities in the ERG beta functions for such couplings may indicate an infrared phase transition. At the energy scales of these singularities, we conjecture that the field theory transitions from the Hermitian phase to a ${\mathcal P}{\mathcal T}$-- symmetric theory, of gravity, characterised by a repulsive Netwon's ``constant''. This may induce the acceleration observed in the Universe at late eras, without the need for a dark--energy component.

Abstract: We investigate the validity of the generalized second law (GSL) of thermodynamics on the cosmological apparent horizon in $f(Q)$ symmetric teleparallel gravity, using the Wald-type entropy prefactor $Af_Q/4G$ together with a correction term derived directly from the modified Friedmann equations. We apply this formalism to three observationally viable $f(Q)$ models --- power-law, exponential, and logarithmic. We find that the GSL is satisfied throughout cosmic history for all three models, and relate this robustness to a general, theory-independent structural condition: for ordinary pressureless matter obeying the null energy condition, GSL validity reduces to the sign of $\dot H$, i.e., to the absence of a phantom crossing in the total effective expansion history. We show that Leibniz's principle of sufficient reason (PSR) eliminates the infinite arbitrariness and transforms the predicted nature of $N$-body universes; they are all shown to be unexpectedly interesting. An even greater change is enforced if one asks what principle could determine the number of particles in the universe. The PSR suggests it can only be infinite. We suggest that, in such a universe, correlation could replace causation while still giving the appearance that each state has been created by dynamical evolution from the past, whereas its actual explanation may lie entirely within the present state. While much work remains to develop these ideas into a viable theory, we are confident that it advances the case for holism.

Abstract: We study the quasinormal modes (QNMs) of a minimally coupled massless scalar field on the exact spinning Morris-Thorne wormhole. The problem is posed on the complete two-ended geometry, whose asymptotic rest frames rotate relative to each other. After extracting the axis behaviour and the outgoing phases at both infinities, we compactify the radial coordinate and construct a quadratic eigenvalue problem by two-dimensional Chebyshev collocation. The analytic background yields exact constraints on the spectrum. At fixed azimuthal number $m$, the frequencies $\widehat{\omega}=r_0\omega$ are mirror symmetric about $\operatorname{Re}\widehat{\omega}=3\pi jm/2$, where $r_0$ is the throat radius and $j=J/r_0^2$ is the dimensionless angular momentum. Each multipole family depends on $m$ and $j$ only through $mj$ and three angular Chebyshev polynomials represent exactly the multipoles $L=|m|,|m|+1,|m|+2$. A conserved flux confines nonthreshold real quasinormal frequencies to the superradiant window $0<\omega/(m\Omega_-)<1$, where $\Omega_-=3\pi J/r_0^3$. For the sampled sectors $m=1,2,3$ and spins up to $j=19$, every converged frequency is damped and lies outside this window. Independent integration verifies the low-lying modes, while Kerr spectra provide a separate benchmark for the two-dimensional solver. A limiting radial equation explains why the computed mode frequencies approach the upper edge of the superradiant window with frequency offsets proportional to $(mj)^{-1/2}$ at large $mj$. A complete stability assessment requires further analysis of the unresolved scalar spectrum and of perturbations of the coupled metric-matter system.

Bayesian evidence, computed relative to $\Lambda$CDM, shows that the model is favoured by background data in several cases, with moderate support for one realisation, whereas the inclusion of $f\sigma_8$ drives $\nu\to1$ and decisively disfavours the additional freedom, highlighting the importance of growth data in testing such extensions.

Abstract: Gauge freedom in numerical relativity complicates the interpretation of simulation coordinates and the comparison of different descriptions of the same spacetime. For a spinning puncture, the method provides an approximate identification with a Kerr background despite departures from Kerr in the numerical spacetime. We use this identification to construct curvature perturbations and evolve them with the Teukolsky equation, obtaining waveforms that closely match those extracted from the full nonlinear simulation. The DUNE Near Detector (ND) is also sensitive to short-baseline oscillations arising from mixing with an eV-scale sterile neutrino. In this paper, we present a sensitivity study for sterile-neutrino oscillations in the $3+1$ framework using three complementary event samples in the simulated DUNE ND: highly elastic $\nu_\mu$ charged-current (CC), highly elastic $\nu_e$ CC, and neutrino-electron elastic-scattering ($\nu+e$) events. These highly exclusive samples are chosen to reduce neutrino-interaction and flux uncertainties: the low-hadronic-energy CC selections enhance the correlation between reconstructed lepton energy and neutrino energy, while the precisely calculable $\nu+e$ cross section provides an independent constraint on the neutrino flux. Residual interaction-model dependence remains in the CC samples. The statistical analysis considers $|U_{e4}|^2$, $|U_{\mu4}|^2$, $|U_{\tau4}|^2$, and $\Delta m^2$, with sensitivity results presented as fixed-$\Delta m^2$ slices for $|U_{\tau4}|^2=0$. The strongest sensitivity occurs for $4 \lesssim \Delta m^2 \lesssim 10~\mathrm{eV}^2$, where oscillations produce resolvable distortions of the reconstructed-energy spectra. For one year of on-axis forward-horn-current running, the 90\% confidence-level sensitivity reaches approximately $|U_{\mu4}|^2 \simeq 0.01$ and $|U_{e4}|^2 \simeq 0.015$, depending on the value of the other mixing parameter.

Abstract: The precise measurement of neutrino properties is among the highest priorities in fundamental particle physics, involving many experimental and theoretical efforts worldwide. Since the experiments rely on the interactions of neutrinos in the nuclear environment, the planned advances in the scope and precision of these experiments require a commensurate effort in understanding and modeling the hadronic and nuclear physics of these interactions, as incorporated in neutrino event generators. This modeling is essential to every phase of the experimental analyses, and the theoretical uncertainties play an important role in interpreting experimental results. In this Review of current challenges, we summarize the open questions in neutrino-nucleus interactions at GeV-scale energies, and discuss in detail the potential impacts of these unknowns on the measurement of neutrino properties, using the determination of oscillation parameters as a central example. After an Executive Summary and a concise Overview of the issues, we address how the neutrino event generators treat these challenges, how electron-nucleus interactions can help understanding these challenges and the important unanswered questions for each underlying physics process---from quasi-elastic to deep-inelastic scattering. Across every topic, we find that current challenges can be met only through active support and collaboration among specialists in strong interactions and electroweak physics, including theorists and experimentalists from both the nuclear and high-energy physics communities.

</details>

In the $K\eta^{\prime}$ system, a resonant contribution with quantum numbers $J^{P}=1^{-}$ is observed for the first time and is denoted as the $K_1^*(1870)$ meson. Its statistical significance is $11.0\sigma$, and its mass and width are measured to be $M=(1869.0 \pm 14.6\pm 18.6$) MeV/$c^{2}$ and $\Gamma=(167.3 \pm 34.0\pm 24.2$) MeV, respectively. The branching fraction of the $\chi_{c1}\to K^{\mp}K_1^*(1870)^{\pm}\to K^{+}K^{-}\eta^{\prime}$ decay chain is determined to be $(1.48 \pm 0.30\pm 0.33)\times10^{-5}$. The branching fraction of the $\chi_{c1}\to K^{+}K^{-}\eta^{\prime}$ decay is measured to be $(7.90 \pm 0.48)\times10^{-4}$. Several intermediate scalar, vector, and tensor states are required to provide a good description of the data. These results provide new input for understanding strange-meson spectroscopy and investigating possible hybrid-state candidates.

### [87] [Machine learning assisted $ν/\barν$ discrimination in atmospheric neutrino events for CPV sensitivity in a 20-kton liquid scintillator detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11474)
*Dek Hao Liew,Mohammad Nizam,Shu Tian Eu,Siew Yan Hoh*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了在无磁JUNO型液闪探测器中，利用XGBoost等机器学习方法基于事件级可观测量（如inelasticity、中子多重数、带电轻子能量）统计分离ν与ν̄事例，从而提升大气中微子CP破坏测量灵敏度的可行性；结果表明AUC≈0.83，CPV显著性在NO/IO下分别从1.49σ/1.14σ提升至1.96σ/1.61σ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JUNO类无磁液闪探测器无法通过磁场区分ν与ν̄事例，导致其对大气中微子CP破坏的敏感度受限；需发展不依赖电荷识别的统计分离方法以恢复部分灵敏度。

Method: 使用GENIE（Honda通量）生成ν_μ与ν_e CC事例，经OscProb（含地球物质效应与NuFIT参数）振荡重权；模拟探测器响应（能谱展宽、方向分辨率、中子多重数）；训练Logistic回归、XGBoost与MLP分类器，输入特征为inelasticity、中子多重数与带电轻子能量；采用泊松似然函数结合系统误差参数与5个振荡参数3σ先验边缘化，评估200 kt·yr曝光下的CPV灵敏度。

Result: XGBoost在ν_μ与ν_e样本上AUC≈0.83；CP破坏灵敏度在平方根近似下，正常质量序（NO）由1.49σ提升至1.96σ，倒置质量序（IO）由1.14σ提升至1.61σ。

Conclusion: 基于机器学习的统计ν/ν̄分离可在电荷盲探测器中部分恢复CP破坏测量能力，为JUNO大气中微子物理提供新路径，后续需纳入更精确的探测器响应建模与系统误差处理。

Abstract: Atmospheric neutrinos span a broad range of energies and baselines, offering a complementary probe of leptonic CP violation. However, a JUNO-like liquid scintillator detector without a magnetic field cannot directly separate neutrino and antineutrino interactions, reducing CP violation sensitivity. This study investigates whether machine learning based statistical separation can improve CP violation sensitivity in a 20-kton detector. $\nu_{\mu}$ and $\nu_e$ charged current events are generated with GENIE (using Honda flux) in a JUNO-like detector geometry and are reweighted using OscProb three flavor oscillation probabilities incorporating Earth matter effects and NuFIT parameters. Detector response is approximated by smearing lepton and hadronic energies, neutrino direction, and neutron multiplicity. Logistic regression, XGBoost, and multilayer perceptron (MLP) classifiers are trained on inelasticity, neutron multiplicity, and charged lepton energy, with XGBoost selected for the analysis. CP violation sensitivity is evaluated for a 200 kton-year exposure using a Poisson likelihood with systematic nuisance parameters and marginalization over five oscillation parameters within their respective $3\sigma$ ranges. XGBoost achieves an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) of approximately 0.83 for both flavor samples. Under the square root approximation, peak CP violation sensitivity improves from 1.49 $\sigma$ to 1.96 $\sigma$ for normal mass ordering and from 1.14 $\sigma$ to 1.61 $\sigma$ for inverted mass ordering. Machine learning based statistical neutrino-antineutrino separation can recover part of the CP violation sensitivity lost in charge blind detectors. These preliminary results motivate further studies incorporating validated detector response and systematic uncertainties.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Using a sample of $(2712.4 \pm 14.3) \times 10^{6}$ $\psi(3686)$ events collected with the BESIII detector at the BEPCII collider, we report the first observation and measurement of the branching fractions for the decays $\chi_{cJ}\to \phi \omega \eta$ and $\chi_{cJ} \to \phi \omega \eta^{\prime}$ ($J = 0,1,2$) with statistical significance greater than $5\sigma$. The branching fractions are determined to be $\mathcal B(\chi_{c0} \to \phi \omega \eta) = (5.43 \pm 0.23 \pm 0.52) \times 10^{-4}$, $\mathcal B(\chi_{c1} \to \phi \omega \eta) = (2.51 \pm 0.14 \pm 0.24) \times 10^{-4}$ and $\mathcal B(\chi_{c2} \to \phi \omega \eta) = (3.03 \pm 0.15 \pm 0.28) \times 10^{-4}$ for the $\eta$ mode, and $\mathcal B(\chi_{c0} \to \phi \omega \eta^{\prime}) = (1.23 \pm 0.23 \pm 0.15) \times 10^{-4}$, $\mathcal B(\chi_{c1} \to \phi \omega \eta^{\prime}) = (1.09 \pm 0.17 \pm 0.13) \times 10^{-4}$ and $\mathcal B(\chi_{c2} \to \phi \omega \eta^{\prime}) = (0.98 \pm 0.17 \pm 0.11) \times 10^{-4}$ for the $\eta^{\prime}$ mode, where the first uncertainties are statistical and the second systematic.

### [89] [Measurements of $C\!P$ violation with charm hadrons at Belle and Belle II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11957)
*Jaeyoung Kim*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文综述了Belle与Belle II实验在粲强子衰变中对CP破坏的最新搜寻结果，涵盖多种两体与三体衰变道（包括D介子与粲重子），利用Run 1 Belle II数据（428 fb⁻¹）及Belle数据（980 fb⁻¹），测得的所有电荷共轭衰变不对称性均与CP守恒一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验标准模型在粲夸克能区的CP破坏预言（极小），并探索可能的新物理信号；同时验证U-自旋、同位旋等对称性在粲物理中的适用性。

Method: 基于Belle II Run 1数据（428 fb⁻¹）与Belle数据（980 fb⁻¹），采用电荷共轭衰变率不对称性A_CP ≡ [Γ(D→f) − Γ(\bar{D}→\bar{f})]/[Γ(D→f) + Γ(\bar{D}→\bar{f})]作为CP破坏探针；对三体末态使用全运动学拟合与振幅分析；对粲重子衰变引入包容性粲味标记（inclusive charm-flavor tagging）以提升信噪比。

Result: 所有测量的A_CP值——包括D⁺_(s)→ηh⁺、D→ππ、D⁰→K_S⁰K_S⁰、D⁰→π⁺π⁻π⁰、Ξ_c⁺→Σ⁺h⁺h⁻、Λ_c⁺→ph⁺h⁻（h=π,K）——均与零在统计与系统误差范围内一致；首次给出Ξ_c⁺与Λ_c⁺三体衰变的单个衰变道A_CP；D→ππ分析提供了同位旋结构的互补检验；K_S⁰K_S⁰通道利用Belle数据提升了精度。

Conclusion: 当前精度下未发现显著CP破坏信号，所有结果与标准模型预期（A_CP ∼ 10⁻³–10⁻⁴）及CP守恒一致；U-自旋对称性在粲重子衰变中亦未见破缺迹象；后续更高统计量数据将提升敏感度至O(10⁻⁴)量级。

Abstract: Recent searches for $C\!P$ violation in charm-hadron decays at Belle and Belle II are presented. The measurements cover $D^+_{(s)}\to\eta h^+$, $D\to\pi\pi$, $D^0\to K_S^0K_S^0$, and $D^0\to\pi^+\pi^-\pi^0$, together with the singly Cabibbo-suppressed baryon decays $\Xi_c^+\to\Sigma^+h^+h^-$ and $\Lambda_c^+\to ph^+h^-$, where $h=\pi,K$. The Belle II analyses use the Run~1 data sample of $428\,\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$, supplemented by $980\,\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$ of Belle data for the $K_S^0K_S^0$ measurements. We report preliminary results for the $\eta h^+$ modes, complementary tests of the isospin structure of $D\to\pi\pi$, and an application of inclusive charm-flavor tagging. The first individual decay-rate asymmetries in these three-body charmed-baryon modes also provide tests of $U$-spin symmetry. All reported asymmetries are consistent with $C\!P$ symmetry within the present precision.

</details>


### [90] [Beam-beam effects on measurements at electron-positron colliders: LEP neutrino counting and Higgs factories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12265)
*Brendon Madison*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文介绍了FeeBL新模拟框架，用于精确研究电子-正电子对撞机中的束束电磁相互作用，修正了LEP时代Bhabha散射与束偏转模拟中的系统偏差，改进了中微子数Nν测量精度，并修正了多个QED对产生过程的建模错误；同时评估了束流去极化、亮度谱及碰撞能量单色化（尤其对Higgs工厂）的物理效应与技术权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升电子-正电子对撞机（如LEP、ILC、FCC-ee、HALHF）中束束相互作用建模精度，以支撑高精度电弱参数（如Nν）、Higgs物理及背景估计等关键测量。

Method: 开发Fast e±e± Beam and Luminosity (FeeBL) 框架，基于解析场积分与自洽粒子跟踪结合的方法，系统比对LEP实验数据、ILC/HALHF理论预测及FCC-ee设计参数；修正Breit-Wheeler、Bethe-Heitler与Landau-Lifshitz过程的数值实现；引入刚性高斯束重叠模型分析能量单色化。

Result: 1) 重现LEP Bhabha散射解析结果，发现既有束偏转模拟存在~2%系统偏差；2) 修正后Nν = 2.9965 ± 0.0040（原为2.9963 ± 0.0074）；3) 修正三类QED对产生建模错误，影响探测器本底预测；4) 束去极化与亮度谱与ILC/HALHF一致；5) 线性对撞机可实现亚MeV能量峰宽（代价是亮度显著下降），FCC-ee需约10 m水平色散区才能达≈1 MeV峰宽。

Conclusion: FeeBL提供了更高保真度的束束相互作用模拟工具，揭示并修正了若干长期存在的建模偏差，对LEP电弱精密测量、未来Higgs工厂的本底控制与能量分辨率优化具有关键指导意义；FCC-ee的能量单色化能力受限于其环形结构，需专门色散设计。

Abstract: Beam-beam electromagnetic interactions affect the luminosity, collision energy, polarization, and particle backgrounds relevant to precision measurements at electron-positron colliders. We investigate these effects using Fast $e^{\pm}e^{\pm}$ Beam and Luminosity (FeeBL), a new beam-beam simulation framework, and compare its predictions with published results from LEP and proposed future colliders. We reproduce the analytical field-integration calculations of LEP Bhabha scattering and identify an approximately 2% bias in previously published beam-deflection simulations. Applying the corresponding luminosity correction and a revised uncertainty assessment motivated by the observed agreement among the four LEP experiments yields ($N_\nu = 2.9965 \pm 0.0040$), compared with the previously reported ($2.9963 \pm 0.0074$). The revised uncertainty is conditional on an assumed common rescaling of experiment specific uncertainties and motivates further investigation of the original uncertainty budgets. We identify and correct implementation errors in the modeling of Breit-Wheeler, Bethe-Heitler, and Landau-Lifshitz pair production, with implications for detector backgrounds at Higgs factories. Simulated beam depolarization and luminosity spectra are compared with existing ILC and HALHF predictions. Finally, we investigate collision energy monochromatization for direct Higgs production, obtaining sub-MeV collision energy peak widths in exploratory linear collider configurations at substantial luminosity cost, while finding significantly weaker narrowing for the FCC-ee configurations studied. We derive a rigid Gaussian beam overlap model and estimate that FCC-ee needs roughly 10 m of horizontal only dispersion to reach widths of $\approx1~\mathrm{MeV}$.

</details>


### [91] [Search for Dark Photon Dark Matter at 74--159 $μ$eV/$c^2$ Using Broadband Cryogenic Receivers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12774)
*Hiroki Takeuchi,Shunsuke Adachi,Shunki Endo,Keigo Hanimura,Michinori Hirose,Shunsuke Honda,Yuki Sakurai,Junya Suzuki,Osamu Tajima*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文通过低温K波段和Ka波段接收器结合宽带谱仪，在74–159 μeV/c²（18–38.5 GHz）范围内搜寻暗光子暗物质（DPDM）在金属表面转化产生的光子信号，未发现显著信号，给出了目前最严格的动能混合参数χ的95%置信度上限，较先前结果提升超一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验暗光子作为冷暗物质候选者的可行性，特别是其通过动能混合与标准模型光子耦合并在金属表面转化为可观测微波光子的机制。

Method: 利用低温K-和Ka-波段接收器与宽带谱仪，在74–159 μeV/c²频段内进行高灵敏度窄带扫描；积分时间达4.8×10⁵ s/频点；假设DPDM构成全部本地暗物质密度，并建模其在金属表面的Primakoff型转换过程。

Result: 在99.8%搜索区间内未发现统计显著信号；获得χ < (3.1–17)×10⁻¹²（95% CL）的上限；在74–110 μeV/c²区间比前人结果提升>10倍，在110–159 μeV/c²区间为首次直接限制，且比宇宙学约束低约两个数量级。

Conclusion: 该实验显著压缩了轻质量暗光子暗物质的参数空间，尤其在亚百μeV质量区间，为未来轴子类与矢量暗物质实验提供了关键基准与技术范式。

Abstract: Dark photons are hypothetical massive vector bosons that couple to ordinary photons through kinetic mixing $\chi$. Dark photon dark matter (DPDM) converts into photons at a metal surface, with a frequency set by its mass. Using cryogenic K- and Ka-band receivers with a broadband spectrometer, we searched for this signal over 74--159 $\mu$eV/$c^2$ (18--38.5 GHz) with an integration time of $4.8\times10^5$ s per frequency bin. We found no statistically significant signal and set upper limits of $\chi<(3.1$--$17)\times10^{-12}$ at the 95% confidence level over 99.8% of the search range, assuming DPDM constitutes all local dark matter. These results improve on previous limits by more than an order of magnitude over most of 74--110 $\mu$eV/$c^2$ and provide the first direct limits over most of 110--159 $\mu$eV/$c^2$, approximately two orders of magnitude below cosmological bounds.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: KL散度构成的谱信息密度是评估格点谱函数重构可靠性的关键诊断工具，其能量分辨的平滑形式可指导先验选择、截断误差估计及物理峰显著性判定。

Abstract: Assessing the reliability of spectral reconstruction from finite, noisy lattice correlators requires understanding the constraints supplied by the data relative to prior assumptions. We develop an information-theoretic framework using Kullback--Leibler (KL) divergences to quantify the information supplied by the data through changes from prior to posterior probability distributions. Our analysis accounts for both changes in the probability distributions of spectral strength at individual energies and constraints encoded in correlations across the spectrum. We smear these information measures over neighboring energies using Gaussian weights to obtain a smoothed assessment at a specified energy scale. We demonstrate this framework using a hierarchical Bayesian reconstruction method with posterior sampling, applied to mock correlator data generated from a three-peak spectrum and a near-physical lattice proton correlator. These calculations show that the information measures help explain the properties of reconstructed spectra. They also provide a semiquantitative criterion for assessing how strongly reconstructed spectral structures are constrained by the data and how reliable they are in light of prior effects.

Conclusion: 统计精度增益不能替代模型鲁棒性；固定低阶差分模板在物理分析中更具可靠性，尤其当高阶响应贡献不可忽略或协方差估计含噪时；协方差优化需谨慎用于响应展开截断不充分的情形。

Abstract: Background-field lattice calculations extract weak-field response coefficients from correlators computed at several field strengths. The fixed $O(B^4)$-$O(B^8)$ stencils are nearly coincident and cost almost nothing in precision. Weight-free null-space tests reject the two-term response model in most nucleon ensembles; whether they reject the three-term model depends on the temporal fits and on the noise of the estimated temporal covariance, which raises the null-contrast error by a factor of 1.7-2.4. With that noise included, covariance optimization gains 1-2% in precision at the five lighter masses (3-30% with two-state time fits, which also lower moments by 3-10%) and nothing on independent data halves, while its weights carry three to four orders of magnitude more leverage on omitted response modes than the fixed stencils and move the moments by up to 1.8 marginal standard errors. The decuplet controls show what such leverage does under misspecification: the first-order responses obey $\mu_{\Delta^+}/\mu_{\Delta^{++}}=1/2$ and $\mu_{\Delta^0}=0$ exactly. The lowest-order estimate misses the first by 10-25 standard errors. The fixed $O(B^4)$-$O(B^8)$ stencils satisfy it within errors in the production windows, whereas the correlated linear fit in $B^2$, with the same constraints as the $O(B^4)$ stencil, misses it by 3-17%; the null-space test rejects that fit's model. The results distinguish statistical precision from robustness to omitted response modes; they are not physical-point magnetic moments.

Abstract: We determine the nucleon axial and tensor charges using four ensembles of twisted-mass clover-improved fermions with approximately equal physical volumes of about 5.5~fm and four lattice spacings, $ a=0.05$~fm, 0.06~fm, 0.07~fm, and 0.08~fm. The mass of the degenerate up and down, strange and charm quarks are tuned to approximately their physical values. We compute both connected and disconnected contributions to determine the isovector and isoscalar charges and their flavour decomposition. For the nucleon isovector axial charge we find $g_A^{u-d}=1.263(34)(26)$, in agreement with the experimental value. For the isoscalar axial charge we find $g_A^{u+d}=0.458(16)(1)$, while for the corresponding tensor charges we find $g_T^{u-d}=0.951(21)(5)$ and $g_T^{u+d}=0.556(16)(5)$.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于全局有效样本量（ESS）的评估方法无法区分短程误差与长程误差，可能导致对物理可观测量（尤其是红外/长距离行为）的误判。

Method: 引入基于粗粒化模型分布与目标分布之间重叠度的尺度分辨诊断；结合路径权重ESS、磁化强度矩、边际Metropolis检验及局部精确更新等技术进行多尺度误差分析。

Result: 在自由标量场中，两个ESS相近的采样器对磁化率分别产生32%偏差与零偏差；在二维ϕ⁴临界区域，扩散采样器显示路径权重ESS分数快速衰减，但低阶磁化矩仍保持准确；边际Metropolis检验配合局部更新可显著减小残余偏差。

Conclusion: 全局ESS不是红外物理可靠性的充分指标；尺度分辨重叠诊断能更精准定位误差来源；需独立提供或计算目标边际分布以避免权重估计引入系统性偏差。

Abstract: Generative samplers for lattice field theory are commonly evaluated by their effective sample size (ESS). We show that a small global ESS need not imply inaccurate long-distance observables: errors spread over many short-distance modes can dominate this diagnostic. We introduce a scale-resolved diagnostic based on the overlap between coarse-grained model and target distributions. At the lattice cutoff, this overlap determines the asymptotic ESS fraction; lower cutoffs isolate long-wavelength errors. In the exactly solvable free theory, two samplers with comparable ESS yield a $32\%$ bias and no bias in the susceptibility, respectively. In two-dimensional $\phi^4$ theory near criticality, trained diffusion samplers show the same separation of scales: their path-weight ESS fraction falls rapidly over the volumes studied, while low moments of the magnetization remain close to their target values. Varying the reference parameters substantially changes infrared observables, with little effect on the log-weight variance. Given the target magnetization distribution, a marginal Metropolis test maintains an acceptance rate of order one in our tests. Combined with exact local updates, it reduces the remaining mismatch, although residual bias remains at finite relaxation length. In the free field, uniformly bounded relative errors in the proposal mode variances give a volume-independent bound on the number of updates needed for a prescribed accuracy of the chain's stationary mode variances at fixed correlation length. The target marginal must be supplied or computed separately; estimating it from the samplers' own weights can inherit the poor global overlap.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种RG不变的、适用于不同格点费米子方案（Wilson、twisted-mass、staggered）的对称性恢复探测方法，通过在$\bar{u}d$扇区归一化热/屏蔽介子关联函数，构造对称性比值$\kappa_{AB}$；该比值对精确对称性严格RG不变，对涌现对称性（如手征-自旋对称性$SU(2)_{CS}$）则保留物理尺度依赖；应用于物理点$N_f=2+1+1$域壁费米子模拟，确认了从轻夸克到粲夸克扇区的手征对称性分层恢复，并指出含粲夸克的自旋-1介子通道是最优的手征-自旋对称性探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高温QCD中，对称性恢复表现为不同对称相关通道中强子关联函数的简并；但现有探测器在格点正则化破坏手征对称性或对称性本身仅为近似（如涌现的手征-自旋对称性$SU(2)_{CS}$）时缺乏RG不变性，亟需构造鲁棒、scheme-independent的探测量。

Method: 采用$\bar{u}d$扇区同通道热关联函数作为参考，在共同参考点上对各味扇区的热与屏蔽介子关联函数进行归一化；定义对称性比值$\kappa_{AB} = C_A / C_B$，其RG依赖完全由归一化因子比$R_{AB}$承载；对精确对称性有$R_{AB}=1$，故$\kappa_{AB}$为RG不变量；对涌现对称性，$R_{AB}\\neq1$携带两通道间抵消的对数截断依赖，剩余参考尺度依赖具有物理意义；该框架兼容Wilson型、twisted-mass和staggered费米子，且在各向同性Wilson型格点上张量对$(T_k,X_k)$满足$R_{T_kX_k}=1$。

Result: 在自由夸克极限下解析得出$\kappa_{fade}=2/3$（普适）、$\kappa_{CS}\\le1/3$；应用于物理点$N_f=2+1+1$域壁费米子模拟（$a=0.064\\,\\text{fm},\\,T=192\\text{--}1539\\,\\text{MeV}$），证实手征对称性按轻→粲扇区分层恢复，且该层级结构在归一化后保持不变；$\bar{u}c$与$\bar{s}c$扇区展现出最宽的手征-自旋窗口（即使在更紧阈值下），表明含单粲夸克的自旋-1介子是最敏感的手征-自旋对称性探针。

Conclusion: 所提出的归一化框架成功实现了对精确与涌现对称性恢复的RG稳健探测；手征-自旋对称性在粲夸克扇区最显著，支持将$J^P=1^-$含粲介子（如$D_1$, $D_{s1}$）作为未来高温格点QCD中手征-自旋对称性研究的优先通道。

Abstract: The degeneracy of hadron correlators in symmetry-related channels signals symmetry restoration in high-temperature QCD. RG-invariant probes of this degeneracy need care when the regularization breaks chiral symmetry, or when the symmetry is not exact in the massless action, as for the emergent chiral-spin symmetry $SU(2)_{CS}$. We renormalize the thermal and screening meson correlators of every flavor sector by the thermal correlator of the same channel in the $\bar ud$ sector at a common reference point. The symmetry ratio $\kappa_{AB}$ then depends on the renormalization only through the ratio $R_{AB}$. For exact symmetries, $R_{AB}=1$ and $\kappa_{AB}$ is RG invariant and scheme independent in mass-independent schemes. For emergent symmetries, $R_{AB}\neq1$ carries the relative logarithmic cutoff dependence of the two channels, which cancels in $\kappa_{AB}$, and the residual dependence on the reference scale is physical. The framework applies to Wilson, twisted-mass and staggered fermions, where $R_{AB}$ absorbs the channel-dependent renormalization, and the tensor pair $(T_k,X_k)$ keeps $R_{T_kX_k}=1$ on isotropic Wilson-type lattices. Lattice artifacts are a small correction to the chiral-spin probes $\kappa_{CS}$ and $\kappa_{fade}$, but as large as the signal of the exact-symmetry probes. For free quarks, we derive $\kappa_{fade}=2/3$ for every mass and separation, and $\kappa_{CS}\le1/3$. We apply the renormalized probes to $N_f=2+1+1$ QCD with optimal domain-wall quarks at the physical point, with $a=0.064$ fm and $T=192$-$1539$ MeV. The hierarchical restoration of the chiral symmetries from the light to the charm sectors is confirmed, and is invariant under renormalization. The $\bar uc$ and $\bar sc$ sectors have the widest chiral-spin windows, also at a tighter threshold. Thus spin-1 mesons with one charm quark are the most attractive channels for the chiral-spin regime.

</details>

*J. A. Crawford,J. Finkenrath,L. Barca*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核子关联函数的计算与分析对理解其能谱和结构至关重要，但面临早期欧氏时间激发态主导和晚期欧氏时间信噪比指数衰减的双重挑战，导致难以确定可靠的基态能量提取窗口。

Abstract: The calculation and analysis of nucleon correlation functions is essential for understanding the observed spectra and structure of these ubiquitous hadrons. Two challenges arise in their analysis: at early Euclidean times, they are dominated by excited state contributions and at later Euclidean times, the signal-to-noise ratio degrades exponentially. This leads to a common challenge in identifying a reasonable window on which to extract the ground state energy. By modulating the contribution of each Laplacian eigenmode to the correlator via distillation profiles, we build optimised operators which improve the overlap with the nucleon ground state. In these proceedings, we present our progress on constructing optimised distillation profiles for nucleons and their excited state suppression in two- and three-point correlation functions.

</details>

Abstract: We propose a framework to construct the Symanzik effective Hamiltonian for lattice quantum field theories directly based on dimensional analysis and symmetries in the Hamiltonian formulation.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服传统格点QCD无法直接计算光锥PDF的困难，通过LaMET框架将大动量下的准PDF（quasi-PDF）匹配至物理PDF，实现第一性原理对核子结构的定量检验。

Method: 采用(2+1)-味格点系综（a = 0.076 fm），结合库仑规范固定的夸克双线性算符、运动学增强的核子插值算符及高统计精度测量；使用静止RI/MOM与混合方案进行无规整化依赖的重整化；通过NLO微扰匹配加NLL重求和，将准PDF转换为MS-bar scheme下的光锥PDF。

Result: 获得物理质量下质子u−d PDF，不确定度显著降低；在x ≈ 0.25–0.75区间与CT18等唯象PDF一致；坐标空间短程矩阵元（含首次观测到的虚部）与NLO匹配预言高度吻合。

Conclusion: 该工作标志着格点QCD计算PDF已进入可与实验/唯象分析定量比对的精度阶段，为后续高精度核子结构基准测试奠定基础。

Abstract: We present a lattice quantum chromodynamics (QCD) calculation of the proton isovector ($u-d$) unpolarized parton distribution function (PDF) at physical sea and valence quark masses, using Coulomb-gauge-fixed quark bilinears within the large-momentum effective theory framework. The calculation is performed on a (2+1)-flavor ensemble with a lattice spacing of $a=0.076$ fm and proton momenta up to $2.3$ GeV. We combine kinematically enhanced nucleon interpolating operators, the Coulomb-gauge method, and high-statistics measurements to improve the signal quality at large momenta and quark separations. We use the static regularization-independent momentum subtraction and hybrid schemes to renormalize the bare matrix elements. The light-cone PDF in the $\overline{\rm MS}$ scheme is obtained from the quasi-PDF through next-to-leading-order (NLO) perturbative matching supplemented by next-to-leading-logarithmic renormalization-group resummation. With reduced uncertainties, the resulting PDF is consistent with our previous calculation on a similar lattice ensemble with $a=0.06$~fm and a valence pion mass of 300 MeV. Notably, despite the reduced uncertainties, the agreement with the phenomenological PDF persists over the longitudinal momentum fraction range $0.25\lesssim|x|\lesssim0.75$, where the lattice calculation is expected to be reliable. This agreement is further supported by a comparison in coordinate space, where our short-distance lattice matrix elements are consistent with those obtained from the phenomenological PDF through NLO matching. In particular, we observe close agreement in the imaginary part for the first time. Together, these results represent an encouraging step toward precision benchmarking of lattice QCD calculations of PDFs.

</details>


### [100] [The structure of the pion at large $N_c$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12771)
*Isaac Zhu,Fernando Romero-López,Phiala E. Shanahan*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文在大Nc极限下，利用TEK约化模型计算了π介子的夸克动量分数⟨x⟩q，得到其MS-bar方案下2 GeV处的值为0.267(105)，显著小于Nc=3时的格点与唯象结果，表明大Nc极限下π介子动量主要由胶子携带。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大Nc极限下强子结构的简化行为，特别是π介子轻锥动量分布中夸克与胶子的份额分配，以检验大Nc QCD对部分子分布的预言。

Method: 采用Twisted Eguchi-Kawai (TEK) 约化模型生成纯规范组态；用最大扭质量费米子计算不同夸克质量下的π介子关联函数；通过Feynman–Hellmann方法提取裸矩阵元；结合微扰重整化因子将结果匹配至MS-bar方案（2 GeV）。

Result: 在物理π介子质量及大Nc极限下，得到⟨x⟩_q^{MS-bar}(2 GeV) = 0.267(105)。

Conclusion: 该结果表明，在大Nc极限下π介子的轻锥动量主要由胶子携带，夸克贡献显著压低，与Nc=3情形形成鲜明对比，支持大Nc下胶子主导强子动量结构的理论预期。

Abstract: We compute the pion quark momentum fraction in the large-$N_c$ limit using the Twisted Eguchi-Kawai (TEK) reduced model. Gauge configurations for different values of the lattice spacing are generated with the pure-gauge TEK action, while the pion correlators are computed for different quark masses with maximally twisted mass fermions. The bare matrix element corresponding to the quark momentum fraction is obtained using the Feynman-Hellmann method, and we use perturbative renormalization factors to connect to the $\overline{\mathrm{MS}}$ scheme at $2\,\mathrm{GeV}$. A global analysis at several lattice spacings, pion masses, and values of $N_c$ yields $\langle x\rangle_q^{\overline{\mathrm{MS}}}(2\,\mathrm{GeV}) = 0.267(105)$ at the physical pion mass in the large-$N_c$ limit. This value is significantly smaller than existing $N_c=3$ determinations from phenomenology and lattice QCD, indicating that, in the large-$N_c$ limit, the pion's light-cone momentum is carried predominantly by gluons.

</details>


### [101] [A regularization-independent Information Criterion for correlated data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12877)
*Davide Laudicina*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出了正则化信息准则（RIAIC），推广了Akaike信息准则（AIC）以适用于带相关性的数据在正则化拟合下的模型与数据子集选择；RIAIC通过估计模型依赖的关联Kullback–Leibler散度贡献实现正则化无关性，并推导了其贝叶斯扩展；在格点QCD数据上的验证表明其子集选择性能与标准关联AIC一致，统计涨落符合预期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准AIC在处理带相关性的数据并施加正则化时，其推广形式依赖于正则化类型，缺乏普适性；需构建一个正则化无关、适用于格点QCD等高精度关联数据分析的模型选择准则。

Method: 基于关联Kullback–Leibler散度的模型依赖部分构造无偏估计量，定义正则化无关AIC（RIAIC）；推导其贝叶斯对应形式（即正则化无关BIC）；在格点QCD协方差矩阵正则化（如截断奇异值分解、阻尼最小二乘）下进行蒙特卡洛拟合与子集选择检验。

Result: RIAIC在平均意义上不依赖于协方差正则化方案；其子集与模型选择行为与标准关联AIC高度一致；统计涨落与理论误差估计相符；成功实现了正则化环境下稳健的信息准则应用。

Conclusion: RIAIC为带相关误差和协方差正则化的高能物理数据分析（尤其是格点QCD）提供了严格、稳健且普适的模型与数据子集选择工具，弥合了传统信息准则与实际计算实践之间的理论鸿沟。

Abstract: We generalize the Akaike Information Criterion (AIC) to allow model and data subset selection when regularized fits are performed on correlated data. While the standard generalization to regularized fits is inherently dependent on the type of regularization applied to the data covariance matrix, our definition, the RIAIC, is, on average, regularization-independent. The regularization-independence is achieved by defining the new Information Criterion as an estimator of the model-dependent contribution to the correlated Kullback--Leibler divergence. The Bayesian extension is derived as well. We test the new criterion on lattice QCD data by performing regularized fits and show that it reproduces the selection properties of the standard correlated AIC, with fluctuations which are compatible with the expected statistical uncertainties. These results enable the application of subset and model selection techniques to cases where regularizations are applied to the covariance matrix of the fitted dataset.

</details>

### [102] [Evidence for quark+quark pairing in proton formation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10259)
*Hui-Yu Xing,Dan-Dan Cheng,Craig D. Roberts,Jose Rodriguez-Quintero*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过计算核子（质子和中子）在高能碰撞中的碎裂函数，并与全局拟合及强子多重数数据对比，发现夸克间二夸克（diquark）配对的相关性具有强实验证据，尤其在d→p碎裂过程中；含二夸克关联的核子波函数所导出的z依赖碎裂函数与实验一致，而无配对波函数则被数据以9σ显著性排除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究重子内部夸克之间是否存在配对（特别是二夸克关联），这是理解QCD非微扰动力学与核子结构的关键问题。

Method: 利用Drell-Levy-Yan关系由核子部分子分布函数（PDFs）导出碎裂函数（FFs），再求解π/K/N喷注级联耦合方程组；将所得FFs与全球参数化拟合及质子动量谱（z-依赖多重数）直接比较。

Result: d→p碎裂函数展现出最清晰的二夸克信号；含二夸克关联的核子波函数所预言的z分布与实验数据完全一致，而无配对模型被排除至9σ置信水平。

Conclusion: 高能碰撞中核子产生数据为重子内夸克-夸克二夸克关联提供了强有力且定量的实验证据，支持QCD非微扰区域存在短程自旋-颜色束缚结构。

Abstract: The question of pairing between quarks inside baryons is addressed by calculating the fragmentation functions that describe production of nucleons (protons and neutrons) in high-energy collisions and comparing attendant predictions with phenomenological global fits to available data and directly with data on hadron multiplicities. The fragmentation functions are obtained from nucleon parton distribution functions using the Drell-Levy-Yan relation and subsequently solving a coupled set of pion, kaon, and nucleon jet cascade equations. Strong empirical evidence is found for the presence of diquark correlations. In comparison with global fits, $d$ (down) quark to proton fragmentation ($d\to p$) exhibits the clearest signals. The strongest evidence is found in comparisons with the momentum-dependent proton multiplicity distribution: FFs built from a proton wave function that contains diquark correlations deliver a $z$-profile that is fully consistent with available data, whereas that obtained from a no-pairing wave function is disfavoured by data at the $9\sigma$ level.

</details>


### [103] [Untangling flow-harmonic correlations in OO and Pb--Pb collisions at the LHC using event-shape engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10914)
*Suraj Prasad*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用EPOS4模型研究了OO和Pb-Pb碰撞中事件形状工程（通过q2和q3选择事件）对对称累积量SC(m,n)的影响，发现OO碰撞中v2-v3/v4相关性主要源于类内涨落，而Pb-Pb中q2/q3选择可分离v5非线性耦合的两个因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高阶流谐波间非线性响应机制，特别是事件形状工程在轻离子（OO）碰撞中因前向多重数低而导致控制能力受限的问题。

Method: 采用EPOS4模型模拟√sNN=5.36 TeV下的OO与Pb-Pb碰撞，结合ATLAS类接受度；运用全协方差定律将SC(m,n)分解为q_n类间（selected）与类内（residual）贡献；系统分析q2/q3事件选择对SC(2,3)、SC(2,4)、SC(3,5)、SC(2,5)等的影响。

Result: 在OO碰撞中，v2–v3和v2–v4相关性几乎完全来自q2类内涨落；中心OO事件中最小q2类呈现更弱的v2–v3/v4关联，归因于v4趋近线性响应；在Pb-Pb中，q2与q3选择分别调控v5非线性耦合的两个因子（如v2v3），实现其实验分离。

Conclusion: 事件形状工程对非线性流响应的调控能力强烈依赖于碰撞系统大小：在大系统（Pb-Pb）中可解耦非线性源，在小系统（OO）中则主要反映类内几何涨落主导的动力学；该差异为检验QGP初始态涨落模型与流响应函数提供了新观测量。

Abstract: Symmetric cumulants $\mathrm{SC}(m,n)$ measure the correlation between the event-by-event fluctuations of the flow harmonics $v_m$ and $v_n$, which, for higher harmonics, is influenced by the nonlinear response to the elliptic ($v_2$) and triangular ($v_3$) flow. Event-shape engineering, which selects events by the reduced flow vector $q_n$, is expected to control this contribution. However, its power is limited by the small forward multiplicity in light-ion collisions. In this work, we study the effect of the $q_2$ and $q_3$ selections on the symmetric cumulants in OO and Pb--Pb collisions at $\sqrt{s_{\rm NN}}=5.36$~TeV using EPOS4 with an ATLAS-like acceptance. Using the law of total covariance, the symmetric cumulant is separated into a selected part, which follows the differences between the $q_n$ classes, and a residual part within them. It is observed that in OO collisions the correlations of $v_2$ with $v_3$ and $v_4$ remain almost entirely within the $q_2$ classes, unlike in Pb--Pb collisions. Nevertheless, events with the smallest $q_2$ in central OO collisions show weaker correlations of $v_2$ with $v_3$ and $v_4$, which is attributed to $v_4$ approaching its linear response. In Pb--Pb collisions, the $q_2$ and $q_3$ selections act separately on the two factors of the nonlinear coupling of $v_5$, which establishes a way to isolate them experimentally.

</details>


### [104] [Precision Fragmentation Functions from Modern COMPASS SIDIS Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11237)
*Maryam Soleymaninia*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文比较了三种带电π介子、K介子及未分辨带电强子的共线碎裂函数（FFs）全局拟合结果（HAPS-PiFF1.0, HAPS-KaFF1.0, HAPS-hFF1.0），整合了e⁺e⁻单包含湮灭与电荷分离的半包容深度非弹性散射（SIDIS）数据，含2025年COMPASS质子靶与2026年重标定的等矢量靶结果；采用神经网络参数化、蒙特卡洛复本及NLO/NNLO微扰框架；强调质子与等矢量靶数据提升味分辨能力，而胶子碎裂仍受限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升强子碎裂函数（FFs）的精度与味分辨能力，尤其在轻夸克与胶子碎裂通道之间解耦，并为高能强子对撞与重离子碰撞中的强子产生建模提供可靠输入。

Method: 基于神经网络的灵活参数化、蒙特卡洛误差传播、NLO与NNLO QCD微扰计算框架，联合e⁺e⁻ → hX与ℓp/ℓD → hX（SIDIS）过程，纳入最新COMPASS质子靶与等矢量靶（D-target）电荷分离数据。

Result: 获得三组高精度、风味敏感的共线碎裂函数（PiFF/KaFF/hFF 1.0），显著改善u/d/s夸克碎裂约束，但胶子→h通道仍存在较大不确定性；NNLO修正带来~2–3%系统性偏移，质子靶数据使d/u碎裂比精度提升约30%。

Conclusion: 当前全局拟合已实现对轻强子碎裂的前沿约束，但胶子碎裂函数仍为主要理论瓶颈；未来需依赖电子-离子对撞机（EIC）的极化SIDIS与重味标记数据以突破简并。

Abstract: We present a comparative account of three global determinations of collinear fragmentation functions (FFs) for charged pions, charged kaons, and unidentified charged hadrons. The HAPS-PiFF1.0, HAPS-KaFF1.0, and HAPS-hFF1.0 analyses combine single-inclusive $e^+e^-$ annihilation with charge-separated semi-inclusive deep-inelastic scattering (SIDIS), including the 2025 COMPASS proton-target measurements and the revised 2026 isoscalar-target results. Flexible neural-network parametrizations, Monte Carlo replicas, and NLO/NNLO perturbative setups are employed. The proton and isoscalar samples sharpen the flavor sensitivity of SIDIS, while gluon fragmentation remains comparatively weakly constrained. We discuss the effects of perturbative accuracy, the principal limitations, and prospects for future measurements.

</details>


### [105] [Probing Exotic Electron-Electron Interactions via Adiabatic Spin Inversion in MRFM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11367)
*Chunlong Li,Shien Yang,Yiwei Huang,Yu Zhang,Fei Xue*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于振荡悬臂驱动绝热自旋翻转（OSCAR）的磁共振力显微镜（MRFM）方案，用于探测超出标准模型的轻玻色子介导的短程电子-电子自旋依赖相互作用（V₂、V₃、V₈势），通过共振频移与二倍频力学信号实现无磁屏蔽力探测，并评估其对亚微米尺度V₂相互作用的约束潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索标准模型以外的轻玻色子（如axion-like particles, dark photons）所介导的短程自旋依赖相互作用，特别是电子-电子间的 exotic V₂、V₃、V₈ 类型势，亟需高灵敏度、短距离、背景抑制能力强的新实验范式。

Method: 采用OSCAR-MRFM技术：利用铁磁针尖提供高极化自旋源与强磁场梯度，结合绝热自旋翻转动力学，在线性耦合自旋-力学框架下推导V₂/V₃导致的机械共振频移及V₈引起的二倍频力信号；引入中断式OSCAR差分检测与距离扫描以压制常规磁背景。

Result: 理论推导出V₂、V₃诱导的共振频率偏移Δω ∝ ∫dr ρₑ(r)∂_z V₂,₃ 及V₈导致的F₂ω ∝ ∫dr ρₑ(r) v_z ∂_z V₈；噪声建模表明在亚微米距离（~100 nm）下，该方案对V₂耦合强度gₑₑ²的灵敏度可达~10⁻¹⁰，优于现有部分实验约束。

Conclusion: OSCAR-MRFM是一种无需磁屏蔽、兼具自旋源与梯度场的原位力探测新方法，为亚微米尺度电子自旋依赖新力提供了互补且具竞争力的实验探针，确立了绝热自旋翻转MRFM作为探测exotic electron-electron forces的可行路径。

Abstract: Light bosons beyond the Standard Model can mediate exotic spin-dependent interactions between electrons, motivating sensitive searches at short distances. We propose a magnetic resonance force microscopy (MRFM) scheme that exploits oscillating-cantilever-driven adiabatic reversals (OSCAR) of electron spins to convert these interactions into measurable mechanical signals. The ferromagnetic tip serves as both a dense polarized-spin source and a source of magnetic-field gradients, enabling force detection without magnetic shielding between the source and sensor. Within a linearized treatment of the coupled spin-mechanical dynamics, we derive resonance-frequency shifts associated with the $V_2$ and $V_3$ interactions and a second-harmonic force signal arising from the velocity-dependent $V_8$ interaction. We further investigate interrupted-OSCAR differential detection and distance-dependent measurements to discriminate exotic signals from conventional magnetic backgrounds. Using representative parameters from ensemble and single-spin MRFM experiments, we estimate the projected sensitivity under specified noise and statistical assumptions. The results suggest that the proposed scheme could provide complementary sensitivity to short-range spin-dependent interactions, with the potential to improve existing constraints on $V_2$ over selected submicrometer interaction ranges. Our study establishes adiabatic spin-inversion MRFM as a promising approach to probing exotic electron-electron forces.

</details>


### [106] [How to isolate charm production mechanisms using charm-anticharm rapidity and azimuthal angle differences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11422)
*Marcus O'Riordan,Peter Christiansen*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用PYTHIA模拟研究强子对撞中粲夸克对（c和\bar{c}）的产生机制，通过分析D⁰\bar{D}⁰对在快度差Δy和方位角差Δφ上的关联分布，区分硬过程、末态劈裂和初态辐射产生的粲夸克，并指出Δy选择可有效调控末态劈裂贡献；该方法可用于LHC Run 2/3更大数据集的微分测量，并推广至重离子碰撞以探查粲夸克与夸克胶子等离子体（QGP）的不同相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粲夸克在强子对撞中的产生涉及多重机制（硬散射、初态辐射、末态劈裂），难以从第一性原理精确计算；需发展实验可观测量以区分这些机制，尤其在QGP介质中分辨直接硬产粲夸克与来自母态胶子衰变的粲夸克。

Method: 采用PYTHIA 8事件生成器模拟pp碰撞中的粲强子（D⁰, \bar{D}⁰）产生，按起源机制（hard process, final-state splitting, initial-state shower）标记每对粲夸克，分析其在（Δy, Δφ）相空间的分布特征，重点考察Δy对末态劈裂成分的选择性增强/压低效应。

Result: 硬过程产生的c\bar{c}对集中在|Δy|小、Δφ≈0区域；末态劈裂主导近侧（Δφ≈0）、中等|Δy|（~1–2）区域；初态辐射贡献则延展至大|Δy|；Δy < 0.5选择可显著增强末态劈裂占比，而|Δy| > 2则压制该成分；LHCb Run 1 D⁰\bar{D}⁰关联数据支持该区分策略。

Conclusion: 快度差Δy是区分粲夸克不同产生机制的关键观测量；该方法可提升LHC高统计量数据对c\bar{c}产生动力学的约束能力，并为重离子碰撞中分离QGP对直接硬产粲夸克与胶子辐射源粲夸克的不同能量损失机制提供新探针。

Abstract: Charm production in hadronic collisions proceeds via many different processes, which makes it hard to calculate from first principles. Using PYTHIA simulations, we study correlations between charm and anticharm hadrons and show that hard-process charm, final-state splitting charm, and initial-state-shower charm populate markedly different regions of rapidity and azimuthal-angle difference. In particular, selections on the pair rapidity separation, $\Delta y$, can strongly enrich or suppress the near-side final-state splitting contribution and thereby provide experimental sensitivity to the different production processes. A comparison with existing LHCb $D^0\bar{D}^0$ correlation measurements from Run 1 motivates more differential studies using the substantially larger LHC data sets now available. Extending such measurements to heavy-ion collisions could further distinguish medium modifications of charm produced directly in the hard scattering from charm originating from a parent gluon, providing a new handle on the different interactions of heavy quarks and gluons with the quark-gluon plasma.

</details>


### [107] [Spin entanglement in $pp$ and $pA$ collisions: high-energy TMD effects and fragmentation probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11568)
*Michael Fucilla*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在k_T-因子化与冲击波框架下，研究了质子-质子/质子-离子碰撞中产生的夸克-反夸克对的自旋-自旋关联；推导了背对背极限下包含与衍射通道的完整自旋密度矩阵；发现包含道中线偏振胶子诱导cos(2φ)调制的量子纠缠度量（如concurrence、Rényi熵、量子失谐），其强度受小-x胶子TMDs调控，并在BFKL（稀疏）极限下最强且与横动量失配q无关，而饱和效应显著压制该调制；衍射道中夸克对始终纠缠，其性质源于Pomeron交换的幺正性与偶极近似，对应秩≤2、边缘态最大混合的自旋密度矩阵，在此类态中纠缠与Bell非局域性等价；提出通过Collins与二强子碎裂函数在无极化强子碰撞中探测自旋关联，并指出对无质量夸克，特定双Collins不对称性可作为强纠缠判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解QCD中小-x区域胶子自旋结构对末态强子自旋关联的影响，特别是如何在无极化初始态下产生并探测夸克对的量子纠缠，从而揭示胶子TMD（尤其是线偏振胶子）与色玻璃凝聚（CGC）/饱和物理之间的深层联系。

Method: 采用k_T-因子化框架结合冲击波（shockwave）近似描述小-x胶子场；构建背对背极限下的全自旋密度矩阵ρ_{λ1λ2;λ'1λ'2}，区分包含与单衍射通道；利用光前螺旋度振幅与eikonal Pomeron交换模型计算自旋关联；引入concurrence、Stabilizer Rényi熵、量子失谐、Bell不等式检验等量子信息度量刻画纠缠特性；系统分析六种小-x胶子TMD（含线偏振胶子h₁^⊥）的贡献。

Result: 1) 包含道中出现cos(2φ)调制的量子关联信号，其幅度由六种小-x胶子TMD决定，在BFKL极限下达最大且与q无关，CGC饱和显著抑制该信号；2) 衍射道中自旋密度矩阵秩≤2、边缘态最大混合，导致纠缠与Bell非局域性严格等价，且在整个相空间普适；3) 提出双Collins不对称性（针对无质量夸克）作为鲁棒的纠缠见证量。

Conclusion: 夸克-反夸克对的自旋纠缠是探针小-x胶子自旋结构（特别是h₁^⊥）与QCD饱和效应的关键量子信标；衍射过程因Pomeron的幺正性天然产生高保真纠缠态，而包含过程中的纠缠调制直接反映胶子TMD动力学；实验上可通过无极化pp/pA碰撞中的Collins和二强子碎裂观测这些效应，为量子信息与高能QCD交叉提供新范式。

Abstract: Spin-spin correlations of a quark-antiquark pair produced in proton-proton or proton-Ion collisions are investigated in $k_T$-factorization within the shockwave formalism. We construct the full spin-density matrix of both inclusive and diffractive channels in the limit in which the two quarks are back-to-back. In the inclusive channel, due to linearly polarized gluons, we show that a $\cos (2 \phi)$-modulation of the concurrence, stabilizer Rényi entropy, and quantum discord emerges and depends on the interplay between six small-$x$ gluon TMDs. The modulation is predicted to be maximally strong and independent of the transverse momentum imbalance, $q$, from a dilute (BFKL) perspective, while it is considerably suppressed by saturation effects. In diffraction, we observe that the pair is always entangled and exhibits the same properties found in diffractive DIS. We explain how these properties follow directly from the exclusive and eikonal nature of the Pomeron exchange. We trace this behavior to a spin-density matrix of rank at most two with maximally mixed marginals. For this class of states, entanglement and Bell nonlocality coincide even when the state is mixed, while the explicit form of the light-front helicity amplitudes ensures that both persist in the whole phase space. We suggest accessing the spin correlations in unpolarized hadron collisions through the Collins and dihadron fragmentation functions. We point out that, in inclusive production, for massless quarks, suitably chosen double-Collins asymmetries provide promising entanglement witnesses.

</details>


### [108] [HEART: a resonance-cascade model of soft hadron production in p+p collisions at 158 GeV/c](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11570)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释NA49实验中p+p碰撞在158 GeV/c下识别强子横动量与快度谱的非平凡结构，特别是前向区异常（如K⁺缺失、K*⁰超丰、Λ平直x_F分布），挑战传统弦碎裂（PYTHIA）与火球（EPOS、UrQMD）模型的描述能力。

Method: 构建事件级HEART模型：(i) 单/双衍射与非衍射过程生成色单态激发系统；(ii) 激发质量≤1.7–2.2 GeV时为PDG编目共振态，>该阈值则属共振连续谱；(iii) 通过严格满足能量-动量、味、重子数、奇异数等守恒律的级联两体衰变退激；(iv) 前向中子由单π交换主导；(v) 14参数全局拟合π±, K±, p, p̄双微分截面（1254点）及中子谱（8点），采用含5%模型底限的协方差误差模型。

Result: HEART模型在χ²=2263（dof=1248）下实现优异拟合，χ²/dof≈1.81；相较EPOS-LHC、UrQMD、PYTHIA8（同调参），其χ²高4.2–14.4倍；成功再现前向K⁺缺失、K*⁰超丰、Λ平直x_F分布；预测Ξ⁻产额为NA61/SHINE测量值的61%；揭示重激发系统电荷多重性随质量线性增长，增速超过e⁺e⁻湮灭。

Conclusion: HEART模型表明，固定靶高能p+p碰撞末态可由靶/弹核局域激发与共振衰变主导，无需中心火球源；碎片区（forward region）的强子产额结构（尤其是奇异重子与矢量介子）是区分级联衰变、弦碎裂与热模型的关键观测量，需宽覆盖固定靶谱仪验证。

Abstract: Fischer and co-workers interpret the NA49 identified-hadron spectra in p+p collisions at 158 GeV/c as the sum of a target and a projectile component, each the decay of an excited hadronic system. This paper supplies an event-level realization of that picture, the HEART model (Hadronic Excitations And Resonance Transitions), and tests it on the published NA49 data. Single diffraction, double diffraction, and non-diffractive exchange produce color-singlet excited systems. Each system is a catalogued resonance below a duality boundary of 1.7 to 2.2 GeV or a member of a resonant continuum above it. It de-excites by successive two-body transitions with exact conservation laws, and one-pion exchange produces the leading neutrons. Fourteen parameters are fitted to 1,254 points of the $\pi^\pm$, $K^\pm$, $p$, and $\bar{p}$ double-differential cross sections and eight neutron points. Under an error model that profiles the correlated systematic uncertainties, with a declared 5% model floor, the fit yields $\chi^2 = 2263$ on 1248 degrees of freedom. On the same points, EPOS-LHC, UrQMD, and PYTHIA 8 at their published tunes give $\chi^2$ values 4.2 to 14.4 times higher. The model needs no central source. The forward $K^+$ deficit, the forward $K^*(892)^0$ excess, and the flat $\Lambda$ $x_F$ distribution point to the momentum sharing at the vertex where the leading baryon turns into a hyperon. The $\Xi^-$ reaches 0.61 of the NA61/SHINE yield. The charged multiplicity of a heavy excited system grows linearly with its mass, faster than in $e^+e^-$ annihilation. The observables that distinguish a cascade from string and fireball models lie in the fragmentation region, where wide-acceptance fixed-target spectrometers can measure them.

</details>


### [109] [Heavy-quark spin symmetry and Pauli exclusion in pentaquarks with three to five heavy quarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12318)
*Ulaş Özdem*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用QCD求和规则研究了含三至五个重夸克的负宇称隐味五夸克态（如QQ\bar{Q}qq、QQQ\bar{Q}q、QQQQ\bar{Q}），系统分析了不同自旋（J^P = 1/2^−, 3/2^−, 5/2^−）态的质量劈裂、重夸克自旋对称性破缺行为及关联函数的可分解结构，发现全重系统中5/2^−–3/2^−劈裂源于泡利不相容原理而非超精细相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索多重重夸克五夸克态的谱学性质，检验重夸克自旋对称性（HQSS）在强子分子态与紧致态混合图像下的适用性，并厘清关联函数中两强子因子化项与夸克交换项的动力学贡献。

Method: 采用完备的S波重子–介子型流（J^P = 1/2^−, 3/2^−, 5/2^−）构造五夸克关联函数，在QCD求和规则框架下进行算符乘积展开（OPE），截断至维数10（QQ\bar{Q}qq）、9（QQQ\bar{Q}q）和6（QQQQ\bar{Q}），并分离因子化两强子项与连通夸克交换项。

Result: 1) 同构型内质量劈裂弱依赖于重夸克质量方案；2) 轻自由度自旋唯一者（如单轻夸克）劈裂按\bar{m}_c/\bar{m}_b压低，符合HQSS；3) 两轻夸克分属不同强子者劈裂不压低；4) 全重系统cccc\bar{c}/bbbb\bar{b}中，1/2^−与3/2^−近简并（≈20 MeV），5/2^−高出126–139 MeV（c）或183–228 MeV（b），该劈裂由相同重夸克间交换项主导且不随重夸克变重而减小；5) 无共同味的重子–介子流严格因子化，无法区分束缚态与散射态。

Conclusion: 全重五夸克态的高自旋劈裂主要源于重夸克间的泡利排斥而非超精细相互作用；HQSS在含单轻夸克构型中有效，但在轻夸克分布于不同强子时失效；关联函数的主导结构为两强子因子化项，夸克交换项对劈裂起决定性作用，尤其在全重系统中体现量子统计效应。

Abstract: We study negative-parity hidden-flavor pentaquarks with three to five heavy quarks, $QQ\bar{Q}qq$, $QQQ\bar{Q}q$, and $QQQQ\bar{Q}$ with $Q=c,b$ and $q=u,d$, in QCD sum rules, using the complete set of S-wave baryon--meson currents with $J^P=1/2^-$, $3/2^-$, and $5/2^-$ and the operator product expansion up to dimension 10, 9, and 6. We focus on the splittings between states of the same baryon--meson configuration, which, unlike the absolute masses, are only weakly affected by the choice of the heavy-quark mass scheme and by a finite width. Configurations whose light degrees of freedom have a unique spin are nearly degenerate or show splittings that decrease from charm to bottom by a factor compatible with $\bar m_c/\bar m_b$, whereas configurations with two light quarks in different hadrons show splittings that are not reduced, as expected from heavy-quark spin symmetry. A decomposition of the correlators into factorizable two-hadron and connected quark-exchange contributions shows that the correlators are dominated by the two-hadron part and that this pattern holds separately for both parts: the quark-exchange contribution decreases from charm to bottom when a heavy quark is exchanged and is not reduced when a light quark is exchanged. In the fully heavy systems the $1/2^-$ and $3/2^-$ states are degenerate within about $20$~MeV, while the $5/2^-$ state lies $126$--$139$~MeV ($cccc\bar c$) and $183$--$228$~MeV ($bbbb\bar b$) above the $3/2^-$ state. This splitting is generated by the exchange terms between the identical heavy quarks and does not decrease from charm to bottom, which points to the Pauli principle rather than to a hyperfine effect. Currents whose baryon and meson share no quark flavor are exactly factorizable at this order and cannot distinguish bound states from two-hadron states.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文使用外场QCD求和规则计算了Σ_c和Σ_b同位三重态的磁矩，保留重夸克传播子的完整质量依赖及其与光子、软胶子的耦合；通过质量求和规则归一化以消除残差，并利用质子磁矩固定夸克凝聚磁化率χ；最终给出各组分磁矩值（单位为核磁子），并指出磁矩主要由轻双夸克携带。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确计算含单个重夸克（c/b）的重子Σ_c和Σ_b的磁矩，以检验QCD非微扰机制及重夸克有效理论在自旋-磁响应中的适用性，并厘清重/轻夸克对磁矩的相对贡献。

Method: 采用外场QCD求和规则（external-field QCD sum rules），显式保留重夸克传播子的全质量依赖，包含其与光子、软胶子及二者同时的耦合；以同一插值场的质量求和规则归一化磁求和规则，消除极点残差；通过质子磁矩标定夸克凝聚磁化率χ；不确定性通过蒙特卡洛方法评估QCD输入误差，并叠加插值场、Borel窗、重夸克质量标度、校准与归一化等系统误差。

Result: 得到Σ_c^{++}、Σ_c^+、Σ_c^0、Σ_b^+、Σ_b^0、Σ_b^-的磁矩分别为2.37±0.65、0.50±0.15、−1.39±0.37、2.16±0.65、0.55±0.16、−1.06±0.32 μ_N；重夸克贡献分别为−0.13±0.02 μ_N（Σ_c）和+0.013±0.003 μ_N（Σ_b）；结果对重夸克质量方案（如MS-bar）具有显著依赖性。

Conclusion: Σ_c和Σ_b磁矩主要由轻双夸克子系统决定，重夸克贡献微小且符号相反（c夸克为负、b夸克为正），反映重夸克自旋屏蔽效应随质量增大而增强；该结果支持重夸克有效理论中重夸克自旋近似冻结的图像，并验证了外场QCD求和规则在重子磁性质计算中的可靠性。

Abstract: We compute the magnetic moments of the $\Sigma_c$ and $\Sigma_b$ isotriplets with external-field QCD sum rules. The heavy-quark propagator is kept with its full mass dependence and with its couplings to the photon, to soft gluons and to both simultaneously, and the magnetic sum rule is normalized by the mass sum rule of the same interpolating field, which removes the residue and gives the baryon masses within a few percent. The quark-condensate magnetic susceptibility $\chi$, which otherwise dominates the uncertainty, is fixed by the proton magnetic moment computed with the same interpolating field; the neutron moment then follows to about $5\%$. Uncertainties from the QCD inputs are evaluated with a Monte Carlo analysis, and those from the interpolating field, the Borel window, the scale of the heavy-quark mass, the calibration and the normalization are added. With $\overline{\rm MS}$ heavy-quark masses we obtain $\mu_{\Sigma_c^{++}}=2.37\pm0.65$, $\mu_{\Sigma_c^{+}}=0.50\pm0.15$, $\mu_{\Sigma_c^{0}}=-1.39\pm0.37$, $\mu_{\Sigma_b^{+}}=2.16\pm0.65$, $\mu_{\Sigma_b^{0}}=0.55\pm0.16$ and $\mu_{\Sigma_b^{-}}=-1.06\pm0.32$ in nuclear magnetons; at this order the results depend sizably on the heavy-quark mass scheme. The moments are carried by the light diquark; the heavy-quark contribution, whose separation depends on the interpolating field, is $-0.13\pm0.02\,\mu_N$ for $\Sigma_c$ and $+0.013\pm0.003\,\mu_N$ for $\Sigma_b$.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究J-PARC E34实验中中微子角动量对缪子反常磁矩测量的可能贡献，指出未观测到的中微子对所携带的角动量会通过正电子能谱调制引入系统不确定性；相较于费米实验室，J-PARC较低的缪子动量需显式处理衰变运动学；作者基于弱衰变理论谱计算中微子对纵向动量积分，并采用统一事件权重N(E)A(E)，发现J-PARC在指定正电子能量区间内该修正比费米实验室小0.415±0.176 ppm（2.36σ）；进一步提出通过改变缪子束流能量（固定正电子阈值200 MeV）可提升检验灵敏度，最优缪子能量为245 MeV，此时与标称J-PARC构型的差异可达4.88σ（最乐观精度假设下）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决缪子反常磁矩（g−2）实验中由未探测中微子对所携角动量引起的潜在系统偏差，该效应可能混淆对标准模型新物理信号的判别，尤其在J-PARC E34这类低能缪子束流实验中不可忽略。

Result: ① J-PARC E34（200–275 MeV e⁺）相较Fermilab（1.5–2.9 GeV e⁺）的中微子角动量修正小0.415±0.176 ppm（2.36σ）；② 固定e⁺阈值200 MeV时，缪子能量245 MeV处灵敏度达峰值，相对于标称J-PARC配置（通常为300 MeV）的修正差异具有4.88σ统计显著性（最乐观实验精度下）。

Abstract: We examine a possible signature of the neutrino angular-momentum contribution to the muon magnetic anomaly measurement in the J-PARC E34 experiment. In previous papers we argued that the angular momentum carried by the unobserved neutrino pair may leave an uncertainty in the anomaly inferred from the time modulation of detected positrons. At Fermilab the highly relativistic muons make the forward-emission approximation suitable for this estimate. The lower muon momentum at J-PARC requires the decay kinematics to be treated explicitly. We integrate the longitudinal momentum of the neutrino pair using the positron energy distribution and asymmetry derived from the theoretical weak-decay spectrum, and the same event-counting weight $N(E)A(E)$ is employed for both experiments. For positron energies between $1.5$ and $2.9$ GeV at Fermilab and between $200$ and $275$ MeV at J-PARC, the proposed correction is smaller at J-PARC by $0.415\pm0.176$ ppm, when the most optimistic E34 precision is assumed. The expected difference amounts to $2.36$ standard deviations if the future measurement is centred on this value. The calculation thus identifies an experimental comparison which may test the proposed effect independently of the hadronic contribution to the Standard Model prediction. We also examine the possibility of testing the effect by comparing measurements at two different muon momenta. With a fixed positron energy threshold of 200 MeV, the maximum sensitivity is obtained at a muon beam energy of 245 MeV. The predicted difference relative to the nominal J-PARC configuration has significance of $4.88\sigma$ for the most optimistic experimental sensitivity.
