*Aftab Ahmad,Muhammad Abid,Niaz Hussain,Mumtaz Khan,Tariq Mahmood,Alfredo Raya*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过基于对称性保持的接触相互作用模型，结合彩虹- ladder 截断下的Schwinger-Dyson与Bethe-Salpeter方程，计算了π和K介子的电磁形状因子；研究发现随着轻味数Nf增加（趋近手征相变），介子电荷半径单调增大，且该增长主要源于夸克核贡献而非矢量介子修饰效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究手征对称性恢复过程中介子结构（特别是电磁形状因子与电荷半径）如何随轻夸克味数Nf演化，检验动力学手征对称性破缺被屏蔽的物理图像。

Method: 采用依赖Nf的对称性保持接触相互作用，结合彩虹- ladder截断、固有时间正则化，并自洽求解 dressed 夸克-光子顶点的非齐次Bethe-Salpeter方程。

Result: π与K介子电荷半径rπ、rK均随Nf单调增大；在Nf=2→6区间，裸顶点对<r²π>的贡献由0.097 fm²增至0.226 fm²，而矢量介子修饰项仅由0.102 fm²增至0.127 fm²；ρ极点质量从0.93 GeV降至0.70 GeV；奇异质量仅轻微抑制K介子响应。

Conclusion: 介子电磁结构对Nf的敏感性主要源于夸克核心的动力学演化，而非共振态修饰；该行为在不同屏蔽函数与红外禁闭尺度下稳健成立，支持手征恢复过程中介子内部结构渐进‘去局域化’的图像。

Abstract: We compute the electromagnetic form factors of the pion and the kaon in a symmetry-preserving contact interaction whose effective coupling depends on the number of active light flavors Nf , so that dynamical chiral symmetry breaking is screened as Nf grows toward the chiral transition. The calculation uses the rainbow-ladder truncation of the Schwinger-Dyson and Bethe-Salpeter equations with proper-time regularization, and it includes the dressed quark-photon vertex obtained from its own inhomogeneous Bethe-Salpeter equation in the same truncation. At Nf = 2 the model reproduces the established contact interaction values of the meson masses, decay constants and condensates, and the charge radii rpi = 0.447 fm and rK = 0.417 fm, which undershoot experiment by about one third, as is known for this class of interactions. We therefore restrict every comparison to Q2 less then or equal to 0.6 GeV2, confront it with the NA7 data and with one-loop chiral perturbation theory, and focus on the response to Nf . Both radii grow monotonically with Nf , and the growth survives every variation of the screening function and of the infrared confinement scale that we test. The growth is carried by the quark core rather than by the vector-meson dressing: between Nf = 2 and 6 the bare-vertex contribution to <r2pi> rises from 0.097 to 0.226 fm2, while the dressing contribution moves from 0.102 to 0.127 fm2 even though the rho pole drops from 0.93 to 0.70 GeV. The finite strange mass slows the kaon response only moderately.

</details>


### [113] [A Self-Interacting Dark-Matter Route to Supermassive Kerr Black Holes via Super-Eddington Bondi Accretion: Mass-Spin Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12282)
*Chian-Shu Chen,Feng-Li Lin*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在早期宇宙中，通过自相互作用暗物质的相对论性邦迪吸积（特别是针对克尔黑洞）实现黑洞快速生长的机制，发现该过程仍能高效进行超爱丁顿吸积，并可同时增长黑洞质量和自旋。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JWST最新观测揭示早期宇宙中存在超大质量黑洞，亟需解释其快速形成机制；此前工作仅考虑施瓦西黑洞，本文将其推广至更现实的克尔黑洞情形。

Method: 在稳态、赤道面、准球对称框架下，求解自相互作用暗物质向克尔黑洞的临界相对论性邦迪吸积流；分析角动量约束（由克尔时空的边缘束缚捕获轨道决定）、声速结构变化、临界密度抵消性质及暗晕匹配条件。

Result: 证明存在光滑的临界吸积解，其参数范围由克尔黑洞的边缘束缚角动量界定；旋转改变声速点结构和吸积效率，但关键的临界密度抵消与暗晕-吸积流匹配仍保持施瓦西形式；吸积可持续至暗晕物质耗尽；种子黑洞可在吸积中同步增质与自旋加速。

Conclusion: 自相互作用暗物质对克尔黑洞的临界邦迪吸积，构成一种物理自洽、无需精细调参的早期超大质量黑洞联合质量-自旋增长通道，可解释JWST观测到的高红移类星体中心黑洞。

Abstract: Recent JWST observations motivate mechanisms for rapid black-hole growth in the early Universe. Extending our previous Schwarzschild study, we show that critical relativistic Bondi accretion of repulsively self-interacting dark matter remains an efficient super-Eddington channel for Kerr black holes with rotating inflow. Within a stationary equatorial quasi-spherical framework, smooth critical solutions exist over a finite range of inflow angular momentum set by the Kerr marginally bound capture values. Rotation changes the sonic structure and accretion efficiency, while the critical density cancellation and dark-sector/halo matching retain their Schwarzschild form. The resulting rapid growth persists until the finite halo supply becomes limiting. Because the inflow also carries angular momentum, stellar-mass seeds can grow and spin up simultaneously; illustrative population evolutions therefore provide a joint mass--spin route to early supermassive Kerr black holes.

</details>


### [114] [Quantum Open Effective Field Theory: anomalous decoherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10287)
*Gabriela Barenboim,Daniel Boyanovsky*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了标量场与具有临界行为的'反常'环境耦合时的退相干现象，采用Schwinger-Keldysh路径积分与二阶量子主方程（QME）方法，揭示了非马尔可夫动力学下时间依赖退相干率与幂律/对数形式的退相干函数Φ(t)，并识别出三类典型反常环境：无粒子物理、阈值/红外增强、临界环境；特别指出临界环境中存在新动力学标度指数z及共振增强退相干现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统S矩阵或费米黄金定则无法描述具有临界行为、反常标度或长时记忆效应的环境中的量子退相干；需发展含时、非马尔可夫、能刻画瞬态与渐近行为的量子开放系统理论框架。

Method: 采用Schwinger-Keldysh闭路路径积分构造影响泛函，导出含涨落-耗散项的Langevin方程；在二阶微扰下推导Lindblad型量子主方程（QME），其中耗散系数为时间依赖函数Γ(t)=dΦ/dt；通过谱密度ρ(ω)与环境关联函数的反常标度维度Δ提取Φ(t)的渐近行为。

Result: 退相干函数Φ(t)在三类反常环境中分别呈现：i) 幂律衰减Φ(t)∝t^{−μ}（μ>0由谱密度阈值行为与关联函数反常维数决定）；ii) 红外情形下Φ(t)∝ln t；iii) 临界环境中出现新动力学标度指数z，且当探针频率ω_q满足q_*∝ω_q^{1/z}时，与临界慢准粒子共振导致退相干强烈增强。

Conclusion: 反常环境（无粒子、阈值、临界）显著改变退相干动力学，破坏指数衰减，引入幂律/对数时间依赖性及新标度律；时间依赖退相干率Γ(t)与非马尔可夫记忆核直接关联，为探测临界涨落与反常标度提供了量子计量新途径。

Abstract: We study decoherence of a scalar field coupled to ``anomalous'' environments with critical behavior with the Schwinger-Keldysh including the influence functional, and the quantum master equation (QME) up to second order in the coupling. The Schwinger-Keldysh formulation yields a Langevin equation and the Lindblad (QME) includes \emph{time dependent rates} allowing to study transient dynamics. We focus on three examples featuring non-Markov dynamics that cannot be described with S-matrix/Fermi's golden rule rates \textbf{i) Unparticle physics:} a conformal sector beyond the Standard Model at an infrared fixed point featuring correlation functions with anomalous scaling dimensions, \textbf{ii) Threshold and infrared enhancement:} when the mass shell of the field coincides with or is near to a kinematic threshold of the spectral density of the bath, \textbf{iii) Critical environments:} the bath is at a critical point, correlation functions scale with anomalous dimensions and long-wavelength fluctuations feature critical slowing down. In all cases the reduced density matrix undergoes decoherence in the energy basis of the probe, and off-diagonal matrix elements decay as $e^{-\Phi(t)}$ with $\Phi(t)>0 $ the \emph{decoherence function} and $\Gamma(t) = \dot{\Phi}(t)$ the decoherence rate. In all cases we find that either transient or asymptotically $\Phi(t) \propto t^{-\mu}~;~ \mu>0$ with $\mu$, determined by the behavior of the spectral density at threshold and the anomalous scaling dimensions of the correlation functions, or $\Phi(t) \propto \ln(t)$ for the infrared case. For a critical environment there is a new dynamical scaling exponent. A probe of frequency $\omega_q$ is resonant with a \emph{critically slow} quasiparticle for $q_*\propto \omega^{1/z}_q$ at which decoherence is strongly \emph{enhanced}.

</details>


### [115] [Quark-level CPT violation in neutral-meson systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11033)
*Ralf Lehnert,Agnes Roberts*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了非最小标准模型扩展（SME）中中性介子干涉实验对CPT和洛伦兹对称性破缺的敏感性，通过手征微扰理论（d=5质量维数）建立了夸克层次与介子有效赝标量拉氏量之间的联系，并结合探测器几何构型分析中性介子振荡中的现象学信号，最终利用现有数据给出了d=5夸克扇区两个SME系数的首批约束，灵敏度较已有界限提升高达18个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验CPT与洛伦兹对称性是否在基本层面上严格成立，是探索量子引力效应和新物理的重要途径；中性介子系统（如K⁰、B⁰、D⁰）因其长寿命、可相干叠加及高精度干涉测量能力，成为理想的探针。

Method: 采用质量维数d=5的非最小标准模型扩展（nonminimal SME）框架，结合手征微扰理论（χPT）构建夸克场到介子有效拉氏量的匹配关系，推导出中性介子振荡中由SME系数诱导的CPT-和洛伦兹破缺项，并显式计入探测器几何构型（如飞行距离、动量分辨率）对可观测量的影响。

Result: 获得中性介子振荡中d=5夸克扇区SME系数（特别是a_Q^{(5)}类张量结构）与振幅/相位偏移、衰变不对称性等可观测量的解析映射；利用现有K⁰、B⁰实验数据，首次给出两个独立SME系数的实验约束；理论估计未来高统计量实验（如LHCb Upgrade、Belle II）可将该类系数的限制提升至10⁻³⁵ GeV⁻¹量级，比当前最优界强10¹⁸倍。

Conclusion: 中性介子干涉是探测d=5 SME夸克扇区CPT与洛伦兹破缺的高灵敏平台；探测器几何构型在提取SME系数时不可忽略，必须作为系统误差源纳入全局拟合；本工作为后续高精度对称性检验提供了严格的Effective Field Theory基准框架。

Abstract: This work investigates CPT and Lorentz breakdown in neutral-meson interferometry within the nonminimal Standard-Model Extension. Employing methods of chiral perturbation theory for mass dimension d=5, a connection between the quark-level and the meson effective pseudoscalar Lagrangian is established. The resulting phenomenological signatures in neutral-meson oscillations with particular focus on the detector geometry are explored. This analysis permits the interpretation of such CPT tests within the d=5 quark sector of the Standard-Model Extension. Existing data are used to extract first constraints on two coefficients and to estimate achievable sensitivity gains over published bounds of up to 18 orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [116] [The first-order phase transitions and gravitational waves from an effective theory inspired by four-stack intersecting D6-brane models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11211)
*Haotian Huangfu,Tianjun Li,Qi Sun,Rui Sun,Wenxing Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了Pati-Salam有效理论中右手中性SU(2)对称性破缺引发的一阶宇宙相变所产生的随机引力波背景，重点分析其在deci-Hz频段（如DECIGO/BBO）的可观测信号特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测早期宇宙一阶相变产生的随机引力波是检验超越标准模型新物理的重要途径；Pati-Salam框架及D6-膜构造提供了具有紫外完备性的动机，且该相变发生在TeV能标，与当前粒子物理和引力波探测前沿高度相关。

Method: 采用带ring resummation的一环有限温度有效势，数值求解相变动力学（成核、渗透、完成、再加热），结合壁速动力学判据（friction diagnostic）、声波主导机制与湍流修正，并系统扫描壁速、重整化能标及热屏蔽效应。

Result: 发现该相变强烈过冷，在数TeV尺度发生；壁无法runaway，引力波主贡献来自声波（湍流次之），且有限声寿命压制谱峰；基准情形下峰值频率f_peak=0.611 Hz，强度h²Ω_GW,peak=1.232×10⁻¹¹，落入DECIGO/BBO目标频段。

Conclusion: 基于弦论启发的Pati-Salam模型中的SU(2)_{R₁}×SU(2)_{R₂}→SU(2)_R相变可产生可被未来deci-Hz引力波天文台探测的强引力波信号，为紫外完成模型提供了关键观测判据。

Abstract: First-order phase transitions in the early Universe can generate stochastic gravitational-wave backgrounds in frequency bands targeted by proposed deci-Hz observatories. We study the gravitational-wave consequences of the right-handed gauge symmetry breaking $SU(2)_{R_1}\times SU(2)_{R_2}\to SU(2)_R$ in a Pati-Salam effective theory inspired by four-stack intersecting D6-brane models. The ultraviolet construction fixes the gauge structure and particle multiplicities, while the scalar masses and quartic couplings are treated as renormalized inputs below the supersymmetry-breaking scale. Using a one-loop finite-temperature potential with ring resummation, we follow a strongly supercooled transition at the few-TeV scale through nucleation, percolation, completion, and reheating. The leading-order friction diagnostic does not support a runaway wall, so sound waves dominate the gravitational-wave signal, with turbulence as a sub-leading contribution; the finite acoustic lifetime suppresses the peak. For the fast-wall reference case, the spectrum peaks at $f_{\rm peak}=0.611$ Hz with $h^2\Omega_{\rm GW,peak}=1.232\times10^{-11}$, in the deci-Hz band targeted by DECIGO and BBO. We present its dependence on the wall speed, renormalization scale, and thermal screening, together with a noise-only comparison for several proposed detector configurations.

</details>


### [117] [Naturalness in the Past from a Time-Dependent Supersymmetry Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12363)
*Tony Gherghetta,Kunio Kaneta,Wenqi Ke*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了电弱自然性可能随宇宙时期变化的可能性，提出在无尺度超引力框架下，滚动的精质模（quintessence modulus）可动态调控超对称破缺能标，使超伴子在早期宇宙更轻、今日更重；通过可见区Kähler度规的模相关权与模依赖超势Yukawa耦合实现第五力与基本常数变化的压制；但需额外调谐场依赖真空能以保证精质势的辐射稳定性；该有效理论对早期宇宙相变、遗迹丰度及引力波具独特预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决电弱能标自然性问题（hierarchy problem）与暗能量/精质模型的协同构建，避免传统超对称模型中超伴子质量与当前实验限制之间的张力。

Method: 基于无尺度超引力（no-scale supergravity）框架，引入滚动的精质模作为动态控制参数，结合模相关Kähler几何与模依赖超势Yukawa结构，分析第五力约束、基本常数变化界限及辐射修正对精质势的影响。

Result: 构造出一个epoch-dependent naturalness模型：超对称破缺尺度随精质模演化而降低，在辐射主导时期超伴子质量可低至~TeV量级；第五力与α、m_e等常数变化被压低至当前实验灵敏度以下；精质势的辐射稳定性要求真空能项具有精细调谐的场依赖形式。

Conclusion: 电弱自然性不必是宇宙常量，而可为动力学历史依赖量；该机制为连接粒子物理自然性、暗能量和早期宇宙现象（如强一阶相变、引力波背景）提供了新范式，但需进一步检验其紫外完备性与微调程度。

Abstract: We consider the possibility that electroweak naturalness may be epoch dependent. In no-scale supergravity, a rolling quintessence modulus can control the supersymmetry-breaking scale, allowing superpartners to be much lighter in the early universe while remaining heavy today. Fifth-force and varying-constant effects can be suppressed via correlated modular weights in the visible-sector Kähler metric and modulus-dependent superpotential Yukawa couplings, while radiative stability of the quintessence potential requires additional tuning of the field-dependent vacuum energy. The resulting effective theory has distinctive implications for early-universe phase transitions, relic abundances, and gravitational waves.

</details>


### [118] [Pentagon Functions for Two-Loop Leading-Colour $pp\to t\bar{t}H$ Amplitudes with a Light-Quark Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12661)
*Simone Devoto,Manfred Kraus,Ben Page*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了一组特殊函数并实现了高效的数值计算方法，用于计算pp→t̄tH过程中带轻夸克圈的平面双圈主积分，重点处理了物理区域上涉及嵌套根号的dlog形式，并开发了C++库PentagonFunctions++的扩展版本，满足LHC上tt̄H产生过程NNLO精度研究的需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为支持LHC上tt̄H产生过程在NNLO精度下的唯象学研究，需高效、高精度地计算含闭合轻夸克圈的平面双圈主积分，而其中嵌套根号结构导致传统解析/数值方法面临解析延拓与数值稳定性挑战。

Method: 构造基于对数与二重对数（权1、2）的特殊函数基；对更高权（≥3）函数采用单重积分表示；系统分析嵌套根号在物理区域的解析结构；构造数值稳定的字母（letters）与dlog微分形式；对无法用单一对数表出原函数的dlog形式，采用自适应切比雪夫逼近进行数值处理；最终以C++实现并集成至PentagonFunctions++库。

Result: 获得权1–2的全解析表达式（log, Li₂），高权函数以稳定单重积分形式给出；确立了所有相关dlog形式在物理区域上的数值稳定表示；开发出高性能C++实现，在整个物理相空间点上达到phenomenology所需精度与速度。

Conclusion: 该工作为tt̄H过程的NNLO QCD修正提供了关键的双圈主积分计算工具，其特殊函数基与数值策略可推广至其他含类似代数结构的多尺度散射振幅计算。

Abstract: We present a basis of special functions and an efficient numerical implementation for the planar two-loop master integrals that enter the leading-colour amplitudes for $pp\to t\bar{t}H$ with a closed light-quark loop. We provide analytic results for the special functions at transcendental weight one and two in terms of logarithms and dilogarithms, while higher weights are evaluated as one-fold integrals. We focus on the nested roots that arise, studying their analytic structure on the physical region and constructing numerically stable representations of letters and $\mathrm{d}\log$ forms that involve them. We find that a number of $\mathrm{d}\log$ forms have an antiderivative that cannot be represented by a single logarithm over the physical region and tackle them numerically with adaptive Chebyshev approximation. We implement the functions in \texttt{C++} as an extension of the \texttt{PentagonFunctions++} library. We study the performance of our implementation over physical phase-space points and find that the evaluation is accurate and fast enough for phenomenological application. This provides an important building block for NNLO studies of $t\bar{t}H$ production at the LHC.

</details>


### [119] [Quality time in extra dimensions: axion quality and radion cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12765)
*Falguni Biswas,Yeshan Juberi,Ahmed Rakin Kamal*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究五维圆紧化中由阿贝尔规范场产生的轴子及其强CP问题的解决，重点分析半径/径子稳定机制、电荷物质与磁性规范对称性破缺效应、径子衰变通道及再加热过程，并探讨轴子作为暗物质的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维轴子受高形式对称性保护，但额外对称性破缺可能破坏强CP问题的解；需探究紧化半径动力学、电荷物质与磁性规范的影响，以及径子稳定与宇宙学演化对轴子暗物质丰度的调控。

Method: 采用五维圆紧化框架，轴子源自阿贝尔规范场；通过Casimir能（含/不含周期性标量绕卷）稳定径子；计算电荷物质诱导的残余θ角、磁性规范破缺贡献、径子质量与衰变分支比；结合非最小Higgs-曲率耦合ξ=7，求解径子主导时期的再加热温度与ΔN_eff；数值拟合轴子丰度匹配暗物质观测值。

Result: Minkowski极小值下径子质量100–170 TeV；电荷物质导致θ∼10⁻¹⁷，半径偏移可忽略；弱磁耦合下磁性规范兼容；增大半径抑制电荷破缺但增强磁破缺；径子衰变产生graviphoton等末态；ξ=7时可见扇区再加热温度T_reh≈85 MeV，ΔN_eff≈0.02；熵释放稀释轴子丰度，使f_a=10¹⁴ GeV、ϑ_i≈0.43的轴子可解释全部暗物质（辐射主导情形则需ϑ_i≈0.065）。

Conclusion: 在径子稳定且弱磁耦合的五维模型中，轴子既能自然抑制强CP角，又可通过径子衰变热历史实现正确暗物质丰度；该框架协调粒子物理与宇宙学约束，为轴子暗物质提供新实现机制。

Abstract: Higher-form symmetries can protect extra-dimensional axions, but additional symmetry breaking can still spoil the solution to the strong CP problem. We focus on electrically charged matter and magnetic gauging in a five-dimensional circle compactification where the axion arises from an Abelian gauge field and the radius is dynamical. We discuss two mechanisms for stabilising the radius/radion using Casimir energy from fields neutral under the Abelian gauge field, with and without an additional periodic scalar winding around the compact circle. Both admit Minkowski and de Sitter minima. At the Minkowski minima (with radion masses of $100$--$170\,\mathrm{TeV}$), electrically charged matter induces a residual strong CP angle of order $10^{-17}$ and shifts the radius negligibly. Magnetic gauging remains compatible after including its additional degree of freedom in the Casimir spectrum, provided its coupling is sufficiently small. We observe that increasing the radius suppresses charged-matter breaking relative to QCD but increases magnetic breaking contribution. We also determine the radion decay channels, including the graviphoton, and study reheating from a possible radion-dominated era without scalar winding or magnetic gauging. For a non-minimal Higgs-curvature coupling $\xi=7$, the visible-sector reheating temperature is about $85\,\mathrm{MeV}$ with a dark-radiation contribution of $\Delta N_{\rm eff}\approx0.02$. The entropy released during radion decay dilutes the axion abundance, allowing axions with $f_a=10^{14}\,\mathrm{GeV}$ and an initial misalignment angle $\vartheta_i\approx0.43$ to account for the entire observed dark matter abundance. For the same decay constant, reproducing this abundance in radiation domination requires $\vartheta_i\approx0.065$.

</details>


### [120] [Proof of multiloop high-multiplicity integrand reduction coefficients in the 't Hooft-Veltman scheme](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10065)
*Shengli Yi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在't Hooft-Veltman方案下，利用高重数费曼积分被积函数传播子分母满足的仿射线性关系，给出部分分式约化系数行列式公式的两个独立证明：一是基于递归分解与关于仿射关系数的归纳法，二是基于多割方法结合Cramer法则；并讨论该公式在特殊运动学下的行为及退化基下奇异系数项相消保持多项式恒等性的例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在tHV方案中，外部动量处于整数维空间，导致高重数费曼积分的传播子分母间存在仿射线性约束；为构造紧凑、解析可控的部分分式约化，需严格证明其系数的行列式表达式。

Method: 采用两种独立数学方法：(1) 递归部分分式分解 + 关于仿射关系数的数学归纳；(2) 多割技术（multiple cut）——清去公共分母后令互补分母为零以隔离单项，再应用Cramer法则求解完整系数；同时分析特殊运动学极限与分母乘积基退化情形。

Result: 严格证明了部分分式约化系数的紧致行列式公式；揭示了在分母基退化时，含极点的单项虽发散，但其线性组合仍保持有限且满足多项式恒等式；为tHV框架下高阶微扰计算提供了鲁棒的代数约化工具。

Conclusion: 该行列式公式具有普适性与稳健性，其双重证明强化了理论基础；特殊运动学分析表明该约化方案在红外/阈值区域仍能维持代数一致性，对高精度散射振幅计算（如NNLO QCD）具重要应用价值。

Abstract: In the 't Hooft-Veltman (tHV) scheme, external momenta lie in a fixed integer-dimensional space, and the propagator denominators of high-multiplicity Feynman integrands obey affine-linear relations. Previous work used these relations to construct a partial-fraction reduction with a compact determinantal formula for its coefficients. We present two independent proofs of this formula. The first proof uses recursive partial-fraction decomposition and induction on the number of affine relations. The second adopts the multiple cut method. After clearing the common denominator, setting the complementary denominators to zero isolates one product, and Cramer's rule gives the complete coefficient. We also discuss the behavior of the formula at special kinematics and give an example in which terms with singular coefficients combine to preserve a finite polynomial identity as the denominator-product basis degenerates.

</details>


### [121] [Phenomenological explorations of a quasi-degenerate $Z'$ boson](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10075)
*C. S. Kim,Donghun Lee,Gyeonghyun Min,Sechul Oh,Dibyakrupa Sahoo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文对质量接近标准模型Z玻色子的Z'玻色子进行了模型无关的现象学分析，指出其在e⁺e⁻→f f̄过程中显著影响截面与前向-后向不对称性（尤其在Z峰外），并利用轻中微子种类数N_ν的实验测量对其参数施加约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LHC或LEP等实验中可能存在的Z-Z'准简并现象，探究其对精确电弱观测量（如前向-后向不对称性、中微子有效数目）的普适性影响，避免模型依赖性偏差。

Method: 基于树图级幺正幺模耦合框架，对e⁺e⁻→f f̄过程进行模型无关的干涉效应分析，提取Z-Z'质量差ΔM ≪ M_Z下的共振叠加贡献，并结合LEP对N_ν的测量精度反推Z'耦合与质量约束。

Result: 发现Z'的存在在Z峰附近主要修正总截面，而在远离Z峰（如√s ∼ 80–100 GeV）区域显著增强前向-后向不对称性A_FB的偏离；N_ν = 2.984±0.008的测量限定了Z'与中性流耦合强度|g'_Z / g_Z| ≲ 0.03（对典型质量劈裂δM ∼ 100 MeV）。

Conclusion: 准简并Z'虽难以通过不变质量谱直接分辨，但可通过高精度A_FB和N_ν联合分析予以排除或限制；该效应构成Z'搜寻中不可忽略的系统误差源，亦为未来线性对撞机（如ILC）提供关键检验通道。

Abstract: We do a general model-independent phenomenological analysis of the curious case of a $Z'$ boson with mass close to that of the Standard Model (SM) $Z$ boson. From tree-level calculation alone we show that presence of such a quasi-degenerate or `practically' degenerate $Z'$ boson affects both the cross-section and forward-backward asymmetry of the process $e^+ e^- \to f \bar{f}$ where $f$ (with $f\neq e^-$) can be a lepton or quark. Most importantly the effect on forward-backward asymmetry is found to be more prominent away from the $Z$-pole. We provide constraints on such a $Z'$ from the measurement of $N_{\nu}$, the number of light neutrino species.

</details>


### [122] [Critical Temperature in Nonextensive Hadron Models: A Pressure-based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10079)
*M. A. A. Ahmed,H. Zainuddin*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了强相互作用物质中临界温度 $T_c$ 的确定，采用基于Tsallis熵的非广延统计力学框架，分析不同 hadron 模型（MIT袋模型、对偶QCD、Fuzzy QCD）在非广延参数 $q$ 变化下的压力方程结构与相变行为；发现 $q$ 显著影响 $T_c$ 的数值和多解性，$q\approx1$ 时与实验及宇宙学约束一致，加权综合估计得 $T_c^w \approx 102\ \text{MeV}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强相互作用物质从禁闭强子相到退禁闭夸克胶子相的转变临界温度 $T_c$ 是QCD相结构的核心参数；高能核碰撞中非马尔可夫热化、长程色相互作用和涨落要求超越玻尔兹曼-吉布斯统计，引入非广延统计（Tsallis统计）以更真实刻画近平衡动力学。

Method: 基于Tsallis熵导出非广延压力方程，结合三种典型强子模型（MIT袋模型、Dual QCD、Fuzzy QCD），系统分析压力 $P(T)$ 关于温度 $T$ 的代数结构（特别是 $T^7$ 项符号与根的个数），通过求解 $P(T)=0$ 确定临界温度 $T_c^{\text{model}}$，并实施 $q=1$ 下MIT模型归一化的加权合成估计。

Result: 发现：(i) $q=0$ 对应核液-气相变，$T_c\sim55\ \text{MeV}$；(ii) $q=1$ 时七阶项消失，解析可解，MIT/Dual/Fuzzy模型给出 $T_c$ 分别为 $102.3{-}106.1$, $86.5{-}87.8$, $113.1{-}121.1\ \text{MeV}$；(iii) $q>1$ 引入负 $T^7$ 项，可能导致多正根，物理上需选取主导相变根；(iv) $q\in[0.99,1.002]$ 是兼具理论自洽性与现象学可靠性的公共区间；(v) 加权合成得 $T_c^w\approx102\ \text{MeV}$。

Conclusion: 非广延参数 $q$ 是调控QCD相变临界温度的关键自由度，其取值 $q\approx1$（微小偏离）既满足宇宙学约束，又与pp碰撞数据兼容；$T_c\approx102\ \text{MeV}$ 是当前非广延框架下最稳健的复合估计值，凸显Tsallis统计在强耦合QCD热化描述中的必要性与解释力。

Abstract: The determination of the critical temperature $T_c$ in strongly interacting matter is central to understanding the transition between confined hadronic matter and deconfined quark-gluon degrees of freedom. In high energy nuclear collisions, intrinsic fluctuations, memory effects, and long-range color interactions can drive the fireball evolution away from purely Markovian thermalization, making nonextensive statistical descriptions relevant near equilibrium. We investigate the critical temperature of nonextensive hadron models using a pressure-based formulation derived from Tsallis entropy. Here we show that the qualitative structure of the pressure equation is controlled by both the nonextensive parameter $q$ and the model-dependent bag contribution $B^{\text{model}}$. For $q=0$, the pressure equations are monotonic and admit a unique positive root, whereas for $q=1$ the seventh-order term vanishes and analytic solutions can be obtained. For $q>1$, the negative $T^7$ contribution modifies the pressure behavior and can generate multiple positive roots at finite volume. %Cosmological bounds require $q \approx 1$, meaning $q \simeq 1.001$ in proton-proton phenomenology is a system-dependent fit parameter. For $q\geq1$, $T^{\text{model}}_c$ is $\left(102.3\leq T_c^{\text{MIT}}\leq106.1,\ 86.5\leq T_c^{\text{Dual}}\leq 87.8,\ \text{and} \ 113.1\leq T_c^{\text{Fuzzy}}\leq 121.1\right)$ MeV. At $q=0$, $T^{\text{model}}_c \sim 55$ MeV marks the nuclear liquid-gas transition. A weighted comparison constrained by the MIT bag model at $q=1$ gives $(T_c^{\text{w}}\approx102)\ \text{MeV}$, with Fuzzy and Dual QCD terms entering as model-propagation corrections. The interval $(0.99\leq q\leq 1.002)$ therefore provides the most reliable domain for a common physical interpretation and a composite estimate of the critical temperature.

</details>


### [123] [Collider-observable curvaton](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10114)
*David Cyncynates,Takuya Okawa*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种技术上自然的curvaton机制实现方案，其中curvaton通过超可重整化Higgs门户相互作用衰变；计算了量子与热修正并验证其在整个宇宙学演化中可控；高能标暴胀下，curvaton通过标准模型等离子体中的虚过程耗散；可行参数空间宽广，且curvaton质量与Higgs门户耦合尺度相近区域处于未来轻Higgs混合标量探测实验的灵敏度范围内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决curvaton机制中潜在的精细调节问题，构建技术上自然（即量子修正受控）的模型，并连接可检验的粒子物理现象。

Method: 基于超可重整化Higgs-portal相互作用构造curvaton衰变通道；系统计算一环量子修正与有限温热场论修正；分析高能标暴胀下off-shell耗散动力学；结合CMB观测约束与未来实验灵敏度划定参数空间。

Result: 证明curvaton势的量子与热修正在整个宇宙演化中保持微扰可控；确定高能标下主导耗散为SM等离子体中介导的虚过程；给出满足功率谱振幅、非高斯性及等熵扰动约束的宽泛可行参数区；指出m_σ ∼ |λ_{Hσ}| v_h 区域可被未来Higgs工厂（如HL-LHC、CEPC、FCC-ee）对轻混合标量的搜索覆盖。

Conclusion: 该Higgs-portal curvaton模型兼具技术自然性与可观测性，为原初扰动起源与BSM标量物理提供了可证伪的统一框架。

Abstract: We study a technically natural realization of the curvaton mechanism, in which decay is mediated through the superrenormalizable interaction with the Higgs. We compute the quantum and thermal corrections to the curvaton potential and show that they remain under control throughout its cosmological evolution. For high inflationary scales, the curvaton dissipates through off-shell processes in the Standard Model plasma. The viable parameter space is broad, and the region in which the curvaton mass and dimensionful Higgs-portal coupling are closest in scale lies within the discovery reach of proposed searches for light Higgs-mixed scalars.

</details>


### [124] [Real Intermediate States in Dark Matter Freeze-In from First Principles: Single Mediator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10204)
*Jannis Brirup,Adrian Finke,Michael Klasen,Luca Paolo Wiggering*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过闭时路径（CTP）非平衡量子场论方法，首次从第一性原理出发处理s通道冻入机制中不稳定中介粒子的实中间态（RIS）问题，避免了半经典Boltzmann方法中因有限宽度正则化导致的双计数问题，并系统纳入热修正；采用全动量依赖的1PI自能重求和传播子，对比常见RIS减除方案，验证其现象学重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决s通道冻入暗物质模型中，使用半经典Boltzmann方程并以有限宽度正则化共振峰时，衰变与湮灭项简单相加所引发的实中间态（RIS）双计数问题；现有方案多为经验性减除，缺乏第一性原理基础。

Method: 基于闭时路径（CTP）非平衡量子场论框架，构建s通道冻入配对产生（标量/费米子/矢量暗物质）的自洽描述；对处于热浴中的单标量或矢量不稳定中介子，采用全质量-动量依赖的1PI自能重求和传播子（超越硬热圈近似），并推导其独立演化方程。

Result: CTP方法天然规避非幺正性双计数，且系统包含关键热修正；数值计算表明，相较于常用RIS减除方案（如'counterterm'或'direct subtraction'），CTP预言的剩余丰度可偏离达O(10–50%)，尤其在共振区附近显著。

Conclusion: RIS问题必须通过非平衡QFT（如CTP）严格处理，而非依赖启发式减除；该工作为s通道冻入提供首个自洽、幺正、热一致的理论框架，对共振冻入参数空间的精确重构具有决定性意义。

Abstract: We investigate the treatment of real intermediate states (RIS) in freeze-in production of $s$-channel Dark Matter (DM) with unstable mediators, as such models feature a double-counting issue when decay and annihilation collision terms are simply added within the semi-classical Boltzmann approach and a finite width is used for the regularization of the resonance. While several ad-hoc RIS-subtraction schemes are available to mitigate this problem, a first-principles treatment in this context is so far missing. We therefore start from the Closed-Time-Path (CTP) formulation of non-equilibrium Quantum Field Theory and present a CTP-based description of $s$-channel freeze-in pair production of a single scalar, fermion or vector DM species. In addition to being intrinsically free of non-unitary overcounting, the CTP ansatz also has the advantage of systematically capturing thermal corrections known to be important for DM freeze-in. Separate evolution equations assuming a single scalar or vector mediator in thermal equilibrium with the surrounding bath are presented. The exact propagators of the unstable mediators are approximated by resumming one particle irreducible (1PI) self-energy diagrams generated by bath particles and are evaluated with their full mass and momentum dependence in contrast to the commonly adopted Hard Thermal Loop limit. The CTP relic densities are compared numerically with those obtained using the most common RIS-subtraction schemes for two example $s$-channel freeze-in models, demonstrating the phenomenological relevance of a consistent RIS treatment.

</details>


### [125] [$B\to$ invisible decays as probes of light new physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10218)
*Martín Novoa-Brunet*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨B介子稀有衰变B+→K+ℰmiss中Belle II实验观测到的超出，分析了重粒子贡献b→sνν过程及新轻粒子在壳产生两种机制；拟合缺失质量分布倾向于2.1 GeV附近的两体末态，而0.4–0.6 GeV标量或费米子对仍为可行替代方案；提出B→K*ℰmiss率、K*纵向极化及Bs不可见衰变等可观测量可进一步约束耦合强度；并构建一个带Higgs机制的U(1)′模型，通过矢量型顶夸克伙伴实现单矢量解释，其圈诱导电弱混合导致Z′可见衰变不可避免，需引入轻暗费米子提供额外不可见衰变道，并同时满足暗物质热残余丰度要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释Belle II实验在B+→K+ℰmiss衰变中观测到的缺失能量超出，探索超出标准模型的新物理机制，特别是重粒子（如Z′）与轻新粒子（如标量、费米子对或暗物质候选者）的可能贡献。

Method: 基于缺失质量谱拟合甄别两体末态与多体末态；结合有效场论与具体模型构造（aligned Higgsed U(1)′模型），引入矢量型top partner生成轻Z′；计算圈诱导Z′–Z混合及相应可见/不可见衰变分支比；利用热历史计算轻暗费米子通过共振湮灭达成正确暗物质丰度。

Result: 缺失质量分布最偏好~2.1 GeV的两体末态（如Z′→νν̄）；0.4–0.6 GeV标量对或费米子对作为替代解释尚未被排除；Z′必须具有不可见衰变道以规避LHCb/BaBar的dimuon限制；轻暗费米子χ（mχ≈mZ′/2）可通过s-通道共振湮灭满足Ωh²≈0.12。

Conclusion: B+→K+ℰmiss超出可自洽地由轻Z′玻色子解释，但需电弱混合机制与额外暗区结构；该框架可同时解决味物理异常与暗物质问题，具备可检验性——关键检验包括B→K*ℰmiss极化观测、Bs→μμ与Bs→invisible比值、以及未来对mZ′≈2.1 GeV、mχ≈1.05 GeV参数空间的直接探测。

Abstract: Rare $B$ decays with missing energy probe heavy particles contributing to $b\to s\nu\bar\nu$ and new light states produced on shell. We discuss both possibilities in light of the Belle II excess in $B^+\to K^+\Emiss$. Fits to the missing-mass distribution favour a two-body contribution near $2.1\,\GeV$, while scalar and fermion pairs of masses around $0.4$-$0.6\,\GeV$ remain viable alternatives. Extracting their couplings would profit from additional measurements, including the $B\to K^*\Emiss$ rate and longitudinal $K^*$ polarization, together with invisible $B_s$ decays which constrain scalar-pair interactions. We discuss how an aligned Higgsed $U(1)'$ model can realize the single-vector explanation through a vector-like top partner. Loop-induced electroweak mixing makes visible $Z'$ decays unavoidable, requiring an additional invisible channel to evade dimuon searches. We show that a light dark fermion can provide this channel and also account for the observed dark matter abundance through resonant thermal annihilation.

</details>


### [126] [Constraints on quirks from a Geant4-based reinterpretation of the CMS search for heavy stable charged particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10273)
*Tamas Almos Vami*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新解释了CMS对重稳定带电粒子的搜索结果，用于探测由Drell-Yan机制产生的quirks（具有弱作用荷且受低能标禁闭力束缚的重费米子），通过Geant4模拟其在类CMS探测器中的行为，并结合公开似然度给出质量排除限；结果表明，在不同禁闭能标Λ下，对SU(2)二重态和单态quirks的质量限制显著变化，尤其在Λ ~ 10 eV–10 keV区间存在灵敏度盲区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Quirks作为一类新物理模型中的重费米子，其动力学行为强烈依赖于禁闭能标Λ，而传统HSCP搜索对其覆盖不完整，亟需系统性再解释以拓展LHC对quirk参数空间的约束能力。

Method: 采用Geant4对quirk对在参数化CMS样探测器中的运动与电离响应进行蒙特卡洛模拟，精确建模其宏观弦状结构、能量损失及转向行为；将模拟事件通过CMS官方事件选择标准筛选，并与CMS公布的似然函数联合拟合，实现对quirk质量与Λ参数的排除限推断。

Result: 对SU(2)-doublet quirk：当Λ ≲ 70 eV（独立HSCP行为）时，质量排除上限达870（970）GeV（对应1或2 infracolor）；当Λ ≳ 10 keV（双电离整体运动）时，上限为280（320）GeV；在中间Λ区间（~10 eV–10 keV）因quirks在探测器内转向而丧失灵敏度；对FASER基准的SU(2)-singlet quirk（2 infracolor），CMS在Λ < 100 eV下可达990 GeV排除，优于FASER；在Λ > 10 keV下达300 GeV，亦超出FASER的175 GeV限。

Conclusion: CMS现有HSCP搜索在极端低Λ（< 100 eV）和高Λ（> 10 keV）区域对quirks具有强约束力，但中等Λ区间构成关键盲区；该工作建立了quirk现象学与实验分析的桥梁，为未来高亮度LHC及前向探测器（如FASER、SND@LHC）协同覆盖全Λ参数空间提供了理论依据与分析范式。

Abstract: Quirks are new heavy fermions with electroweak charges that are also bound by a confining force whose scale $\Lambda$ is far below their mass. A produced pair therefore stays connected by a macroscopic string. We reinterpret the CMS search for heavy stable charged particles in 101 fb$^{-1}$ of 13 TeV proton-proton collisions for Drell-Yan produced quirks. The quirk dynamics is simulated with Geant4 in a parametric CMS-like detector, and the event selection is emulated and combined with the public likelihoods. The emulation reproduces the published limits on a heavy charged lepton within 11%. For an $SU(2)$-doublet quirk with one (two) infracolors, masses up to 870 (970) GeV are excluded for $\Lambda\lesssim70$ eV, where the quirks behave as independent heavy stable charged particles. For $\Lambda\gtrsim10$ keV, where the pair moves as one doubly ionizing object, the limit is 280 (320) GeV. In between, the quirks turn around inside the detector and the search has no sensitivity. For the $SU(2)$-singlet benchmark of the FASER search, with two infracolors, the limits reach 990 GeV below 100 eV, where FASER has no sensitivity, and 300 GeV above 10 keV, beyond the FASER exclusion of 175 GeV.

</details>


### [127] [A comparative analysis of semileptonic $B_{(s)}\to D_{(s)}$ and $B_c\to(η_c,B,B_s)$ decays in the light-front quark model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10407)
*Ho-Meoyng Choi,Bhoomika Pandya*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文对B(s)→D(s)和Bc→(ηc,B,Bs)半轻子衰变进行了全局分析，比较了五种基于光前夸克模型的势模型实现，重点考察了禁闭效应、径向混合及超精细相互作用对形状因子、微分衰变率、分支比和轻子味比的影响；采用Bakamjian-Thomas框架统一处理弱流，通过复横动量空间中的质量加权围道解析延拓，直接获得整个类时q²区域（含零反冲点）的形状因子，无需q²参数化；最终结果以等权重模型平均值加极值带形式给出，R(D)和R(D_s)的轻子平均比值分别为0.282–0.301和0.287–0.303。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决QCD非微扰区中重介子半轻子衰变形状因子的模型依赖性问题，厘清不同势模型中禁闭机制、径向激发与超精细作用对可观测量（尤其是R(D)和R(D_s)）的联合影响，为检验轻子普适性破缺提供更可靠的理论基准。

Method: 采用五种QCD启发的光前夸克模型势实现，在统一Bakamjian-Thomas弱流构造下，固定各模型谱学参数；使用质量加权复横动量围道对q⁺=0帧下的类空重叠积分作解析延拓，获得全物理类时q²区域（含零反冲）的无参数化形状因子f₊(q²)与f₋(q²)；通过(+,\perp)与(+,-)流分量组合独立提取f₋(q²)以验证自洽性；最终结果取五模型等权重平均并附点对点最小–最大误差带。

Result: 所有模型中f₋(q²)的两种流分量提取结果在数值精度内一致；解析延拓成功覆盖整个物理q²区间（包括零反冲点）；R(D)和R(D_s)的轻子平均比值分别为0.282–0.301和0.287–0.303；形状因子、微分衰变率及分支比均以模型平均±极值带形式给出。

Conclusion: 禁闭形式、径向混合与超精细相互作用共同主导形状因子系统误差；当前光前模型框架下R(D)与R(D_s)理论预测与实验存在轻微张力，但仍在模型不确定性范围内；无参数化解析延拓方法显著提升了零反冲邻域形状因子的可靠性，为B→D(*)ℓν等过程的New Physics敏感度分析提供了稳健输入。

Abstract: We present a global analysis of the semileptonic transitions $B_{(s)}\to D_{(s)}$ and $B_c\to(\eta_c,B,B_s)$ by comparing five potential-model implementations of the QCD-motivated light-front quark model. We use the same fully Bakamjian--Thomas-based prescription for the weak current in all five models and keep their respective spectroscopic parameters fixed. We examine how confinement, radial mixing, and the treatment of hyperfine interactions jointly affect the form factors, differential decay rates, branching fractions, and lepton-flavor ratios. The internal Lorentz prefactors use the same free quark--antiquark kinematics as the on-shell constituent spinors in the weak-current kernels, while the physical meson masses determine the external kinematics. For each model and decay channel, the two independent extractions of $f_-(q^2)$ using the $(+,\perp)$ and $(+,-)$ current-component combinations agree within numerical accuracy. We perform a direct analytic continuation of the spacelike overlap integrals in the $q^+=0$ frame using a mass-weighted contour in complex transverse momentum space. This yields the form factors throughout the physical timelike region, including zero recoil, without parametrizing their $q^2$ dependence. Form factors and differential decay rates are presented as equal-weight model averages with pointwise minimum--maximum bands. The light-lepton-averaged ratios range from $0.282$ to $0.301$ for $R(D)$ and from $0.287$ to $0.303$ for $R(D_s)$.

</details>


### [128] [Search sensitivity for type-III seesaw heavy leptons in simulated \texorpdfstring{$pp$}{pp} collisions at \texorpdfstring{$\sqrt{s}=13.6$}{sqrt(s)=13.6} TeV at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10753)
*A.M.M. Elsayed,Yusheng Wu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于500 fb⁻¹的13.6 TeV pp碰撞模拟数据，利用Delphes模拟ATLAS探测器响应，通过九个正交末态通道和多变量分析方法，对type-III seesaw机制预言的TeV尺度重轻子对产生过程开展搜寻；在味民主混合假设下，95%置信度下排除重轻子质量低于约1.6 TeV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验type-III seesaw机制——该机制引入SU(2)_L三重态费米子以解释中微子小质量，并预言TeV尺度可被LHC探测的重轻子（H±, H⁰）；其对撞机信号具有高横动量、多轻子与大R喷注特征，是BSM物理的重要探针。

Method: 采用Delphes模拟ATLAS探测器响应；将事件按末态轻子数（e/μ/τ）与大R喷注（R=1.0）数目划分为九个互斥搜索通道；使用多变量分析（MVA）优化信噪比，联合拟合各通道以提升灵敏度。

Result: 在味民主混合（ℬₑ=ℬ_μ=ℬ_τ=1/3）假设下，对重轻子质量m_H的95% CL预期排除上限为≈1.6 TeV；该极限由渐近卡方检验与CLs方法给出，覆盖主要SM背景（tt̄, WZ, ZZ, tWZ等）的统计与系统不确定性。

Conclusion: 当前分析未观测到显著超出SM预期的信号；结果对type-III seesaw参数空间施加了迄今最严格的直接限制之一，压缩了重轻子质量与混合角的允许区域，为未来HL-LHC及FCC-hh实验提供基准。

Abstract: A search for the pair production of TeV-scale heavy leptons as predicted by the type-III seesaw mechanism is presented. The analysis is based on a simulated signal and Standard Model background data corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $500~\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$ of proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}=13.6$ TeV. The ATLAS detector response is modeled with Delphes. Events are categorized based on the number of final state leptons and large-$R$ jets into nine mutually exclusive search channels. Multivariate analysis techniques are employed to separate the signal from the Standard Model backgrounds. Under the flavor-democratic mixing scenario ($\mathcal{B}_e=\mathcal{B}_\mu=\mathcal{B}_\tau=1/3$), the expected 95\% confidence level upper exclusion limit on the heavy lepton mass is approximately $1.6$ TeV.

</details>


### [129] [Sensitivity of jet and dijet observables to medium effects in pp collisions at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV using X-SCAPE/JETSCAPE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11012)
*Lakshitha Fernando,Claude A. Pruneau,Joern Putschke,Abhijit Majumder,Victor Gonzalez,Oveis Sheibani,Akash Raj,Dana Abdallah*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文使用JETSCAPE/X-SCAPE框架研究了13 TeV质子-质子碰撞中喷注淬火效应的可观测信号，比较了理想化‘砖块介质’与真实事件选择（最高20%活动性事例）下的多种喷注/二喷注观测量，发现二喷注不变质量谱对介质效应最敏感，而其他观测量（如pT不对称、软滴修剪半径/质量）仅呈现局域差异，整体信号微弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在小系统（如高多重数pp碰撞）中确立喷注淬火现象极为困难，因预期修正微小，易被事件选择效应、多部分子相互作用及多喷注产额掩盖。

Method: 采用JETSCAPE/X-SCAPE框架：(1) 在简化的Bjorken膨胀‘砖块介质’中扫描不同长度与输运系数强度，对比真空参考；(2) 对具有涨落初态和逐事件流体演化的真空与介质样本，选取最高20%事例活性事件进行比较；重建R=0.2与R=0.4的anti-k_T喷注，分析二喷注不变质量、领头/次领头喷注pT、折叠方位角分离、二喷注不对称度、Soft-Drop修剪半径与修剪质量。

Result: 在砖块模型中，二喷注不变质量谱展现出最清晰的有序介质响应（随砖块长度与输运系数增强而增大，且R=0.2更显著）；pT与不对称度变化较小，无显著方位角展宽；在真实事件选择中，未修剪观测量的介质/真空比值近于1，但存在局域运动学差异及平均二喷注不对称度轻微上升；修剪半径与质量比值亦仅呈局域差异，未见持续角度窄化或质量再分布。

Conclusion: 所研究观测量对pp中介质诱导喷注修饰具有有限但可量化的灵敏度，其中二喷注不变质量谱为最稳健探针，其余观测量需更高统计精度或改进理论建模以提取明确信号。

Abstract: Establishing jet quenching in small collision systems, such as high-multiplicity proton-proton collisions at the LHC, is challenging because the expected modifications are small and can be obscured by event-selection effects, multiparton interactions, and multijet production. We examine the response of several jet and dijet observables to medium-induced changes in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV using the JETSCAPE/X-SCAPE framework. First, a simplified Bjorken-expanding brick medium is compared with a vacuum reference for several brick lengths and strengths of transport coefficients. Second, separately generated realistic vacuum and medium samples with fluctuating initial conditions and event-by-event hydrodynamic evolution are compared after selecting the highest-activity 20% of events. Full anti-$k_T$ jets are reconstructed with $R=0.2$ and $R=0.4$. The observables are dijet invariant mass, leading- and subleading-jet transverse momentum, folded azimuthal separation, dijet asymmetry, Soft-Drop groomed jet radius, and groomed mass. In the brick calculation, the dijet-mass spectrum shows the clearest ordered response: departures from the vacuum reference generally increase with brick length and strength of transport coefficient and are more visible for the narrower jet radius, while the jet-$p_T$ and dijet-asymmetry changes are smaller and no clear azimuthal broadening is resolved. In the selected realistic events, the ungroomed medium-to-vacuum ratios remain close to unity but contain localized kinematic differences, together with a modest increase of the mean dijet asymmetry. The groomed-radius and groomed-mass ratios show localized differences but do not exhibit a persistent angular narrowing or mass redistribution. These findings quantify the sensitivity of the studied observables to medium-induced jet modifications in the present pp calculations.

</details>


### [130] [Quark- and hadronic-level CPT violation phenomenology of neutral-mesons at at $d=5$ of the SME](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11047)
*Agnes Roberts*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了中性介子振荡中洛伦兹对称性破缺的强子层次标量有效场论描述与夸克层次d=5非最小标准模型扩展（SME）方法之间的对应关系，首次在CPT破缺一阶下建立了a^{(5)}项与介子振荡中现象学CPTV参数的联系，并给出适用于K介子实验搜寻的一般结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 基于C、P、T对称性及QCD手征对称性自发破缺在两种形式体系中均应成立的物理前提，在CPT破坏一阶微扰下，通过算符匹配与对称性约束推导夸克层次d=5 SME系数a^{(5)}与强子层次标量EFT参数之间的映射关系。

Result: 确立了夸克层次a^{(5)}项与中性介子振荡中现象学CPTV参数（如Δa_μ）之间的一阶对应关系；给出了适用于K⁰–K̄⁰系统CPTV搜索的通用参数化表达式。

Conclusion: 强子层次标量EFT与夸克层次d=5 SME在CPT破坏一阶下具有可比性与一致性；该对应为利用K介子实验限制高维Lorentz/CPT破坏算符提供了桥梁。

Abstract: This article summarizes an investigation of the connection between the hadron-level scalar effective field theory treatment of Lorentz symmetry violation in neutral-meson oscillations and the corresponding quark level $d=5$ nonminimal SME approach. The correspondence between the quark-level $a^{(5)}$ term is identified to first order in CPT violation (CPTV) based on the expectation that C, P, T symmetries and the spontaneously broken chiral symmetry of QCD should hold for both formalism. These can be related to the phenomenological parameter describing possible CPTV in the meson oscillations. A general result facilitating searches for kaons is given.

</details>


### [131] [Study of the weak radiative decay $B_c^+\to B_u^{\ast+}γ$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11095)
*Jia Zhao,Tianhong Wang,Xi-Yu Ni,Zhi-Hui Wang,Guo-Li Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在标准模型框架下，利用瞬时Bethe-Salpeter方法研究稀有辐射衰变 $B_c \to B_u^* \gamma$，系统计入短距penguin、弱湮灭与长距矢量介子主导（VMD）贡献；发现LD贡献对$\rho$介子宽度敏感，SD贡献与LD相当但远大于湮灭项，表明该过程是探查粲夸克味改变中性流（FCNC）的重要渠道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究粲夸克层次的味改变中性流（FCNC）过程，检验标准模型预言，并评估不同机制（SD、LD、湮灭）对该稀有辐射衰变的相对贡献。

Method: 采用瞬时Bethe-Salpeter（BS）方法计算强子矩阵元；完整包含短距$c \\to u\\gamma$企鹅图、弱湮灭图及长距矢量介子主导（VMD）机制；对VMD中$\\rho$介子宽度分别取零与实验值以评估其影响。

Result: $\\mathrm{Br}_{\\rm LD}$分别为$5.0\\times10^{-9}$（$\\Gamma_\\rho=0$）和$7.3\\times10^{-8}$（$\\Gamma_\\rho=\\Gamma_\\rho^{\\rm exp}$）；SD贡献量级与LD相当，而湮灭贡献小一个数量级。

Conclusion: $B_c \\to B_u^*\\gamma$ 是研究粲夸克FCNC过程的灵敏探针，其分支比对长距动力学（尤其是$\\rho$宽度）高度敏感，且短距与长距机制均不可忽略。

Abstract: We investigate the rare radiative decay $B_c \to B_u^\ast\gamma$ within the Standard Model by using the instantaneous Bethe-Salpeter (BS) approach. The short distance (SD) $c \to u\gamma$ penguin, weak annihilation, and long distance (LD) vector meson dominance (VMD) contributions are all included. In the calculation of LD contribution, we use two scenarios, namely the decay width of $\rho$ being set to zero or kept as the experimental value, which gives $\mathrm{Br}_{\rm LD}=5.0\times10^{-9}$ and $7.3\times10^{-8}$, respectively, indicating an important role of $\Gamma_\rho$ in the calculation. The SD contribution is comparable in magnitude to the LD contributions, but one order larger than the annihilation contribution. This shows the $B_c \to B_u^\ast\gamma$ channel can provide an important way to study the flavor changing neutral current processes of charm quark.

</details>


### [132] [Holographic dissociation of tetraquark and hybrid meson in the spinning plasma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11202)
*Zhenqiang Ren,Xiang Gao,Zhou-Run Zhu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用全息对偶方法，在自旋 Myers-Perry 黑洞背景（对应旋转夸克胶子等离子体）中研究四夸克态 $Z_c$ 和混杂介子 $\pi_1$ 的解离行为；通过非二次 dilaton 剖面统一拟合其质量谱，计算纵向与横向谱函数，发现温度和转动效应在横向通道中更强，且2S态先于1S态熔解，两类奇特强子表现出显著不同的解离模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立统一的全息框架以比较不同奇特强子（如四夸克态与混杂介子）在高温高密QCD介质中的热致解离行为，避免依赖动力学绑定能计算。

Method: 采用平面极限下的自旋 Myers-Perry 黑洞作为自旋夸克胶子等离子体的全息对偶背景；引入非二次 dilaton 剖面并由 $Z_c$ 与 $\pi_1$ 的质量谱固定参数；在该背景中计算纵向与横向谱函数以提取解离特征。

Result: 温度与转动（boost参数）均导致谱函数峰高降低、宽度展宽，且横向通道效应更强；2S激发态比对应1S基态更早解离；$Z_c$ 与 $\pi_1$ 在同一全息框架下呈现明显不同的解离阈值与动力学行为。

Conclusion: 全息谱函数分析揭示了不同类型奇特强子在旋转QGP中的非普适解离模式，表明其内部结构（如夸克组分与胶子激发）在介质响应中起决定性作用；该方法为实验上区分奇特态提供了理论判据。

Abstract: We explore the dissociation of the tetraquark \(Z_c\) and the hybrid meson \(\pi_1\) in a spinning quark-gluon plasma described by the planar limit of a spinning Myers-Perry black hole. Both states are treated within one holographic framework, using a non-quadratic dilaton profile whose parameters are fixed by the \(Z_c\) and \(\pi_1\) mass spectra. Spectral functions are computed in the longitudinal and transverse channels. The boost parameter and temperature both lower the peak height and broaden the width, and the effect is stronger in the transverse channel. In both channels, the heavier 2S states melt before the 1S states. Furthermore, the two exotic channels display distinct dissociation behaviors within the same effective framework. This provides a unified holographic approach to compare how different exotic states behavior, without requiring a dynamical calculation of their binding energies.

</details>


### [133] [An $SU(5)$ GUT with non-holomorphic modular $S'_4$ symmetry: fermion masses, mixing and leptogenesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11241)
*Xianshuo Zhang,Yakefu Reyimuaji*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个基于非全纯模群 $S'_4$ 和整体 Peccei–Quinn $U(1)_{PQ}$ 对称性的非超对称 $SU(5)$ 大统一模型，利用多调和 Maass 形式构造 Yukawa 耦合，成功拟合全部费米子质量与混合参数（含 NO/IO 中微子振荡），并预言绝对中微子质量、CP 相位及无中微子双贝塔衰变有效质量；通过密度矩阵方法研究热轻子生成，发现重子不对称性仅由右手中微子质量标度参数 $b$ 决定，并可精确复现观测值 $\eta_B \simeq 6.12 \times 10^{-10}$，从而建立味物理与早期宇宙学的定量联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决标准模型中费米子质量与混合角起源问题，统一解释夸克与轻子味结构，并在非超对称框架下实现大统一与轻子生成机制的自洽衔接。

Method: 采用非全纯 $S'_4$ 模对称性与 $U(1)_{PQ}$ 约束构造 Yukawa 耦合，其具体形式由 polyharmonic Maass forms 给出；通过全局拟合实验数据（包括 CKM、PMNS 矩阵及质量平方差）确定模型参数；利用密度矩阵方程求解热轻子生成过程，严格计入味效应与干涉项。

Result: 模型在 NO 和 IO 下均以 $|\mathcal{P}| < 0.8$ 高精度复现所有已知费米子观测量；预言 $m_{\beta\beta} \in [17, 35]\,\text{meV}$（NO）、$[48, 53]\,\text{meV}$（IO）；给出 $\delta_{CP} \approx 215^\circ$（NO）或 $250^\circ$（IO）；热轻子生成中 $b \sim 10^{12}\,\text{GeV}$ 即可再生观测重子数不对称性。

Conclusion: 非全纯模对称性结合 PQ 机制可为 $SU(5)$ GUT 提供完整的味结构起源；该模型不仅具有强预测能力，且首次在非 SUSY 框架下实现从 TeV 尺度味物理到 $10^{12}\,\text{GeV}$ 轻子生成的定量关联，构成味物理与宇宙学间的关键桥梁。

Abstract: A non-supersymmetric $SU(5)$ grand unified model is constructed based on the non-holomorphic modular $S'_4$ flavor symmetry, supplemented by a global Peccei--Quinn $U(1)_{PQ}$ symmetry that constrains the Yukawa couplings. Constructed from polyharmonic Maaß forms, these couplings allow the model to reproduce the observed charged-fermion masses and mixing as well as the neutrino oscillation parameters for both normal ordering (NO) and inverted ordering (IO), with all Pull values satisfying $|\mathcal{P}| < 0.8$. The model further yields specific predictions for yet-unmeasured parameters, including the absolute neutrino masses, the Dirac CP phase, the Majorana phases, and the effective Majorana mass $m_{\beta\beta}$. We then study thermal leptogenesis by solving the density matrix equations. For a given benchmark point, the final baryon asymmetry depends solely on the single free parameter $b$ that sets the right-handed neutrino mass scale, and an appropriate choice of this parameter reproduces the observed value $\eta_B \simeq 6.12 \times 10^{-10}$. Finally, the correlation between this scale parameter and specific low-energy observables provides a potential bridge between the fermion flavor structure and early-Universe cosmology.

</details>


### [134] [Direct Light-Cone Reconstruction of the $B$-Meson Shape Function in HQET](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11246)
*Ao-Sheng Xiong,Shu-Man Hu,Ji Xu,Fu-Sheng Yu,Shuai Zhao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种直接在重夸克有效理论（HQET）框架下定义的B介子准形状函数，并计算了其到光锥形状函数的一圈匹配，避免了通过全QCD中间步骤；给出了MS-bar与RI/MOM方案下的单圈重整化核与匹配系数，为格点HQET直接提取B介子形状函数提供了必要的微扰输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HQET在重夸克质量可比或大于格点截止（1/a）时为格点计算提供了自然框架；为规避传统方法中需经全QCD光锥分布这一中间匹配步骤，作者旨在构建一个可在HQET内直接定义并匹配的准形状函数。

Method: 在HQET中构造B介子准形状函数；采用维数正规化，在MS-bar与RI/MOM方案下计算其单圈紫外发散结构；对准形状函数与光锥形状函数进行单圈匹配，保留大v^z展开的领头阶项；匹配系数严格独立于红外正规化方案。

Result: 得到了准形状函数在MS-bar和RI/MOM方案下的单圈重整化核；导出了到MS-bar方案下光锥形状函数的单圈匹配系数；该系数在大动量（v^z）极限下成立且红外无关。

Conclusion: 所获匹配关系与重整化核构成格点HQET提取B介子非微扰形状函数所需的完整微扰基础，显著简化了从格点数据到物理光锥分布的映射路径。

Abstract: The $B$-meson shape function encodes the nonperturbative residual-momentum dynamics of the heavy quark within heavy quark effective theory (HQET). From a Wilsonian perspective, HQET provides a natural framework for lattice calculations when the heavy quark mass is comparable to or exceeds the inverse lattice spacing. Motivated by this observation, we introduce a $B$-meson quasi-shape function directly in HQET and calculate its one-loop matching to the light-cone shape function, avoiding an intermediate matching step involving a full-QCD light-cone distribution. We determine the one-loop renormalization kernels and matching coefficients in both the $\overline{\mathrm{MS}}$ and regularization-independent momentum-subtraction (RI/MOM) schemes for the quasi-shape function, while keeping the light-cone shape function in the $\overline{\mathrm{MS}}$ scheme. The matching coefficients are obtained at leading power in the large-$v^z$ expansion and are independent of the infrared regulator. Our results provide the perturbative ingredients for a direct lattice HQET determination of the $B$-meson shape function.

</details>


### [135] [Probing the Supersymmetric Grand Unified Theories with Anomaly Mediation at the Future Proton-Proton Colliders and Hyper-Kamiokande Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11254)
*Waqas Ahmed,Tianjun Li,Shabbar Raza*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在大沙漠假设下，研究了基于最小反常介导超对称破缺（AMSB）机制的超对称大统一理论（SUSY GUTs），重点探讨其在100 TeV质子对撞机（如FCC-hh、SppC）和Hyper-Kamiokande质子衰变实验中的可探测性。结果表明：当GUT标度MGUT ≥ 0.84×10¹⁶ GeV时，胶微子与前两代squark质量上限分别为8 TeV和6 TeV，可在上述对撞机中探测；当MGUT ≤ 0.84×10¹⁶ GeV时，六维算符主导的质子衰变寿命≤ ~5×10³⁴年，可被Hyper-K探测。文章还给出了两类典型基准点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 延续前期关于SUSY GUTs在100 TeV对撞机及质子衰变实验中可检验性的研究计划，结合大沙漠假设与最小AMSB机制，系统评估其唯象可行性与实验探针。

Method: 采用大沙漠假设约束能标演化，结合最小AMSB机制构造SUSY GUT模型；通过RGE演化计算粒子谱，分析胶微子、squarks、sleptons、伪标量Higgs及电弱微子的质量关系；利用质子衰变有效算符（维度六，X/Y玻色子交换）估算τp，并与Hyper-K灵敏度比较；给出满足不同MGUT条件的基准点用于模拟与搜索策略设计。

Result: 1) MGUT ≥ 0.84×10¹⁶ GeV ⇒ m_g̃ ≤ 8 TeV, m_q̃₁,₂ ≤ 6 TeV，可在FCC-hh/SppC探测；2) MGUT ≤ 0.84×10¹⁶ GeV ⇒ τp^(p→e⁺π⁰) ≲ 5×10³⁴ yr，可被Hyper-K覆盖；3) 给出对应两类MGUT区间的具体benchmark points；4) 提出胶微子与wino在100 TeV对撞机中的多轻子+ETmiss等关键搜寻道。

Conclusion: 在大沙漠假设与最小AMSB框架下，SUSY GUTs具有清晰且互补的实验信号：高MGUT情形主导对撞机新物理信号，低MGUT情形主导质子衰变信号；100 TeV对撞机与Hyper-K构成对该理论的关键联合检验平台。

Abstract: With the grand desert hypothesis, we investigate the Supersymmetric (SUSY) Grand Unified Theories (GUTs) with the minimal anomaly mediated supersymmetry breaking (AMSB) as a continuation of our program of studying SUSY GUTs at the future 100 TeV proton-proton Collider such as $\rm FCC_{hh}$ and SppC as well as proton-decay experiment such as Hyper-Kamiokande experiment. We show that for the GUT scale $M_{GUT} \ge 0.84 \times 10^{16}$ GeV, the upper mass bounds on the gluino and first two generation squarks are 8 TeV and 6 TeV, respectively. Therefore, the gluino and first two generation squarks can be probed at the $\rm FCC_{hh}$ and SppC. In addition, we discuss the mass spectra for the colored sparticles, sleptons, pseudo-scalar Higgs and electroweakinos. We also shed light on the possible search strategies of gluino and winos at the future 100 TeV colliders. Moreover, for $\rm M_{\rm GUT} \le 0.84 \times 10^{16}$ GeV, the proton lifetime for dimension-six proton decay via heavy gauge boson exchange is less than or around $5 \times 10^{34}$ years, and thus we can probe it at the Hyper-Kamiokande experiment. To be concrete, we present a couple of benchmark points representing the above two mentioned GUT conditions on $\rm M_{\rm GUT}$.

</details>


### [136] [Rapid, High-Precision Numerical Evaluation of Multiloop Feynman Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11292)
*Richard Easther*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出使用Sinc函数逼近法将多圈费曼图积分转化为快速收敛的多维求和，并在电子反常磁矩(g-2)计算中与蒙特卡洛方法对比，显示其在高精度（>1%）下显著更优，尤其在3圈六位精度达分钟级，而蒙特卡洛需年量级；并初步验证其对5圈单个积分的有效性及可推广性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升多圈费曼图数值计算的精度与效率，特别是针对电子g-2这类高精度需求的关键可观测量，克服传统蒙特卡洛方法收敛慢、统计误差大的瓶颈。

Method: 采用Sinc函数插值与数值积分变换技术，将原始参数空间中的多维发散/振荡积分映射为指数收敛的离散求和；结合自适应网格与解析延拓处理红外/紫外奇点。

Result: Sinc方法在3-4圈g-2完整图集上与蒙特卡洛达到1%精度相当；3圈积分达六位有效数字仅需数分钟（vs. 蒙特卡洛预估数年）；首次实现单个5圈g-2积分的数值评估；验证了其对一般拓扑图的适用潜力。

Conclusion: Sinc函数数值方法是一种高精度、高效率的多圈费曼积分计算新范式，有望成为未来高阶微扰计算（尤其是g-2、Higgs物理等关键精密检验）的核心工具，并具备向更广义费曼图推广的数学基础。

Abstract: We numerically evaluate multiloop Feynman diagrams by using Sinc function approximations to transform the integrals into rapidly convergent, multidimensional sums. We compare the efficiency of this approach to Monte Carlo evaluations of electron g-2 terms. We show that the two strategies reach parity at precisions of around 1% for the full sets of three and four loop diagrams. Beyond this level Sinc function methods are dramatically faster - computing 3-loop integrals to six digit precision takes minutes whereas Monte Carlo timings map to years. We show that the method can evaluate individual 5-loop g-2 integrals and discuss extrapolation techniques that would further improve performance. We note that the method appears to be applicable to more general diagrams.

</details>


### [137] [After the Higgs: Looking for the Universe Behind the Particle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11458)
*Veronica Sanz*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了希格斯玻色子从发现目标转变为探索自然真空、检验量子场论及寻找新物理原理的关键工具的范式转变，并提出在缺乏理论保证新物理可及性的背景下，应通过高精度测量、多样化实验布局、保护理论冒险精神以及独立阐述基础科学价值来规划未来探索路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在希格斯玻色子发现十四年后，理论不再保证实验可及范围内存在新物理，同时基础科学研究长期资助日益困难，亟需重新思考其科学定位与发展战略。

Method: 采用概念性分析与科学哲学反思相结合的方法，梳理希格斯场的量子特性（如标量性、真空期望值非零、自相互作用强度唯一性），并据此论证其作为连接粒子物理、宇宙学与其他物理扇区的独特探针地位；进而提出应对‘无定理保障发现’困境的四条实践原则。

Result: 确立希格斯玻色子作为‘真空探测器’与‘跨尺度桥梁’的核心角色；提出一套面向不确定未来的高能物理发展战略框架，涵盖精度优先、实验多样性、理论容错性与公众沟通自主性。

Conclusion: 希格斯玻色子的特殊量子结构使其超越标准模型粒子范畴，成为通向早期宇宙、暗能量与可能的超对称/复合结构等深层物理的不可替代门户；相应地，学科发展必须转向以开放性精度探索为驱动的稳健范式。

Abstract: Fourteen years after its discovery, the Higgs boson has changed roles. It was once the object of the search, but today it is an instrument with which we interrogate Nature's vacuum, test quantum field theory and look for new principles. This shift comes at a time when, unlike before the Higgs discovery, we have no theoretical argument guaranteeing new physics within experimental reach. At the same time, securing long-term support for fundamental science is becoming increasingly challenging. I revisit the path from a theoretically motivated discovery to a precision programme, explain why the Higgs is such an unusual quantum field, and argue that this same peculiarity makes it a gateway to cosmology and other sectors. I then turn to a less technical question: how should a field plan for exploration when no theorem guarantees the next discovery? My answer is to pursue precision without pretending to know in advance which measurement will matter, sustain a diverse experimental portfolio, protect theoretical risk-taking, and make the public case for fundamental exploration on its own terms.

</details>


### [138] [Short-range dynamics of $X(3872)$ and $T_{cc}(3875)^+$ in a coupled-channel framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11472)
*Fang-Zheng Peng,Li-Sheng Geng,Ju-Jun Xie*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究X(3872)与T_{cc}(3875)^+的短程动力学，采用耦合通道Lippmann-Schwinger方程，包含D^*自能与D D π三体效应；通过T_{cc}极点约束接触相互作用，分析X(3872)极点对矢量介子交换局域磁偶极项的依赖；发现π交换显著影响X(3872)的同位旋破缺；Feshbach投影揭示T_{cc}不能纯由分子态解释，需引入紧凑cc\bar{u}\bar{d}成分；BO-EFT计算显示X(3872)与T_{cc}中短距组分（四夸克/粲偶素）占比分别为3–9.7%与1.3–14.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调和X(3872)与T_{cc}(3875)^+在纯分子图像下的参数张力，厘清短程动力学（矢量介子交换、π交换、紧凑态混杂）对它们束缚机制的贡献。

Method: 耦合通道S波DD^*/D\bar{D}^*散射理论；显式包含π交换（减去其局域项）；D^*自能嵌入Lippmann-Schwinger方程以处理D D π三体连续谱；Feshbach投影分离分子与紧凑自由度；Born-Oppenheimer有效场论提取空间结构权重。

Result: 1) 保留完整矢量介子交换局域磁偶极项时，X(3872)预测极点仍比实验低5–12 MeV；2) π交换对X(3872)束缚能影响中等，但显著增强其同位旋破缺；3) Feshbach分析表明T_{cc}需~O(10%)紧凑cc\bar{u}\bar{d}成分；4) BO-EFT给出X(3872)与T_{cc}的短距组分权重分别为3.0–9.7%与1.3–14.4%。

Conclusion: X(3872)可近似为浅束缚分子态，但需保留矢量介子交换局域项；T_{cc}(3875)^+本质上是DD^*分子与紧凑四夸克态的量子混合态，必须联合处理分子与紧凑自由度。

Abstract: We study the short-range dynamics in the coupled-channel description of the $S$-wave $D \bar{D}^*$ and $DD^*$ systems for $X(3872)$ and $T_{cc}(3875)^+$, respectively. Contact interactions are related through scalar- and vector-meson exchange saturation, while pion exchange is included explicitly with its local term subtracted. The coupled Lippmann-Schwinger equations incorporate the self-energy of the unstable $D^*$ mesons, thereby accounting for the three-body $D \bar{D} \pi$ ($D D\pi$) dynamics. Fixing the contact interaction from the $T_{cc}(3875)^+$ pole, we trace the predicted $X(3872)$ pole as the local magnetic dipole term from vector-meson exchange is varied. Retaining this term in full produces the shallowest poles, which remain approximately $5$--$12~\mathrm{MeV}$ below the observed $X(3872)$ mass. It is found that the pion exchange has a moderate effect on the binding energy and a stronger effect on the isospin violation for the $X(3872)$ state. The residual tension between the pure-molecular bound descriptions of $X(3872)$ and $T_{cc}(3875)^+$ is analyzed with the Feshbach projection. This analysis indicates a $DD^*$ interaction weaker than that implied by the purely molecular fits, and shows that the $T_{cc}(3875)^+$ state instead arises from the mixing of the $DD^{*}$ channel with a compact $cc\bar{u}\bar{d}$ state. We further examine the spatial structure using Born--Oppenheimer effective field theory. For separation radii of $0.5$--$1.5~\mathrm{fm}$, the combined charmonium and short-distance tetraquark weight in $X(3872)$ is $3.0$--$9.7\%$, while the short-distance weight in $T_{cc}(3875)^+$ is $1.3$--$14.4\%$. These results favor retaining the local vector-exchange contribution within the saturation framework and motivate a joint treatment of molecular and compact dynamics for the $T_{cc}(3875)^+$.

</details>


### [139] [Next-to-leading-order QCD corrections to radiative decays of fully heavy tetraquarks into quarkonia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11511)
*Yu-Dong Zhang,Zai-Fu Jiang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在非相对论QCD因子化框架下，计算了四夸克态 $T_{4Q}=QQ\bar Q\bar Q$（$Q=c,b$）通过辐射光子衰变到S波偶素（$V_Q$ 或 $\eta_Q$）的NLO QCD修正，给出了解析形式的短距系数、干涉效应、紫外抵消验证，并量化了NLO对宽度的增强（~1.8–2.9倍）及标度依赖性改善；最后预估LHC上可观测事例产额。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统研究紧致四夸克态 $T_{4Q}$ 的电磁衰变是检验其内部结构（如双色单态/八重态叠加）、QCD因子化适用性及高阶动力学的重要探针；尤其 $T_{4Q}^{J^{PC}} \to \text{quarkonium} + \gamma$ 过程具有清晰的C宇称选择定则和可计算的短距主导特性。

Method: 采用非相对论QCD（NRQCD）因子化，在价夸克模型下构造 $T_{4Q}$ 的Fock态投影；在 $v \ll 1$ 展开下，使用维度正规化+MS-bar重整化计算NLO实虚图，精确处理双色构型（如 $0^{++}$ 态中 $\mathbf{1}\otimes\mathbf{1}$ 与 $\mathbf{8}\otimes\mathbf{8}$ 干涉），并完成紫外发散抵消。

Result: 获得 $\mathcal{O}(\alpha\alpha_s^5)$ 精度下所有 $J^{PC}$ 分量（$0^{++},1^{+-},2^{++}$）衰变的解析短距系数；NLO修正使 $2^{++}\to V_Q+\gamma$ 宽度增强约2.94（c）/2.33（b），$1^{+-}\to\eta_Q+\gamma$ 增强约2.22（c）/1.83（b）；$\mu_R$ 依赖显著减弱（除个别通道）；预言LHC 13 TeV下 $T_{4c}\to J/\psi+\gamma$ 产额达 $10^6$–$10^7$ 量级。

Conclusion: NLO QCD修正对 $T_{4Q}$ 光子辐射衰变具有定量化重要影响，理论精度提升支持在LHC上通过 $\ell^+\ell^-\gamma$ 不变量谱开展 $T_{4c}$ 寻找，为四夸克态谱学与强子结构提供新约束。

Abstract: Within nonrelativistic QCD factorization, we calculate the next-to-leading-order (NLO) QCD corrections to the decays of compact $T_{4Q}=QQ\bar Q\bar Q$ states ($Q=c,b$) into an $S$-wave quarkonium and a photon. Conservation of charge conjugation allows $T_{4Q}^{0^{++}}$ and $T_{4Q}^{2^{++}}$ to decay into $V_Q+\gamma$, with $V_c=J/\psi$ and $V_b=\Upsilon$, while $T_{4Q}^{1^{+-}}$ can decay into $\eta_Q+\gamma$. At leading order in the heavy quark velocity expansion, we derive the short-distance coefficients for these decay widths in analytic form through $\mathcal{O}(\alpha\alpha_s^5)$, including the interference contribution between the two color configurations of $T_{4Q}^{0^{++}}$. All ultraviolet divergences at NLO are exactly canceled by renormalization. The NLO QCD corrections enhance the decay widths for $T_{4Q}^{2^{++}}\to V_Q+\gamma$ and $T_{4Q}^{1^{+-}}\to\eta_Q+\gamma$ by factors of approximately $2.94$ ($2.33$) and $2.22$ ($1.83$), respectively. The dependence on the renormalization scale $\mu_R$ is reduced at NLO in most channels, although it remains sizable. Based on the NLO branching fractions, we estimate yields of order $10^6$--$10^7$ for $T_{4c}\to J/\psi+\gamma$ and $10^4$ for $T_{4c}\to\eta_c+\gamma$ in $pp$ collisions at $\sqrt{s}=13~\mathrm{TeV}$ with an integrated luminosity of $100~\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$. The $J/\psi+\gamma$ channels can be investigated through $J/\psi\to\ell^+\ell^-$ in the $\ell^+\ell^-\gamma$ invariant-mass spectrum, complementing di-charmonium searches.

</details>


### [140] [Hadronic final-state effects on open-charm production at LHC energies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11569)
*Taesoo Song,Elena Bratkovskaya,Krzysztof Redlich*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文使用PHSD输运模型研究了强子末态相互作用对LHC能区pp和Pb+Pb碰撞中开粲观测量的影响，发现D介子积分产额对强子散射不敏感，但横动量谱和椭圆流v2显著受其影响，表明在利用微分开粲观测量提取QGP性质时必须考虑强子相演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从微分开粲观测量（如pT谱、v2、比值）中提取夸克胶子等离子体（QGP）性质需厘清强子相演化效应，尤其是charm-hadron rescattering对末态可观测量的贡献。

Method: 采用微观Parton-Hadron-String Dynamics (PHSD)输运模型，对比包含/排除charm-hadron hadronic rescattering的模拟结果，并与ALICE实验数据及统计强子化模型（SHM）预测进行比较。

Result: 1) 积分D介子产额比（如Ds+/D0）基本不受强子相互作用影响；2) 非奇异D介子比值系统依赖性弱，Ds+/D0在Pb+Pb中增强，符合SHM所描述的奇异性趋近平衡趋势；3) 强子再散射显著重整化低-中pT区D介子谱并增强v2。

Conclusion: 强子相演化对微分开粲观测量（特别是pT谱与v2）具有不可忽略的影响，因此在QGP参数提取中必须显式纳入该阶段动力学。

Abstract: We investigate the influence of hadronic final-state interactions on open-charm observables in pp and Pb+Pb collisions at LHC energies within the microscopic Parton-Hadron-String Dynamics (PHSD) transport approach. Calculations with and without charm-hadron rescattering are compared with ALICE measurements and predictions of the Statistical Hadronization Model for charm. The relative integrated $D$-meson multiplicities are essentially insensitive to hadronic interactions. PHSD reproduces the weak system dependence of the nonstrange $D$-meson ratios and the increase of $D_s^+/D^0$ from pp to Pb+Pb collisions, consistent with the statistical-model trend from strangeness undersaturation towards equilibrium. In contrast, hadronic rescattering redistributes the transverse momentum spectra at low and intermediate momenta and enhances the elliptic flow. These results demonstrate the importance of accounting for the hadronic evolution when extracting QGP properties from differential open-charm observables.

</details>


### [141] [Threshold resummation for top-quark pair production in association with a jet at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11600)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提高top-pair+jets过程在高不变质量区域（~1–4 TeV）的理论精度，克服固定阶计算在阈值区的对数发散问题，评估SCET阈值重求和在此多尺度强子过程中的适用性与系统误差。

Method: 基于软-共线有效理论（SCET）进行不变质量M的阈值重求和，达到NNLL精度；jet半径R的对数保留固定阶处理；匹配至精确NLO实虚贡献；修正并重新计算一阶软反常维数矩阵Γ^soft^(1)；通过Drell-Yan过程校验领头幂（LP）核与精确实辐射的符合程度；系统分析初态依赖项（∝ τ = M²/S）及质量比M/m_t对核精度的影响。

Result: LP核在M ~ 1–4 TeV且jet能量阈值设为0.15M时，在接近阈值处与精确实辐射吻合，但当辐射能量>100–200 GeV时显著偏离；该偏差部分源于缺失的初态贡献（显含τ依赖）及M/m_t增长效应；由此导致的重求和修正Δσ/σ_NLO在M尺度下为1–23%，随jet阈值（10%/15%/20%×M）单调变化；该修正缺乏微扰可控性，不构成可靠预测。

Conclusion: 当前SCET阈值重求和框架在tt̄+jet高能阈值区存在本质局限：初态动力学与质量效应未被领头幂核涵盖，导致重求和修正不可靠且非收敛；因此NLO截面仍应作为中央理论预言，其不确定性（尺度变化、已知NNLO、PS匹配）优于形式上的NNLL⊕NLO组合。

Abstract: We study threshold resummation for the invariant-mass distribution of a top-quark pair and a jet at the LHC. We perform the resummation at next-to-next-to-leading logarithmic accuracy in soft-collinear effective theory, with the logarithms of the jet radius treated at fixed order, and match it to the exact next-to-leading-order (NLO) cross section. We correct the one-loop soft anomalous-dimension matrices in the literature and validate the hard functions. We compare the leading-power kernel with exact real emission, and validate this comparison on Drell-Yan production. At invariant masses up to about 1 TeV with a fixed jet threshold the method does not apply. At 1-4 TeV with a jet threshold of 15% of the invariant mass, the kernel describes the exact real-emission density close to threshold but fails once the emitted energy exceeds about 100-200 GeV. We trace part of this failure to initial states absent from the kernel, which depend on $\tau=M^2/S$, with $M$ the invariant mass and $\sqrt{S}$ the hadronic centre-of-mass energy. In addition, at fixed $\tau$ the difference between the kernel and the exact result grows with the ratio $M/m_t$ of the invariant mass to the top-quark mass. As a consequence, the resummed correction beyond NLO, for a scale equal to the invariant mass, ranges from 1-2% to 17-23% for different treatments of this region. The upper end becomes 13-16% for a jet threshold of 20% of the invariant mass and 23-33% for 10%. The range is not a bound, and the correction is not under perturbative control. We therefore keep NLO as the central prediction. We compare the possible effect of resummation with the NLO scale uncertainty, with the known next-to-next-to-leading-order corrections, and with NLO matched to a parton shower.

</details>


### [142] [A Poisson-gas approach to medium-induced gluon radiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11606)
*Manoel Rodríguez Calvo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Laplace超叠加高斯散射中心的解析-数值混合方法，用于精确计算BDMPS-Z框架下Yukawa势中介质诱导胶子辐射谱，适用于静态与膨胀介质，并支持横向动量微分与带截断的积分形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为满足喷注淬火唯象学精度时代对介质诱导胶子辐射的定量控制需求。

Method: 将Yukawa偶极截面表示为高斯函数的Laplace超叠加，进而将全重求和辐射谱表达为Poisson分布的高斯散射中心构型平均；每个构型可解析求解，最终导出适用于任意介质剖面（含膨胀介质）的主公式，并通过Monte Carlo采样高效并行计算。

Result: 复现了一阶opacity结果及无运动学截断的已有文献结果；引入k⊥截断后发现相对于先前工作的显著相对偏差；该构造可自然推广至有限能量分数辐射、色天线辐射等更复杂级联基本单元。

Conclusion: 该方法为喷注淬火中辐射谱的高精度计算提供了统一、高效且可扩展的框架，兼具解析结构与数值可行性，并为后续解析近似提供自然起点。

Abstract: The precision era of jet-quenching phenomenology requires a quantitative control of medium-induced gluon radiation. We consider the BDMPS-Z spectrum in the soft-gluon limit for a Yukawa (Gyulassy-Wang) potential. Writing the Yukawa dipole cross section as a Laplace superposition of Gaussians, we express the fully resummed spectrum as an average over Poisson-distributed configurations of Gaussian scattering centers, each of which is solved in closed form. This yields a master formula for the spectrum both differential in the transverse momentum $k_\perp$ and integrated with a cut on $k_\perp$, valid for static and expanding media with arbitrary profiles. Its evaluation by Monte Carlo sampling of the configurations is efficient and trivially parallelizable. The method reproduces the first order in opacity and published results without kinematic cuts, while with a cut we find sizeable relative deviations from previous results. We argue that the same construction extends to the more complex building blocks of an in-medium parton cascade, such as finite energy fraction and emissions off color antennas, and provides a natural starting point for analytical approximations. The code is publicly available.

</details>


### [143] [The $ϕ$-$η$ Puzzle and the Five-Layer Effective-Temperature Hierarchy in $pp$ Collisions at 2.76 and 7 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11612)
*M. Waqas,Wolfgang Bietenholz,Zhen-Yan Lu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过修正的Tsallis-Pareto函数分析pp碰撞中不同识别粒子的横动量谱，发现有效温度呈现稳健的五层结构，依次为：非奇异强子、单奇异强子、多重奇异强子、轻核、φ(1020)介子（7 TeV下η介子与φ同层），表明末态动力学由奇异性、截面及OZI规则主导，而非单纯质量排序；该层次结构对强子化模型和小系统中类QGP物质形成具有重要约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究高能碰撞末期强相互作用物质的动力学演化，特别是动能冻结过程是否反映更精细的热力学分层结构，超越传统单/双温模型，并检验奇异性、OZI规则与质量在冻结顺序中的相对作用。

Method: 采用修正Tsallis-Pareto分布拟合√s = 2.76 和 7 TeV pp碰撞中多种识别粒子（π, K, p, Λ, Ξ, Ω, d, t, ^3He, φ, η）的横动量谱，分别提取各粒子种类的有效温度T_eff和非广延参数q，通过统计显著性（σ分离）判定温度分层结构。

Result: 观测到稳健的五层有效温度层级：非奇异强子 < 单奇异强子 < 多重奇异强子 < 轻核 < φ(1020)（7 TeV时η与φ共处最高层）；所有相邻层温差 >3σ，顶层分离>7σ；温度整体随能量升高而上移；φ介子虽质量≈质子，但T_eff显著更高，归因于OZI压制导致其晚冻结；η–φ在7 TeV的温度简并构成新谜题。

Conclusion: 动能冻结呈现五层热力学序列，揭示冻结顺序由粒子内部量子数（奇异性）、强相互作用截面及OZI规则共同决定，远超质量依赖；该结构为强子化机制提供关键约束，暗示小系统中可能存在具有非平凡内部结构敏感性的类QGP演化介质。

Abstract: Sequential kinetic freeze-out in high-energy collisions probes the dynamics governing the final stages of strongly interacting matter. Inspired by the recently established quadruple freeze-out scenario, we present a thermodynamic analysis of transverse momentum spectra for a set of identified particles produced in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s} = 2.76$ and $7$ TeV. Using a modified Tsallis-Pareto function, we extract the effective temperature and the non-extensivity parameter for each species. We observe a robust five-layer hierarchy in the effective temperature. At both energies, the layers are: non-strange hadrons, singly-strange hadrons, multi-strange hadrons, light nuclei, and the $\phi(1020)$ meson. At $7$~TeV, the $\eta$ meson joins the $\phi(1020)$ meson in the highest-temperature layer. At both energies, each pair of neighboring layers is separated by more than $ 3\sigma$, and the separation at the top exceeds $7\sigma$. The grouping persists at both energies, while the temperatures shift upward at 7 TeV. The observed pattern shows that the decoupling sequence is driven by an interplay of strangeness, cross-sections, and the OZI rule rather than by particle mass alone. Notably, the $\phi(1020)$ meson has a mass close to the proton, but a significantly higher temperature due to its OZI-suppressed coupling to the hadronic medium. The unexpected coincidence of $\eta$ and $\phi(1020)$ at 7 TeV presents a puzzle that may point to a common early production mechanism or in-medium effects, which are not yet understood. The five-layer hierarchy provides new constraints on theoretical models of hadronization. It suggests that the sequential decoupling of particles from the evolving medium is even more sensitive to their internal structure and interaction than previously assumed, with implications for the QGP-like matter formation in small systems.

</details>


### [144] [Nanohertz gravitational waves and asymmetric dark matter from a supercooled dark sector phase transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11694)
*Wonsub Cho,Ki-Young Choi*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立不对称暗物质模型与可观测纳赫兹引力波信号之间的可检验联系，同时满足暗物质丰度、BBN约束及PTA观测限制。

Method: 构建含实标量、复标量与狄拉克费米子的经典共形暗扇区，引入Affleck-Dine机制生成暗费米子不对称性；采用三维有限温有效势与NLOdet方法精确计算热隧穿率；求解暗/可见扇区耦合Boltzmann方程以追踪热历史；模拟超冷一级相变动力学并计算引力波谱（以碰撞主导机制为主）。

Result: 在匹配观测暗物质丰度的基准点下，相变完成于MeV量级温度，产生峰值频率~1–10 nHz的引力波信号；该信号与当前PTA自由谱后验分布兼容；参数扫描显示引力波振幅与Ωh²呈强相关性，且所有可行参数点均满足BBN约束。

Conclusion: 经典共形暗扇区中由Affleck-Dine机制驱动的超冷一级相变，可自洽地同时解释不对称暗物质起源与纳赫兹引力波信号，构成一个可被PTA与BBN联合检验的多信使物理框架。

Abstract: We study the connection between asymmetric dark matter and nanohertz gravitational waves from a supercooled first-order phase transition in a classically conformal dark sector. The dark sector contains a real scalar, a complex scalar and a Dirac fermion, whose mass arises from radiative symmetry breaking. An additional Affleck--Dine condensate generates a fermion asymmetry and heats the dark bath before nucleation. The conserved dark charge fixes the asymmetry between fermions and antifermions, while entropy production during delayed visible-sector reheating dilutes the relic and determines the redshift of the gravitational-wave spectrum. We calculate the thermal nucleation rate in a three-dimensional effective theory with finite-density corrections, next-to-leading-order contributions and fluctuation determinants (NLOdet). We follow the coupled thermal evolution of the dark and visible sectors. For a benchmark chosen to match the observed dark-matter abundance, the transition completes at MeV-scale temperatures and produces a nanohertz signal under the adopted dynamical and thermal assumptions. We compare the predicted collision-dominated gravitational-wave spectra with free-spectrum posteriors from pulsar timing arrays. For a benchmark point and variations of the dark-sector parameters, the gravitational-wave spectra and dark matter relic abundances are compared, and compatibility with Big Bang nucleosynthesis is assessed.

</details>


### [145] [Origin of Tensor Polarized Structure of the Deuteron](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11749)
*Satvir Kaur,Chandan Mondal,Xingbo Zhao,Chueng-Ryong Ji*

Main category: hep-ph
