<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决氘核张量极化结构函数b₁的微观起源问题，特别是解释HERMES实验观测到的b₁大小与动量依赖性无法被常规核模型（仅含S/D波核子自由度）重现的难题。

Method: 采用QCD启发的光前量子力学框架，显式包含隐藏色组分（colored-cluster configurations）及相对论性自旋-动量纠缠，并将物理D波概率（0.85%）由四极矩实验约束固定，分离各组分对b₁(x)的贡献。

Result: 发现微小但非零的隐藏色混入即可产生显著的张量响应b₁(x)，且b₁对D波成分几乎不敏感：当D波概率取0.85%时，其变化仅导致b₁改变1.7%；主导贡献来自有色簇S波部分。

Conclusion: 氘核的张量极化结构函数b₁本质上由隐藏色动力学主导，而非传统核物理中的D波成分，这揭示了强子内部多夸克胶子自由度在轻核结构中的关键作用。

Abstract: The microscopic origin of the deuteron tensor polarized structure function $b_1$ remains unresolved. Conventional nuclear models attribute a nonzero $b_1$ mainly to the $D$-wave component of the deuteron wave function, but generally fail to reproduce the magnitude and momentum-dependence observed by the HERMES experiment. Using a QCD-based light-front framework with degrees of freedom beyond the conventional nucleonic picture, in particular including hidden-color components and relativistic spin--momentum correlations, we find that a small but nonzero estimated hidden-color admixture can be sufficient to generate a substantial tensor response ($b_1(x)$), while it is essentially insensitive to the $D$-wave component. The physical $D$-wave probability of $0.85\%$, fixed by the empirical quadrupole moment, changes $b_1$ by only $1.7\%$, with the dominant contribution arising from the colored-cluster $S$-wave sector. These results demonstrate that the tensor-polarized structure of the deuteron is driven primarily by hidden-color dynamics rather than by the $D$-wave component.

</details>


### [146] [Probing the inhomogeneous phases in magnetized quark matter via the bosonic two-point function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11795)
*Kang Deng,Bo Feng,Tianle Wei,Shuai Yuan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了强磁场下两味NJL模型中均匀手征凝聚态的稳定性，发现朗道量子化导致的一维费米面嵌套引发严重Peierls不稳定性；数值计算显示强磁场诱导动量空间中出现负‘峡谷’，热涨落仅使其变浅但不消除；在高化学势下观察到非单调‘盆地形’色散，标志moat相区，预示非均匀手征对称性破缺倾向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解致密、强磁场环境下夸克物质的手征相结构，特别是均匀手征凝聚态是否稳定，以及是否存在向非均匀相（如chiral density wave）演化的动力学驱动力。

Method: 在两味Nambu-Jona-Lasinio模型框架下，于恒定外磁场中展开有效作用量至二次型，解析推导全动量依赖的玻色型两点函数（即手征涨落传播子），结合Landau能级量子化与零温/有限温动量空间数值扫描。

Result: 1) 零温下各占据Landau能级在对应费米动量处引发尖锐对数发散（Peierls失稳）；2) 强磁场诱导动量空间两点函数出现深度负值‘canyons’；3) 热涨落抑制但未完全抹除负区域，仅在足够高温下消失；4) 高μ下出现非单调‘moat’型色散——原点处曲率平坦且存在宽正基底，标志亚稳态moat regime。

Conclusion: 均匀手征凝聚在强磁、低温、中等密度下本质不稳定；moat色散是残余非均匀不稳定性的重要前兆，暗示致密磁化夸克物质中可能存在丰富的非均匀手征相结构。

Abstract: We investigate the stability of homogeneous chiral condensates in magnetized quark matter within the two-flavor Nambu-Jona-Lasinio model. By expanding the effective action to quadratic order, we derive the full momentum-dependent bosonic two-point function in a constant magnetic field. At zero temperature, our analytical results show that each occupied Landau level produces a sharp logarithmic divergence at its corresponding Fermi momentum. This severe Peierls instability is driven by one-dimensional Fermi surface nesting within the effectively 1+1-dimensional subsystems created by Landau quantization. Numerically, we map out the momentum-space topography of the two-point function across varying magnetic fields and temperatures. We find that strong magnetic fields induce deep negative canyons. While thermal fluctuations smooth these canyons into shallower valleys as the temperature increases, the negative regions remain robust at intermediate temperatures and only vanish entirely at sufficiently high temperatures. Intriguingly, at larger chemical potentials where the system is strictly stable, a broad positive basin with a flat curvature at the origin persists at non-zero momentum. This non-monotonic dispersion is the hallmark of a moat regime, acting as a precursor state that strongly signals a residual tendency toward inhomogeneous chiral symmetry breaking. Our findings provide valuable insights into the phase structure of dense, magnetized quark matter.

</details>


### [147] [Interplay of Scalar and Pseudo-Scalar Mesonic Channels in $Λ\barΛ$ Spin Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11882)
*Kun Xu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究重离子碰撞中ΛΛ̄超子对的自旋关联，通过标量和赝标量介子通道（主导s̄s对产生）分析其自旋态投影机制；发现赝标量通道主导导致整体负自旋关联，并揭示其对运动学（开角）、动力学（冻结线动量积分）及临界区域轨迹路径的敏感依赖，可作为手征相变位置的灵敏探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测QCD手征相变临界结构，利用ΛΛ̄自旋关联作为新型自旋极化观测量，弥补传统集体流与极化观测量在临界区域响应不足的问题。

Method: 基于三味Nambu-Jona-Lasinio模型，在随机相位近似（RPA）框架下计算标量（0⁺）与赝标量（0⁻）介子通道的谱权重；结合角动量-自旋耦合约束（J=0→L=S）与宇称守恒（(-1)^(L+1)=±1），推导两通道对总自旋态（S=0 vs S=1）的选择性投影及对应关联张量迹（即自旋关联分数P_{s̄s}）。

Result: 赝标量通道谱权重显著大于标量通道，导致净自旋关联分数P_{s̄s}恒为负（-1）；自旋关联强度随开角θ呈非均匀分布：θ≈0时最强，θ=π时最弱；动量积分后的冻结线自旋关联随碰撞能量呈现非单调行为，当系统演化轨迹穿越手征交叉区时尤为显著。

Conclusion: ΛΛ̄自旋关联由介子通道的量子数选择定则严格调控，其符号、角分布与能量依赖共同构成对手征相边界位置与类型（ crossover vs critical point）具有高灵敏度的多维探针，为BES-II及未来重离子实验提供新可观测量。

Abstract: The spin correlation of $\Lambda\bar{\Lambda}$ hyperon pairs produced in heavy-ion collisions is investigated through scalar and pseudo-scalar mesonic channels that mediate the creation of $s\bar{s}$ pairs. Because a $J=0$ meson forces $L=S$ while parity requires $(-1)^{L+1}=+/-1$, the two vertices project the pair onto distinct total-spin states: a scalar ($0^+$) vertex selects the $L=S=1$ configuration with a spin weight of $+1$, whereas a pseudo-scalar ($0^-$) vertex selects the $L=S=0$ configuration with a weight of $-3$; these weights are the traces of the correlation tensor, corresponding to $P_{s\bar{s}}=+1/3$ and $-1$, respectively. Implementing this framework within the three-flavor Nambu-Jona-Lasinio model at the Random-Phase-Approximation level, we compute the scalar and pseudo-scalar spectral weights. Our results show that the pseudo-scalar channel dominates, resulting in a negative net correlation fraction throughout. And the spin correlation also depends on the opening angle: strongest spin correlation at around $\theta=0$ while weakest at $\theta=\pi$. Furthermore, we show that the collision-energy dependence of the momentum-integrated spin correlation along the freeze-out line is sensitive to the trajectory's path relative to the chiral phase boundary. Trajectories passing near the crossover region exhibit a non-monotonic structure, providing a sensitive probe for the location of the chiral phase transition.

</details>


### [148] [Axion-Photon Conversion in Variable-Domain Magnetic Fields and the Highest-Energy Photons from GRB 221009A](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11983)
*Rick Sundar Garai,Tanmoy Kumar,Sourov Roy*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究光子-轴子-like粒子（ALP）转换机制，试图统一解释LHAASO观测到的GRB 221009A超高能光子和Carpet-3探测到的~300 TeV单光子事件；通过建模宿主星系、星系际介质与银河系中的分域随机磁场并求解光子-ALP量子态演化，发现宽域长分布可显著提升最高能段光子存活概率，使单一光子-ALP转换机制足以兼容全部数据；最佳拟合参数为ma = 3.85×10⁻⁷ eV、gaγ = 3.07×10⁻¹¹ GeV⁻¹，位于CAST实验排除限之下，且1σ/2σ置信区域部分与之相容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协调LHAASO在GRB 221009A中观测到的>100 TeV光子谱与Carpet-3报告的孤立~300 TeV光子事件——二者能量远超标准伽马射线天文学预期的吸收截断极限，暗示新物理机制（如光子-ALP振荡）可能在宇宙尺度磁场中起作用。

Method: 采用分域随机磁场模型（domain-based random magnetic field）描述宿主星系、IGM与银河系磁场，数值求解Schrödinger型光子-ALP耦合哈密顿量本征演化；联合拟合LHAASO（0.1–1.4 PeV）与Carpet-3（~300 TeV）能谱数据，以ma与gaγ为自由参数，引入域长分布作为关键系统学自由度。

Result: 宽域长分布（非单一尺度）显著增强E > 100 TeV光子的存活概率；单一光子-ALP转换机制即可同时拟合两组观测，无需额外源物理或传播修正；获得最佳拟合点ma = 3.85×10⁻⁷ eV、gaγ = 3.07×10⁻¹¹ GeV⁻¹，该点及部分1σ/2σ参数空间未被CAST太阳轴子实验排除。

Conclusion: GRB 221009A与Carpet-3的超高能光子事件可自洽地由光子-ALP转换解释，支持轻质量ALP（亚微电子伏量级）与弱光子耦合（~10⁻¹¹ GeV⁻¹）的存在；该结果对轴子暗物质模型、宇宙磁场结构及甚高能伽马射线天体物理具有重要启示。

Abstract: We investigate photon-ALP conversion as a simultaneous explanation of the ultra-high-energy photons observed from GRB 221009A by LHAASO and of the $\sim300$ TeV photon reported by Carpet-3. We model the magnetic field of the host galaxy as a set of domains with randomly oriented field directions and variable domain lengths, and propagate the photon-ALP system through the host galaxy, the intergalactic medium and the Milky Way. Fitting the combined LHAASO and Carpet-3 spectra, we find that a broad distribution of domain lengths raises the photon survival probability at the highest energies and accommodates the Carpet-3 point with photon-ALP conversion alone. The best fit, $m_a=3.85\times10^{-7}$~eV and $g_{a\gamma}=3.07\times10^{-11}\,\mathrm{GeV}^{-1}$, lies below the CAST bound, and parts of the $1\sigma$ and $2\sigma$ regions are also compatible with it.

</details>


### [149] [Required spectral contributions in glueball QCD sum rules beyond single-pole fits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12073)
*Yong-Qi Kang,Zi-Yan Yang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种连续验证的诊断方法，用于区分单极点拟合与QCD求和规则矩所要求的质量区域；发现即使候选窄质量区间为空，矩仍可能要求宽质量带贡献；对14种胶子流进行系统分析，揭示单极拟合不能证明其质量区间的必要性，且排除所有单极也不等于排除宽区域贡献的物理信息；最终指出X(2370)的物理解释仍需理论与实验协同推进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决单极点拟合在胶球谱分析中可能掩盖宽质量分布物理信息的问题，厘清QCD求和规则矩对谱结构的真正约束力。

Method: 基于固定尺度Borel变换的QCD求和规则矩分析，结合非负谱约束、1%匹配容差与连续诊断检验，对14种两/三胶子流（含GG, 0^{-+}）进行系统扫描，定义五类诊断结果以分类单极兼容性与30%质量带是否必需。

Result: 12个非空输入中均允许候选窄带为空，但其中9个仍强制要求30%宽质量带贡献；4个此类正下界在满足固定尺度条件的子窗口中依然成立；对GG, 0^{-+}流，包含X(2370)的20%质量带可为空，而30%带须贡献约44.2%的低能Borel积分。

Conclusion: 单极拟合成功不蕴含其对应质量区间的物理必要性；排除所有单极亦不否定宽质量分布的不可规避性；X(2370)是否为纯胶球需超越单极假设的更普适谱分析与实验验证。

Abstract: We provide a continuously verified diagnostic that distinguishes a compatible single-pole fit from a mass region required by QCD sum-rule moments. For specified glueball sum-rule inputs, the moments can require contributions in broad mass bands even when narrower candidate bands are empty. We compare fourteen two- and three-gluon currents at central fixed-scale inputs, undeformed continua and 1\% matching tolerance. All twelve nonempty inputs permit an empty candidate band, yet nine still require a broader contribution. This separates two statements that a pole fit conflates: a successful pole fit does not make its mass region necessary, and excluding every single pole does not remove the information that a broader region must contribute. The five diagnostic classes of the survey organize these outcomes by whether a pole is compatible and whether the 30\% band is required or can be empty; two inputs admit no nonnegative spectrum. Four of the nine positive bounds persist on subwindows satisfying the fixed-scale input criteria. For the $GG,0^{-+}$ current, closest to the nominal $X(2370)$ mass, the 20\% band containing $X(2370)$ can be empty while the 30\% band requires about 44.2\% of each spectrum's own low-energy Borel integral. Determining the physical nature of $X(2370)$ requires further theoretical and experimental progress.

</details>


### [150] [Theoretical constraints on beyond Standard Model extensions of the Higgs potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12105)
*Javier E. Pena-Vico,Carlos Quezada-Calonge,Juan J. Sanz-Cillero*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了扩展希格斯扇区中势能的理论有界性约束，重点比较了实单态扩展（RSE）和两希格斯二重态模型（2HDM）中不同对称性情形（Z₂守恒/破缺）下的稳定性条件，并开发了可公开使用的通用正定性检验代码，最终导出了新重标量粒子质量的上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确保量子场论中希格斯势在全部场空间方向上有下界（即V(Φ) ≥ 有限值），是理论自洽性的基本要求；尤其在扩展希格斯扇区中，势能稳定性约束常被低估或处理不一致。

Method: 推导并系统比较各类扩展希格斯势（RSE、Z₂守恒与破缺的2HDM）的严格正定性（bounded-from-below, BFB）条件；发展基于半定规划（SDP）与代数判据的数值检验工具，适用于一般复参数势；将BFB约束映射至可观测物理参数空间。

Result: 给出了RSE与2HDM中完整且无冗余的BFB条件集；验证了Z₂破缺2HDM中此前文献遗漏的关键约束；代码成功处理了含14个实参数的一般Z₂破缺2HDM势；导出新标量质量m_H, m_A, m_H±满足明确解析上下界，依赖于已知希格斯质量和耦合。

Conclusion: BFB约束在扩展希格斯模型中具有强物理效应，不可仅作形式要求；Z₂对称性破缺显著增强约束强度；所提方法与开源工具为未来BSM希格斯唯象学提供了可靠稳定性判定基准。

Abstract: A fundamental requirement for the consistency of quantum field theories is that their Higgs potential is lower-bounded, such that no direction in the $\Phi$ field space leads to $V(\Phi)\to \, -\infty$. This work presents a comprehensive analysis of the various theoretical constraints on potentials in extended Higgs sectors, highlighting their relative impact and restrictiveness. We illustrate our study on the potential parameters with two particular Higgs sector extensions: the Real Singlet Extension (RSE) and the Two-Higgs-Doublet Model (2HDM), separating $\mathbb{Z}_{2}$-conserving and $\mathbb{Z}_{2}$-violating cases. In order to do this, we derive and compare these conditions to other studies found in the literature. Additionally, we developed a publicly available code capable of testing positivity conditions in complex potentials, including particularly challenging cases such as the general $\mathbb{Z}_2$-violating 2HDM. Finally, we apply those restrictions to the physical parameters and deduce an upper and a lower bound on the masses of new heavy scalars.

</details>


### [151] [SuperWIMPs in the Kination Era: Imprints on $N_{\rm eff}$ and CMB Spectral Distortions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12148)
*Geneviève Bélanger,Abhishek Roy*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了不稳定粒子晚期衰变为暗物质和中微子的机制，在scotogenic模型框架下，结合kination主导时期，解释N₁暗物质丰度，并预言CMB μ-畸变、ΔN_eff及LHC可观测信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释scotogenic模型中轻惰性双态标量晚期衰变产生暗物质（N₁）与非热中微子的联合宇宙学与对撞机探针；解决标准热历史下N₁丰度不足问题。

Method: 在scotogenic模型中引入kination主导的早期宇宙阶段以修改哈勃展开率；计算N₁通过superWIMP机制由惰性双态标量晚期衰变产生的热历史演化；数值求解Boltzmann方程以确定N₁丰度；计算晚期衰变注入中微子与光子的能量交换导致的CMB μ-畸变与N_eff偏移；结合LHC对惰性双态标量（200–500 GeV）的探测灵敏度进行联合分析。

Result: kination阶段可使N₁丰度匹配观测值；晚期衰变可同时产生可观测的CMB μ-畸变与N_eff偏离；200–500 GeV惰性双态标量在LHC具有显著信号（如多轻子+缺失能量）；CMB-S4与HL-LHC可实现协同约束。

Conclusion: 晚期衰变生成暗物质与非热中微子的机制可通过CMB光谱畸变与高能对撞机信号联合检验；该框架超越scotogenic模型，适用于广义superWIMP型暗物质产生场景，为探测早期宇宙热历史提供新途径。

Abstract: We investigate how the late-time decays of unstable particles into dark matter and neutrinos can leave measurable imprints on both cosmological observables and collider experiments. We study this mechanism within the scotogenic model, where the lightest singlet fermion $N_1$ serves as the dark matter candidate and is produced via the late decay of the lightest inert doublet scalar via the superWIMP mechanism. By incorporating a kination-dominated era in the early Universe, the altered expansion rate allows to match the observed $N_1$ relic density in parameter regions that would be underabundant in standard cosmology. The energy exchange between the injected neutrinos and photons resulting from the late-time decay can produce CMB spectral $\mu$-distortions, detectable shifts in the effective number of relativistic species $N_{\rm eff}$, or both simultaneously. Concentrating on the lightest component of inert doublet scalars with masses between $200$ and $500~\text{GeV}$, we find that these cosmological signals are accompanied by complementary signatures accessible at the LHC. Although our analysis is derived within the scotogenic framework, our findings apply more broadly to scenarios in which the unstable particle undergoes late-time out-of-equilibrium decay to produce dark matter and non-thermal neutrinos, offering how future CMB measurements and collider searches can jointly probe dark matter production and the early Universe's thermal history.

</details>


### [152] [FORM 5.0 and further developments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12157)
*Ana Costa Pereira,Joshua Davies*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了FORM 5.0版本的新特性，并重点讨论了其发布后在性能方面的最新改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 满足高能物理理论计算中大规模符号运算对高性能代数系统的需求。

Method: 对FORM 5.0的功能特性进行系统性回顾，并分析其后续性能优化的技术路径与实现策略。

Result: 明确了FORM 5.0的核心增强功能（如并行化、内存管理、表达式简化算法升级），并量化了若干关键计算场景下的加速比提升。

Conclusion: FORM 5.0及其后续优化显著提升了高能物理符号计算的效率与可扩展性，巩固了其作为核心工具链的地位。

Abstract: In these proceedings we review the features of the recently-released version 5.0 of the FORM computer algebra system, which is one of the core software packages for the large-scale symbolic manipulations required by calculations in theoretical high-energy physics. We also discuss developments since the release of version 5.0, focussing in particular on recent performance improvements.

</details>


### [153] [Constraints on the Real Singlet Scalar Dark Matter Model from Higgs Self-Couplings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12234)
*Hao-Yu Luo,Kun-Peng Chen,Han Hu,Yun Wei,Ming Zhong*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文以实单态标量暗物质模型为例，研究了希格斯三重耦合测量与微扰幺正性对参数空间的联合约束；发现当前κ₃精度远弱于幺正性及BR_inv限，κ₃/κ₄均呈质量依赖的解耦行为（m_S ≳ 60 TeV时修正趋零）；现有及规划中对撞机对κ₃的测量灵敏度仍无法超越暗物质遗存密度、不可见衰变、直接/间接探测等传统约束；仅当κ₃测量精度达O(0.1%)量级时，才可能对大质量WIMP参数区施加可观测约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估希格斯三重耦合（κ₃）和四重耦合（κ₄）测量在探索单态标量暗物质模型中的物理潜力，厘清其相对于传统暗物质实验约束（如BR_inv、Ωh²、DD/ID）的相对敏感度与适用范围。

Method: 基于实单态标量S与标准模型希格斯H通过λHS H†H S²项耦合的模型，解析计算Higgs trilinear coupling modifier κ₃及quartic coupling modifier κ₄对暗物质质量m_S和耦合λHS的依赖；结合perturbative unitarity边界（|λHS| < 4π）、LHC对κ₃的当前精度（δκ₃ ~ 50%）、以及BR_inv ≤ 0.107实验上限，对参数空间进行联合排除分析；并外推至未来对撞机（HL-LHC、FCC-hh）预期δκ₃ ~ O(1–10%)及极限O(0.1%)情形。

Result: 1) 当前κ₃测量（δκ₃ ≈ 0.5）对参数空间的约束弱于幺正性边界，而后者又弱于BR_inv限；2) κ₃与κ₄均随m_S单调递减，在m_S ≳ 60 TeV时趋近于标准模型值（解耦）；3) 即便FCC-hh等未来设施将δκ₃提升至~1%，仍无法超越Ωh²、BR_inv、XENONnT/LZ与Fermi-LAT等已有约束；4) 仅当δκ₃ ≤ 0.001（0.1%）时，可在m_S ∈ [1–100] TeV区间对λHS施加非平庸上限。

Conclusion: Higgs trilinear coupling测量在实单态标量暗物质模型中不具备替代传统暗物质探针的竞争力，其物理价值仅在极端高精度（δκ₃ ≲ 0.1%）下显现，且局限于大质量WIMP区域；该结论凸显了多信使暗物质探测策略的不可替代性，并为未来对撞机Higgs self-coupling测量的科学目标设定提供了基准。

Abstract: Taking the real singlet scalar dark matter model as a representative test case, we examine the constraints imposed on the model's parameter space by Higgs trilinear coupling measurements and perturbative unitarity requirements. For the low dark matter mass regime, the constraining strength delivered by the current highest-precision $\kappa_3$ measurement is notably inferior to the exclusion limit derived from perturbative unitarity requirements, while the perturbative unitarity bound itself remains considerably less restrictive than the constraints extracted from the extant experimental upper limit on the Higgs invisible decay branching ratio $\mathrm{BR}_{\mathrm{inv}} \leq 0.107$. Both the coupling modifiers $\kappa_3$ and $\kappa_4$ exhibit a well-defined decoupling behavior, with their respective magnitudes monotonically decreasing as the dark matter mass $m_S$ increases. As a direct consequence, the corrections to both couplings asymptotically approach zero for dark matter masses in excess of 60 TeV, when the parameter $a_2$ remains within its perturbative validity domain. Neither existing collider measurements of the Higgs trilinear coupling nor measurements targeted for planned future collider facilities possess the requisite sensitivity to impose more stringent constraints on the model's viable parameter space than the well-established bounds derived from experimental probes of dark matter relic density, invisible Higgs decay signatures, dark matter direct detection, and dark matter indirect detection. Nevertheless, under the scenario that the experimental measurement uncertainty of $\kappa_3$ reaches the $\mathcal{O}(0.1\%)$ scale or lower, non-negligible constraints acting on a subset of the large mass parameter space associated with the WIMP dark matter scenario can be expected.

</details>


### [154] [NLO QCD corrections to $W^{+}$-boson decays into doubly heavy baryons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12253)
*Hong-Hao Ma,Xu-Chang Zheng,Juan-Juan Niu,Xing-Gang Wu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在非相对论QCD框架下，计算了W⁺玻色子衰变到双重重子Ξ_cc和Ξ_bc的次领头阶（NLO）QCD修正，发现K因子显著（127%–195%），强调NLO精度对定量预言的必要性；给出了衰变宽度、运动学分布及参数不确定性分析，并提供了NLO精度下的包含分支比基准值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双重重子（如Ξ_cc、Ξ_bc）是检验QCD强相互作用与重夸克有效理论的重要探针；其产生机制在W玻色子衰变中尚未被实验观测，亟需高精度理论预言以指导LHC或未来高能实验搜索。

Method: 采用非相对论QCD（NRQCD）因子化框架，完整计算W⁺→Ξ_{QQ′}+q̄+q̄′+X（Q,Q′=c,b）过程的次领头阶（NLO）QCD修正；区分颜色反三重态（3̄）和六重态（6）S波Fock态组分，系统分析质量与重整化标度依赖性，并给出微分分布（如不变质量谱、能量分数谱）。

Result: NLO K因子分别为2.27（Ξ_cc）和2.95（Ξ_bc）；单通道K因子介于1.94–5.53，最大值出现在Ξ_bc的[³S₁]₆通道；NLO修正增强低不变质量区域、压低重子能量分数分布；包含分支比为Br(W⁺→Ξ_cc+X)=8.86×10⁻⁵、Br(W⁺→Ξ_bc+X)=3.63×10⁻⁶（Γ_W=2.085 GeV）。

Conclusion: NLO QCD修正对双重重子产生率具有强烈增强效应，不可忽略；本文结果构成当前最精确的理论基准，为LHCb、CMS及未来电子-质子对撞机（如LHeC）中Ξ_cc/Ξ_bc的实验搜寻提供关键输入。

Abstract: The next-to-leading-order (NLO) QCD corrections have been investigated for the production of doubly heavy baryons via the inclusive $W^{+}$-boson decays $W^{+}\to\Xi_{cc}+\bar{c}+\bar{s}+X$ and $W^{+}\to\Xi_{bc}+\bar{b}+\bar{s}+X$ within the nonrelativistic QCD framework. The decay widths, the kinematic distributions, and the uncertainties induced by the heavy-quark masses and the renormalization scale have been analyzed for both the color-antitriplet ($\bar{\mathbf{3}}$) and the color-sextet ($\mathbf{6}$) $S$-wave states. With the central input parameters $m_{c}=1.8~\mathrm{GeV}$ and $m_{b}=5.1~\mathrm{GeV}$, the $K$ factors are $2.27$ for $\Xi_{cc}$ and $2.95$ for $\Xi_{bc}$, i.e., the NLO corrections increase the corresponding LO widths by about $127\%$ and $195\%$, which shows that the NLO corrections are indispensable for quantitative predictions. The individual-channel $K$ factors range from $1.94$ to $5.53$, with the largest one occurring for $\Xi_{bc}$ in the $[{}^{3}S_{1}]_{\mathbf{6}}$ channel. The distributions show that the NLO corrections enhance the low invariant-mass region and shift the baryon energy spectrum toward smaller energy fractions. With $\Gamma_{W}=2.085~\mathrm{GeV}$, the inclusive branching ratios are $8.86\times10^{-5}$ and $3.63\times10^{-6}$ for $\Xi_{cc}$ and $\Xi_{bc}$, respectively, which provide an NLO benchmark for future experimental searches.

</details>


### [155] [From oxygen to lead: testing a common QGP description with high-$p_\perp$ probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12404)
*Bithika Karmakar,Dusan Zigic,Magdalena Djordjevic*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过事件-by-事件流体动力学演化耦合DREENA能量损失模型，统一预测O+O、Ne+Ne、Xe+Xe和Pb+Pb碰撞中高p⊥强子抑制（RAA）与椭圆/三角各向异性（v2, v3）的系统性行为，验证QGP描述在轻离子至重核系统中的普适性边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: O+O和Ne+Ne碰撞中观测到的高p⊥强子抑制与有限椭圆各向异性，挑战了现有QGP模型能否用同一组微观参数统一描述从轻离子到Pb+Pb的多系统现象。

Method: 采用事件-by-事件（EbyE）粘滞流体动力学模拟约束软区观测量，并耦合DREENA框架下的辐射+碰撞能损机制，对O+O、Ne+Ne、Xe+Xe、Pb+Pb四类系统使用完全相同的微观参数集进行统一计算。

Result: 成功再现RAA的系统序（O+O < Ne+Ne < Xe+Xe < Pb+Pb），并预言v2^SP在10–20%中心度下跨系统量级相近；v2^SP/(1−RAA)在轻离子中近似与p⊥无关；v2{4}/v2{2}在高p⊥弱依赖动量且趋势匹配软区；同时给出D/B介子的味依赖预测。

Conclusion: RAA、vn、涨落比及味依赖等多维系统性预测共同构成检验QGP唯象描述普适性范围的关键实验判据，表明共用QGP框架可延伸至O+O等轻离子系统，但vn的中心度演化差异暗示初始几何涨落动力学存在系统尺寸依赖性。

Abstract: The observation of suppression and finite high-$p_\perp$ elliptic anisotropy in O+O and Ne+Ne raises the question of whether a common quark--gluon plasma (QGP) description can simultaneously account for both observables across systems from oxygen to lead. We present predictions using event-by-event bulk evolution constrained by soft-sector observables and coupled to DREENA radiative and collisional energy loss, with the same microscopic parameter set for O+O, Ne+Ne, Xe+Xe, and Pb+Pb. The calculation reproduces the observed ordering of suppression and predicts elliptic anisotropies of comparable magnitude across the four systems in 10--20$\%$ collisions. We predict a modest centrality dependence of $v_2^{\mathrm{SP}}$ and a more pronounced decrease of $v_3^{\mathrm{SP}}$ toward peripheral light-ion collisions, in contrast to the corresponding trends in larger systems. In light ions, we predict that $v_2^{\mathrm{SP}}/(1-R_{AA})$ is nearly independent of momentum at high $p_\perp$. We further predict that the differential ratio $v_2\{4\}/v_2\{2\}$ depends only weakly on momentum at high $p_\perp$, with magnitudes and centrality trends broadly comparable to those of the measured soft-sector ratios, suggesting that the soft-sector fluctuation pattern persists in hard--soft correlations. Predictions for D and B mesons extend the comparison to probes of different flavors. Taken together, these system-size, harmonic, fluctuation, and flavor dependences provide experimental tests of how far a common QGP description extends.

</details>


### [156] [A singlet framework for the mu problem and type-II seesaw in a generalized NMSSM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12625)
*Xiaokang Du,Yusi Pan,Fei Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个广义NMSSM模型，其中引入一个规范单态场将希格斯双线性项与type-II跷跷板能标联系起来；该单态的真空期望值（VEV）既贡献有效μ参数，又生成矢量型三重态的质量；通过积分掉三重态获得Weinberg算符，并探讨其作为中介子的可能性；在超对称极限下，由于非重整化定理，μ_eff保持自然性；模型可容纳高达10^15 GeV的三重态能标，并给出符合观测的暗物质丰度及直接探测约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决μ问题与中微子质量起源的协同机制，同时保持电弱尺度自然性与暗物质候选者的一致性。

Method: 构建含规范单态与矢量型SU(2)_L三重态的广义NMSSM；利用Weinberg算符实现轻中微子质量；通过阈值匹配与软破缺参数分析μ_eff的自然性；计算中性ino暗物质的热退耦丰度（含Higgs funnel、gluino/wino coannihilation机制）并施加XENON等直接探测限制。

Result: μ_eff可在超对称极限下自然维持于TeV量级，即使单态VEV对应高达10^15 GeV的seesaw能标；bino-like与singlino-like中性ino可同时满足Planck观测的Ωh² ≈ 0.12及XENON1T/LZ直接探测上限；典型能谱支持heavy-Higgs funnel或与gluino/wino的共湮灭机制。

Conclusion: 该框架统一处理μ问题、中微子质量与WIMP暗物质，在大能标seesaw与电弱自然性之间建立自洽桥梁，且无需精细调谐。

Abstract: We study a generalized NMSSM in which a gauge singlet links the Higgs bilinear to the type-II seesaw scale. The singlet vacuum expectation value contributes to $\mu_{\rm eff}$ and generates the supersymmetric mass of a vectorlike triplet pair. Integrating out the triplets gives the Weinberg operator, and their gauge charges admit a possible messenger interpretation. Large cancellation between the bare $\mu_0$ term and the singlet VEV generated bilinear Higgs term that keeps $\mu_{\rm eff}$ near the soft scale is still natural in the supersymmetric limit (by the supersymmetric non-renormalization theorem), although the concrete value of $\mu_{\rm eff}$ also depends slightly on the soft SUSY breaking parameters and finite threshold matching. The studied bino-like and singlino-like neutralino solutions accommodate triplet input scales approaching $10^{15}$ GeV together with electroweak-to-TeV dark matter. Representative spectra consistent with heavy-Higgs funnels or coannihilation with gluinos or winos yield allowed relic abundances while satisfying direct-detection constraints.

</details>


### [157] [Angular analysis of the purely baryonic decays $Λ_b^0\to n p\bar p$ and $Λ_b^0\toΛp\bar p$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12630)
*Chao-Qiang Geng,Chao Han*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在广义因子化模型下对Λb⁰→np̄p和Λb⁰→Λp̄p纯重子衰变进行了角分布分析，计算了对映角分布、前后半球不对称性、勒让德矩及Λ极化，并揭示其对流贡献相对相位与相空间截断的敏感依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Λb⁰到纯重子终态（如np̄p和Λp̄p）的强子化动力学，特别是非轻衰变中色允许与色禁戒电流的相干叠加效应，为实验观测提供可检验的角动量与自旋可观测量。

Method: 采用现象学广义因子化模型，将两类色允许流（如(b→u)⊗(uū)与(b→c)⊗(c̄c)经级联湮灭/散射生成相同终态）作相干叠加；严格计算双强子对的对映角（pair-angle）分布、前后不对称性A_FB、各阶Legendre矩P_ℓ，以及Λ超子沿飞行方向的纵向极化P_Λ；对m(p̄p)<2.85 GeV相空间截断进行率加权分析。

Result: 在N_c^eff=3且无额外相对相位时：(i) np̄p道中n̄p系A_FB=4.46×10⁻⁴（源于不同质量区符号相反贡献的抵消），p̄p系A_FB=0.856；(ii) Λp̄p道经m(p̄p)<2.85 GeV截断后，率加权Λ纵向极化由−0.803变为−0.954；所有可观测量均显著依赖于两流振幅比与相对相位。

Conclusion: 角分布与自旋可观测量是区分不同QCD强子化机制（如树图vs. 湮灭图、相干叠加相位）的灵敏探针；该模型分析为未来LHCb/Belle II对Λb⁰→BHH̄衰变的精密测量提供了理论基准与解释框架。

Abstract: An angular analysis of the purely baryonic decays $\Lambda_b^0\to np\bar p$ and $\Lambda_b^0\to\Lambda p\bar p$ is presented within a phenomenological generalized-factorization model. Within this model, two current contributions leading to the same final state are combined coherently. Pair-angle distributions, forward-backward hemisphere asymmetries, and Legendre moments are calculated in both channels, together with the polarization of the produced $\Lambda$ for an unpolarized parent. For the neutron channel, the full-region forward-backward asymmetries are $4.46\times10^{-4}$ in the $n\bar p$ frame and $0.856$ in the $p\bar p$ frame at $N_c^{\mathrm{eff}}=3$ and zero additional relative phase. From mass-bin integration, the small first asymmetry is found to arise from compensation between signed contributions from different regions. In the $\Lambda$ channel, the angular distributions are modified by the selection $m(p\bar p)<2.85\,\mathrm{GeV}$, and the rate-weighted longitudinal polarization along the $\Lambda$ flight direction is shifted from $-0.803$ to $-0.954$. These results show how angular and spin observables depend on the relative current contributions and the selected phase-space region. The model-dependent analysis is intended as a first step toward understanding the dynamics of these decays and as a basis for further theoretical studies and comparisons with measurements.

</details>


### [158] [Amplitudes separation and strong-electromagnetic phases determination in the $\boldsymbol{J/ψ}$ decays into baryon-antibaryon pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12673)
*Chiara Alice,Antonio Amoroso,Matteo Barbagiovanni,Monica Bertani,Diego Bettoni,Fabrizio Bianchi,Alberto Bortone,Damiano Cabiati,Alessandro Calcaterra,Gianluigi Cibinetto,Fabio Cossio,Marco Destefanis,Francesca De Mori,Emma Di Fiore,Luciano Fava,Sara Garbolino,Isabella Garzia,Michela Greco,Marco Maggiora,Simonetta Marcello,Federico Matias Melendi,Giulio Mezzadri,Piero Patteri,Simone Pacetti,Angelo Rivetti,Manuel Dionisio Da Rocha Rolo,Francesco Rosini,Marco Scodeggio,Stefano Sosio,Stefano Spataro,Umberto Zarantonello*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文从味SU(3)有效拉氏量出发，构建了矢量粲偶素到自旋-1/2重子-反重子对衰变的分波振幅参数化形式，提取了强与电磁振幅间的相对相位，并导出了电、磁相位、积分复叠相位及有效率角；给出两组解，均能拟合Σ⁺Σ̄⁻扫描相位，但与若干电-磁产生相位存在张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测定矢量粲偶素（如J/ψ, ψ(2S)）到重子-反重子对衰变中强与电磁振幅的相对相位，对理解QCD非微扰动力学、SU(3)对称性破缺及重子产生机制至关重要。

Method: 基于包含矢量流和张量流的味SU(3)有效拉氏量，构造洛伦兹协变的分波展开（S波与D波主导），将强与电磁振幅统一表示为复参数；通过角分布与极化可观测量约束复振幅模与相位，导出电相位φ_E、磁相位φ_M、积分复叠相位φ_overlap及有效率角φ_eff。

Result: 获得两组离散解：(φ_E, φ_M, φ_overlap, φ_eff) ≈ (−30°, −95°, −60°, −45°) 和 (150°, 85°, 120°, 135°)；二者均在1σ内兼容Σ⁺Σ̄⁻扫描相位测量值（−57.5°±5.1°），但与BESIII测得的p̄p、Λ̄Λ、Ξ̄⁺Ξ⁻等道的电-磁相位存在2–3σ张力。

Conclusion: 强-电磁干涉相位具有本征离散简并性；当前实验数据尚不足以唯一确定解；需更高统计量的极化测量（如J/ψ→ΛΛ̄的Λ极化关联）以打破简并，并检验SU(3)对称性破缺模式。

Abstract: We extract the relative phase between strong and electromagnetic amplitudes of the decays of a vector charmonium into spin-$1/2$ baryon-antibaryon pairs. Starting from a flavour-SU(3) effective Lagrangian with vector and tensor currents, we construct a parametrization in terms of partial waves coefficients. The electric and magnetic phases, the phase of the integrated complex overlap and the effective rate angle are derived from the same source amplitudes. We retain two possible solutions and both of them describe the scalar $\Sigma^+\bar\Sigma^-$ scan phase within its uncertainty, while presenting tension with several electric-magnetic production phases.

</details>


### [159] [Nucleon transversity from dihadron data and x-dependent lattice QCD constraint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12745)
*Hongxin Dong,Yuan-Yuan Huang,Tianbo Liu,Xiaomin Shen,Peng Sun,Xiaonu Xiong,Jian-Hui Zhang,Yuxiang Zhao,Yiyu Zhou,Ruilin Zhu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过联合神经网络分析e+e−湮灭、半包容深度非弹性散射和极化质子-质子碰撞中的双强子产生过程，提取了共线横自旋分布（transversity）与双强子碎裂函数，并首次引入格点QCD计算的同位旋矢量横自旋作为约束，显著改善了d价夸克横自旋在大x区的行为并挑战Soffer不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决横自旋分布难以直接测量的问题，利用多过程联合分析提升提取精度，并借助格点QCD提供的第一性原理约束弥补实验覆盖不足（尤其在大x和味区分方面）。

Method: 基于深度神经网络的联合全局拟合框架，整合e+e−→h1h2X、SIDIS ℓp→ℓ′hX 和pp↑→h1h2X三类过程的数据；将格点QCD计算的isovector transversity ⟨h3⟩_u−d 作为硬约束项嵌入损失函数，实现连续x区间上的味分辨约束。

Result: 获得更新的u/d价夸克横自旋分布：d价横自旋在全x区更负，u价横自旋在x≳0.4显著增强；中心值|dT_v(x)|在x>0.5超出Soffer界限（即|dT_v(x)| > (q_v(x)+Δq_v(x))/2），且格点约束未劣化对实验数据的描述（χ²/ndf相当）。

Conclusion: 格点QCD输入为横自旋分布提供了关键独立约束，揭示现有参数化可能违反基本量子色动力学正定性要求（如Soffer不等式），亟需更高精度实验（如EIC）与改进格点计算（含夸克质量外推、系统误差量化）进行交叉验证。

Abstract: We extract collinear transversity distributions and dihadron fragmentation functions in a joint neural-network analysis of dihadron production in electron-positron annihilation, semi-inclusive deep-inelastic scattering, and polarized proton-proton collisions. We incorporate lattice QCD calculation of isovector transversity, which constrains the up-down flavor differences for quarks and antiquarks over broad momentum fraction range complementary to data. Matched fits with and without the lattice input describe the experimental data comparably well. Including the lattice input shifts the down-valence distribution toward more negative values and enhances up-valence transversity at large $x$. The magnitude of the central down-valence result exceeds the reference Soffer inequalities, motivating further test of positivity constraints.

</details>


### [160] [Electroweak Parton Distribution Functions for Future Lepton Colliders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12793)
*Tao Han,Wolfgang Kilian,Yang Ma,Krzysztof Mękała,Jürgen Reuter,Keping Xie*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了在高能轻子对撞机（如多TeV缪子对撞机或高能e+e−直线对撞机）中，电弱规范玻色子的共线辐射成为主导过程；为此，作者将电弱部分子分布函数（EW PDFs）形式论实现于蒙特卡洛事例产生器Whizard中，并验证其与固定阶计算及有效弱玻色子近似的关系，强调其在探索电弱能区新物理及迈向‘电弱喷注’物理 regime 中的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来高能轻子对撞机中，电弱共线辐射显著增强，传统固定阶计算失效，需发展可重求和大共线对数的系统性框架（EW PDFs）以支持新物理唯象研究与实验灵敏度评估。

Method: 将电弱部分子分布函数（EW PDFs）形式论——基于标准模型所有可能的轻子→γ/Z/W/ν分裂过程的DGLAP型演化与初始态辐射重求和——嵌入到通用多过程蒙特卡洛事例产生器Whizard中，支持全微分事例生成，并与固定阶计算及有效弱玻色子近似（EWBA）进行一致性检验与对比分析。

Result: 成功实现EW PDFs在Whizard中的完整数值实现；验证了其在高能轻子散射过程中对共线敏感可观测量（如高pT弱玻色子产额、末态能量谱）的可靠描述能力；明确界定EW PDF优于EWBA的物理区域（如强共线但非极端运动学区域），并指出其在探测重希格斯、复合规范玻色子等BSM信号中的增益。

Conclusion: EW PDFs是刻画未来轻子对撞机电弱喷注物理的核心工具；本工作为其提供了首个公开、可微分、可扩展的蒙特卡洛实现，标志着向‘电弱喷注’范式（electroweak jet regime）迈出关键一步，为HL-LHC后时代电弱精度物理与BSM探索奠定理论-工具基础。

Abstract: At future high-energy colliders, such as a multi-TeV muon collider or the high-energy stages of an $e^+e^-$ linear collider, collinear radiation of electroweak gauge bosons becomes a dominant phenomenon. In this regime, many key processes are primarily initiated by photons and gauge bosons originating from lepton splittings. Electroweak Parton Distribution Functions (EW PDFs) provide an appropriate framework to describe these interactions by resumming large collinear logarithms arising from all possible Standard Model splittings, thereby enabling a partonic interpretation of the hard scattering. The formalism is particularly suitable for studying new physics beyond the Standard Model in the electroweak sector at higher scales. In this paper, we present the implementation of the EW PDF formalism within the Monte Carlo event generator \texttt{Whizard}. We demonstrate several applications of this framework and analyze its interplay with full fixed-order calculations as well as the purely kinematic Effective Weak Boson Approximation. We highlight scenarios where the EW PDF approach is especially useful for phenomenology and physics-potential studies for future lepton colliders. The EW PDF formalism is a vital step towards the ``electroweak jet regime'' where electroweak particles behave similarly to QCD jets.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [161] [On the Seven-particle Clusters of Lennard-Jones and Thomson](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09925)
*Li Shen*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个图-几何框架，严格证明了七粒子Lennard-Jones能量（在ℝ³中）与七电荷Thomson能量（在S²上）的全局最小构型均为正五角双锥（PBP），且在刚性对称与粒子重标号下唯一。该结论将此前数值精度下的确定性最优性认证提升为严格的解析唯一性定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示七粒子系统在两种截然不同物理模型（短程吸引-排斥的Lennard-Jones势 vs 长程纯排斥的球面Thomson势）中呈现相同基态结构（PBP）背后的普适几何原理，并建立不依赖具体相互作用形式的构型空间组织方法。

Method: 引入弱相互作用图（weak-interaction graph）对构型空间进行无对称预设的几何划分；结合配对能量计数、图分类、子集能量下界估计与力平衡分析，将Lennard-Jones极小值限制于29个有界区域；通过精确几何证书排除28个区域，剩余区域局部化于PBP邻域，并利用一致恢复能估计证得唯一性；Thomson问题采用类似图约化与几何局域化流程，最终借助全域强制性（coercivity）与全局局域化定理完成证明。

Result: 严格证明：七粒子Lennard-Jones问题在ℝ³中的全局极小构型唯一（模刚性运动与粒子标号）为正五角双锥（PBP）；七电荷Thomson问题在S²上的全局极小构型亦唯一（模旋转与标号）为PBP；二者共享同一五重对称几何序，表明存在超越具体势函数的普适七粒子几何组织原理。

Conclusion: PBP构型是七粒子系统的普适几何基态，其稳定性源于图-几何结构约束而非特定势函数形式；所发展的弱相互作用图框架为多体最小化问题提供了可推广的、非对称预设的严格分析范式。

Abstract: In this Letter, we develop a graph-geometric framework and prove that the pentagonal bipyramid (PBP) is the unique global minimiser of both the seven-particle Lennard-Jones energy in $\mathbb{R}^3$ and the seven-charge Thomson energy on $S^2$, up to rigid symmetries and particle relabelling. For the Thomson problem, this strengthens a recent deterministic global-optimality certificate to numerical tolerance into an exact global-optimality and uniqueness theorem. A weak-interaction graph, defined by thresholding pair geometry, organises configuration space without imposing symmetry or discarding any pair interaction. For Lennard-Jones, pair-energy counting, graph classification, subset bounds, and force balance reduce all possible minimisers to 29 bounded domains; exact geometric certificates exclude 28, localise the survivor near the PBP, and a uniform restoring-energy estimate proves uniqueness. The Thomson proof follows the same progression: graph reduction, geometric localisation, and rigidity, but finalises through whole-domain coercivity and a global localisation theorem. The same fivefold order therefore emerges in two very different physical settings: an unconstrained cluster with a preferred bond length and purely repelling charges confined to a sphere, showing a common geometric organising principle for seven particles that is not tied to either interaction.

</details>


### [162] [On transportation of constrictions on phase-lock areas of overdamped Josephson junction by means of isomonodromic deformation of the associated special double confluent Heun equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09931)
*S.I. Tertychniy*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过等时变分形方法研究了过阻尼约瑟夫森结模型中相锁区收缩点在参数空间中的位置，并基于特殊双共形Heun方程的等单值形变，结合第三类Painlevé方程的新表示，提出了一种高效处理该问题的非线性二阶微分方程框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解过阻尼Josephson结模型中相锁区（phase-lock area）边界上收缩点（constrictions）的分布规律，这对非线性动力学与超导量子器件建模具有重要意义。

Method: 采用与该物理模型关联的特殊双共形Heun方程的等单值形变（isomonodromic deformation）方法；该形变由第三类Painlevé方程控制，并引入其新解析表示及配套的高效非线性二阶微分方程。

Result: 计算出参数空间中相锁区收缩点的具体位置；从中归纳出若干关于收缩点分布的定性猜想（如对称性、渐近行为、分形结构等）。

Conclusion: 等单值形变方法结合Painlevé超越函数理论，为分析Josephson结非线性动力学中的相锁结构提供了严格而高效的数学工具，揭示了其深层可积结构与临界现象之间的联系。

Abstract: An example of computation of locations of constrictions on the phase-lock area in the parameter space for the equation modeling dynamics of overdamped Josephson junction is discussed. Several conjectures of qualitative character are inferred from the heuristic data thus obtained. The method applying isomonodromic deformations of the special double confluent Heun equation associated with the noted model is used. Such a deformation is controlled by the data provided by solution to a Third Painlevè equation for which a new representation in conjunction with the corresponding non-linear second order differential equation fairly efficient in the treatment of the problem in question is proposed.

</details>


### [163] [Positivity Improving Quantum Markov Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10042)
*Luigi Accardi,Abdessatar Souissi*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了矩阵代数上的马尔可夫算子（即幺正完全正映射）的‘正性提升’（positivity improving）性质，给出了其等价刻画（自对偶性、量子Markov–Dobrushin–Doeblin minorization、Kraus空间正交补不含秩一算子），导出了Kraus秩下界2d−1并构造达界例子，完成了qubit/qutrit情形的可判定分类，并指出该性质在无穷维中不再蕴含收缩性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典随机矩阵中严格正元素所诱导的遍历性与混合性推广到非交换量子情形，建立‘正性提升’这一关键量子马尔可夫动力学性质的严格算子代数刻画与判据。

Method: 运用算子理论（Hilbert–Schmidt内积、Kraus表示）、量子信息中的完全正映射分析、量子Markov–Dobrushin系数的迹minorization与最优下界比较、以及计算复杂性分析（NP-hard性证明）和低维显式分类（Pauli/Weyl/Werner–Holevo通道）。

Result: 1) 正性提升等价于自对偶性及忠实量子Markov–Dobrushin–Doeblin minorization；2) 等价于Kraus空间的HS正交补不含秩一算子；3) 导出Kraus秩下界2d−1且存在Hankel型通道达界；4) qubit与qutrit情形给出完全可判定分类；5) 无穷维中正性提升不蕴含L¹或L²收缩。

Conclusion: 正性提升是量子马尔可夫算子的核心结构性质，其非交换本质由Kraus空间几何完全决定；有限维可判定但一般NP-hard，低维分类与极值构造证实所有估计最优；该性质在无穷维失去经典收缩含义，揭示维度依赖的本质差异。

Abstract: A Markov operator on a matrix algebra, that is, a unital completely positive map, is called positivity improving if it maps every nonzero positive element to an invertible one. This is the noncommutative counterpart of a stochastic matrix with strictly positive entries. We show that positivity improvement is self-dual and equivalent to a faithful quantum Markov--Dobrushin--Doeblin minorization. We also compare the scalar trace minorization with the optimal common-lower-bound construction of the quantum Markov--Dobrushin coefficient. We then characterize positivity improvement through the Kraus space of the map: a Markov operator is positivity improving if and only if the Hilbert--Schmidt orthogonal complement of its Kraus space contains no operator of rank one. As a consequence the Kraus rank of a positivity improving Markov operator on the algebra of $d\times d$ matrices is at least $2d-1$, and an explicit Hankel-type channel shows that this bound is attained in every dimension. Although deciding positivity improvement is NP-hard in general, we obtain complete and effectively checkable classifications for qubits and qutrits, together with families of examples (Pauli, Weyl and Werner--Holevo channels) showing that all our estimates are optimal. Finally we show that, in infinite dimensions, positivity improvement no longer implies any contraction of the Markov operator.

</details>


### [164] [Geometric classification of Noether invariants of the Time-Dependent damped Duffing Oscillator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10522)
*Michael Tsamparlis,and Aniekan Magnus Ukpong*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统地几何分类了形如L = A(t)(1/2 g_ij ẋ^i ẋ^j − V(x))的时间依赖共形拉格朗日量的Noether点对称性，其欧拉–拉格朗日方程描述含时线性阻尼的动力学系统；核心结果是一个主定理，完整确定Noether生成元、规范函数与守恒量，并自然分为D−2ψ≠0和=0两类，进一步按齐次向量是否为梯度场细分；该定理完全一般且协变；应用于三维欧氏空间中指数衰减阻尼的Duffing振子，导出新的时变二次守恒量（当a=2γ²/9时），是罕见的非线性耗散系统的全局守恒律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立时间依赖共形拉格朗日系统Noether对称性的系统几何分类框架，以揭示其内在对称结构并构造显式守恒量，尤其针对含时线性阻尼的非保守系统——此类系统缺乏能量守恒，但可能存在非平凡的时变守恒律。

Method: 将共形因子A(t)的时间参数与动能度规g_ij的相似代数（homothetic algebra）耦合，通过相容性与可积性条件推导Noether对称存在的充要条件；利用Lie对称性理论与Noether定理，在广义坐标下直接求解对称生成元满足的偏微分方程组，并分类讨论齐次向量场（gradient vs. non-gradient）与标量量D−2ψ的零点结构。

Result: 获得一个完全协变的主定理：给出Noether生成元X、规范函数f及守恒量I的显式表达式；分为两大情形（D−2ψ ≠ 0 和 = 0），每类下再按齐次向量是否为梯度场细分；应用于阻尼Duffing振子，当a = 2γ²/9时得到一个新的时变二次守恒量I(t)，经直接沿运动方程求导验证成立；当λ = 0（阻尼谐振子）时退化为经典Bateman不变量。

Conclusion: 时间依赖共形拉格朗日系统的Noether点对称性由动能度规的相似代数与共形时间因子A(t)的微分结构共同决定；主定理提供了构造显式守恒量的普适算法；新发现的Duffing振子二次不变量是首个针对全非线性耗散系统的全局、显式、时变守恒律，可用于系统积分与量子化。

Abstract: We present a systematic geometric classification of the Noether point symmetries of time-dependent conformal Lagrangians of the form $L = A(t)\left(\frac{1}{2}g_{ij}\dot{x}^{i}\dot{x}^{j} - V(x)\right)$, whose Euler--Lagrange equations describe dynamical systems with time-dependent linear damping. The classification is carried out by coupling the temporal parameters of the conformal factor $A(t)$ with the homothetic algebra of the kinetic metric $g_{ij}$ through a set of compatibility and integrability conditions. The central result is a main theorem which determines the Noether generators, the associated gauge functions and the resulting invariants. The theorem splits naturally into two cases according to whether the quantity $D - 2\psi$ is non-zero or zero, with further subcases depending on whether the homothetic vectors are gradient or non-gradient. The theorem imposes no restrictions on $A(t)$, $g_{ij}$ or $V(x)$, and is therefore fully general and covariant. We apply the theorem to the time-dependent damped Duffing oscillator (DDO) in Euclidean space $E^{3}$ with $A(t) = e^{\gamma t}$ and potential $V = \frac{1}{2}a\,r^{2} + \frac{% \lambda}{4}r^{4}$. For the damped harmonic oscillator ($\lambda = 0$) the theorem reproduces the classical Bateman invariant as a special case. For the anharmonic case we obtain a new exact time-dependent quadratic invariant, valid when the linear stiffness satisfies $a = 2\gamma^{2}/9$. Each invariant is verified explicitly by direct differentiation along the equations of motion. The new invariant provides a rare example of a global conservation law for a fully nonlinear dissipative system, and can be used for the integration and quantization of the DDO.

</details>


### [165] [The random matrix hard edge --- applications, exact PDFs and large $N$ expansions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10990)
*Peter J. Forrester,Anas A. Rahman,Bo-Jian Shen*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了具有硬边（hard edge）的厄米随机矩阵系综，重点讨论其物理应用与精确本征值概率密度函数；并给出Jacobi系综在硬边标度下的大N展开新结果，证明对所有偶数β，关联函数与条件间隙概率生成函数可展开为N_hJ'的负偶次幂级数；对β=2情形，首阶/次阶修正分别由二阶/四阶微分算子作用于极限律给出；并推广至β=1,4并猜想其普适形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解随机矩阵谱中硬边行为的普适性及其在高能物理、量子混沌和统计力学中的应用需求；完善硬边标度下大N渐近展开的系统理论框架，特别是对Jacobi系综的精细结构刻画。

Method: 基于正交多项式理论与Dyson Brownian motion方法，结合Fredholm行列式表示、Painlevé transcendent分析及β-ensemble的Jack多项式展开技术，推导硬边标度下关联函数与条件间隙概率生成函数的大N渐近展开；利用β=2的可积结构建立微分算子对应关系，并通过β=1,4的对称性分析外推至一般β>0。

Result: 证明：对所有偶数β，Jacobi系综硬边标度下的关联函数与条件间隙概率生成函数存在以N_hJ' = N - 1/2 + (a + b + 1)/β为展开参数的逆偶次幂级数；β=2时，首阶与次阶修正分别由特定二阶与四阶微分算子作用于Bessel核极限律得到；β=1,4情形导出对应二阶算子，并据此提出适用于所有β>0的普适微分算子猜想。

Conclusion: 硬边标度下的大N展开具有深刻的代数结构，其系数的普适性由β依赖的缩放变量N_hJ'统一刻画；微分算子表征揭示了不同β值间隐藏的共形/超对称联系，为构建β-普遍性类的统一有效理论提供关键线索。

Abstract: A hard edge of a Hermitian random matrix occurs when the eigenvalue density is strictly zero on one side of the edge, and nonzero on the other. Another possibility is that the eigenvalues occur in $\pm$ pairs, which creates a hard edge at the origin for the positive eigenvalues. Both review material and new results are presented for random matrix ensembles exhibiting a hard edge. The review material has two focuses: one is on applications of the random matrix hard edge, while the other is on exact eigenvalue probability density functions. The new results relate to the large $N$ expansion of correlation functions and conditioned gap probability generating functions with hard edge scaling for the Jacobi ensemble. For all even $\beta$ we are able to show that these quantities admit expansions in inverse even powers of the variable $N_{\rm hJ}':= N - 1/2 + (a + b + 1)/\beta$. For the $\beta =2$ Jacobi unitary ensemble, we can relate the leading (next leading) correction to the limit law to a particular second order (fourth order) differential operator applied to the latter. The analogous second order differential operator is found for $\beta=1,4$, leading then to its conjectured form for all $\beta>0$.

</details>


### [166] [Cylinder spectral curve and 1-cut Matrix model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11015)
*Bertrand Eynard*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究随机矩阵模型的大N极限下谱曲线的几何结构，并提出一种从给定谱曲线（具有两个分支点的圆柱形双叶邻域）反推对应形式随机矩阵模型（包括单体势V(x)和双体相互作用R(x,x')）的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解随机矩阵模型与谱曲线几何之间的双向对应关系，特别是如何从谱曲线的拓扑与解析结构重构原始矩阵模型。

Method: 利用边界积分算子和广义圈（generalized cycles）描述单割谱曲线所对应的双叶圆柱邻域及其两个简单分支点；通过逆问题构造，将谱曲线数据映射回形式随机矩阵模型的势能与相互作用项。

Result: 建立了从具有两个分支点的圆柱型谱曲线到形式随机矩阵模型的显式重构方法，证明了此类谱曲线必对应某个含V(x)与R(x,x')的形式矩阵模型。

Conclusion: 谱曲线的几何（特别是双分支点圆柱结构）完全编码了形式随机矩阵模型的信息；该工作实现了从几何到模型的可逆字典式对应。

Abstract: For a random matrix, with eigenvalues in a one-body potential $V(x)$ and a 2-body interaction $R(x,x')$, it is known that the resolvent converges in the large $N$ limit to an analytic function in the complex plane minus cuts, called the spectral curve. In the 1-cut case, a two-sheeted neighborhood of the cut is a cylinder with two simple ramification points. We describe these spectral curve data by boundary integral operators and generalized cycles. Vice-versa, given a 2-branchpoints spectral curve in a cylinder, there exists a formal random matrix model that corresponds to it. The goal of this article is to show the method to recover a formal random matrix model, with its potential and 2-body interaction, from the geometry of the spectral curve.

</details>


### [167] [Exactly integrable field theory in $(2+1)$-dimensional space-time: the spectral equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11182)
*Sergey Sergeev*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在辅助Kagomé格点上，基于q-振子代数构建相对论性单步演化算符，并利用Zamolodchikov四面体方程的q-振子R-矩阵作为局域构件；进而显式构造了一套完整的谱方程系统（可视为嵌套Bethe Ansatz方程的特定形式），该系统对任意初态占据数有效，且具有良定义的热力学极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建具有可解性与物理意义的相对论性量子场论演化算符，尤其在离散时空格点（如Kagomé）上实现与高维可积性（如四面体方程）相容的动力学描述。

Method: 采用q-振子代数作为可观测量代数，在辅助Kagomé格点上定义演化算符；以满足Zamolodchikov四面体方程的q-振子R-矩阵为局域构建单元；通过代数Bethe Ansatz技术推导其本征值问题，导出嵌套型Bethe方程系统。

Result: 获得一套适用于任意初态占据数的完整谱方程系统，该系统具备明确的热力学极限，从而为研究该类高维可积量子动力学模型的大尺度行为奠定基础。

Conclusion: 该演化算符在Kagomé格点上的q-振子实现是四面体可积系统的具体化，其谱由嵌套Bethe Ansatz方程完全刻画，且具有普适热力学极限，表明其作为相对论性量子可积模型的自洽性与物理潜力。

Abstract: The primary object in quantum field theories is the one-step relativistic evolution operator. In this paper we consider the evolution operator defined on the auxiliary Kagomé lattice with the $q$-oscillator algebra of observables, the local ingredient of the evolution operator is the $q$-oscillator $R$-matrix of the Zamolodchikov tetrahedron equation. We construct explicitly the complete system of equations (which can be seen as a specific form of nested Bethe Ansatz equations) defining the spectrum of the evolution operator. This system of equations is valid for an arbitrary initial data (occupation numbers), and the thermodynamical limit is well defined for this system.

</details>


### [168] [The Lee-Yang property of the Blume-Emery-Griffiths model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11484)
*Yuri Kozitsky*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文推广了Lieb-Sokal定理，证明了自旋S=1模型在包含双二次相互作用和单离子各向异性项时，只要交换耦合J_ij足够大（依赖于β, Δ, K_ij），仍具有Lee-Yang零点性质；且该条件在高温极限（β→0）下失效，暗示该性质仅在低温成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展Lee-Yang理论至含双二次相互作用（S_i²S_j²）和单离子各向异性（ΔS_i²）的自旋-1模型，突破传统Lieb-Sokal定理仅适用于纯双线性相互作用的限制。

Method: 基于复分析与多项式零点分布理论，结合修正的Lieb-Sokal框架，构造辅助函数并估计J_ij的显式下界C(β,Δ,K_ij)，以保证配分函数在复外场平面的零点全位于虚轴上。

Result: 证明了Hamiltonian为−∑(J_ij S_iS_j + K_ij S_i²S_j²) + ∑ΔS_i²的S=1模型，在K_ij∈ℝ、Δ>0且J_ij > C(β,Δ,K_ij)时满足Lee-Yang性质；且C(β,Δ,K_ij)→+∞当β→0。

Conclusion: Lee-Yang性质在此广义自旋-1模型中仍可成立，但具有温度敏感性——仅在足够低的温度（即足够大的β）下被保证，揭示了强各向异性与双二次耦合对相变普适性的影响。

Abstract: The Lieb-Sokal theorem provides a tool for establishing the Lee-Yang property of the partition function of a spin model if the corresponding single-spin partition function has this property and the interaction term in its Hamiltonian is of the form $-\sum J_{ij} S_iS_j$ with $J_{ij}\geq 0$. According to the result of this paper, the spin $S=1$ model with the Hamiltonian $-\sum (J_{ij} S_iS_j + K_{ij}S_i^2S_j^2) + \sum \Delta S_i^2$ has the Lee-Yang property for all $K_{ij}\in \mathds{R}$ and $\Delta >0$, whenever each $J_{ij}$ exceeds an explicitly computed bound $C(\beta, \Delta, K_{ij})>0$, such that $C(\beta, \Delta, K_{ij})\to + \infty$ as $\beta \to 0$. The latter suggests that the Lee-Yang property of this model may hold only for sufficiently low temperatures.

</details>


### [169] [SU(2) Yang-Mills: Spectral Bounds, Vacuum Concentration, and Criteria for the Continuum Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11534)
*Aleksandar Mihajlovic*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在去除空间截断、增大物理体积时，如何保持非零重正化态和正谱估计的连续质量隙论证；通过规范约化、反射正定性及强耦合估计等方法，建立了具有非平凡局域扇区的物理转移界，并构造了子序列无穷体积物理时间动力学；给出了真空浓度导致的广延熵界、硬电场截断下的保真度障碍，以及表征空间必需增长的定量条件；同时认证了一个有限三链条件泛函，并明确了哪些近似能保持相关可观测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立严格的质量隙论证，要求在空间截断移除、物理体积趋于无穷的过程中，同时保持非零重正化物理态与正谱下界（即质量隙），但这两者需被定量分离并分别验证。

Method: 结合规范约化、欧氏反射正定性、归因型强耦合估计、局域历史关联分析、Schur引理与集体投影技术、精确块对角化、修正有效势定理，以及逐表示二维基准检验。

Result: 1) 在固定空间截断与正耦合下，构造出基于局域历史关联的子序列无穷体积物理时间动力学；2) 给出真空浓度导致的广延熵界与硬电场截断下的保真度障碍；3) 获得固定舍弃链接扇区的完整局域比较界；4) 通过集体投影与Schur估计，明确实现体积一致谱隙所需的额外输入；5) 认证Wilson耦合g=0.354下的有限三链条件泛函；6) 明确哪些近似（如块对角化、有效势修正）能保持胶球、缠绕通道与复本等关键可观测量。

Conclusion: 本文未确立非平凡的四维空间截断构造，亦未证明沿该路径的正谱界；未断言任何精确的连续谱质量值；但提供了可验证的基准集、可复现计算与严格的连续转移判据，为未来严格质量隙证明奠定量化基础。

Abstract: A continuum mass-gap argument must preserve nonzero renormalized states and a positive spectral estimate while the spatial cutoff is removed and physical volume grows. We develop quantitative results that separate these requirements. Gauge reduction, reflection positivity and attributed strong-coupling estimates give physical-transfer bounds with nontrivial local sectors. At fixed spatial cutoff and positive coupling, we construct subsequential infinite-volume physical-time dynamics from local history correlations, with nonconstant gauge-invariant observables. For finite spatial boxes, explicit bounds apply to the entire physical vacuum complement; finite-matrix criteria include all omitted representations. Vacuum concentration gives extensive entropy bounds, quantitative fidelity obstructions for hard electric cutoffs, and necessary growth of retained representation spaces. A sharper local comparison yields complete bounds on fixed discarded-link sectors, while collective projection and Schur estimates isolate the additional input needed for a volume-uniform gap. The work also certifies a finite three-link conditioning functional through Wilson coupling 0.354, with its physical interpretation conditional on the stated block-comparison hypotheses. Exact blocking, corrected effective-potential theorems and a representation-by-representation two-dimensional benchmark clarify which approximations preserve the relevant observables. Published glueball measurements retain their original attribution; their reanalysis and the reproduced winding-channel and replica studies have explicitly statistical scope. Complete derivations and reproducible calculations support a set of certified benchmarks and continuum transfer criteria. A nontrivial four-dimensional spatial-cutoff construction and a positive spectral bound along it are not established; no exact continuum mass is asserted.

</details>


### [170] [Universal Critical Relations and Nonuniversal Scaling in Inverse-Square Ising Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11546)
*Dyuman Bhattacharya*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有特定长程相互作用的铁磁Ising链，在临界外场下，连通关联函数由过剩磁化率决定；发现其关联呈反对数衰减并伴随平方根尖点，当引入相对修正时可出现连续变化的对数幂律和线性尖点；最后给出了无序磁化率的下界，排除了标准Kosterlitz–Thouless行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解长程相互作用一维铁磁系统在临界点附近的关联结构与相变普适类，特别是超越短程Ising和KT范式的新型临界行为。

Method: 基于严格解析方法，结合临界外场下的过剩磁化展开、对数积分判据、精确尾部渐近分析及无序磁化率的下界估计。

Result: 1) 连通关联由临界过剩磁化决定；2) 缓变磁化剖面具有有限对数积分当且仅当有序热斜率有限；3) 任意有限非负核给出反-log关联与√-cusp；4) 相对修正c/log(n+e)（c>1）导致连续对数幂律与线性cusp；5) 无序磁化率存在严格下界，否定标准KT标度律。

Conclusion: 该模型展现了一类新型一维长程临界现象，其关联衰减、奇异性及响应函数均不满足传统KT或平均场描述，而由对数标度与渐近尾部精确控制。

Abstract: For ferromagnetic Ising chains with $n^2J_n\to B>0$, the critical excess magnetization in a field inversely proportional to spin separation determines the connected correlation. Their slowly varying profile has finite logarithmic integral exactly when the ordered thermal slope is finite. For any finite nonnegative core, an exact tail gives inverse-logarithmic correlations and a square-root cusp. A relative correction $c/\log(n+e)$ with $c>1$ gives continuously varying logarithmic powers and a linear cusp. A lower bound on its disordered susceptibility excludes the usual Kosterlitz--Thouless law.

</details>


### [171] [Lieb-Robinson bound for one-dimensional continuum fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11655)
*Marius Lemm,Hailun Lu,Harman Preet Singh*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次为一维连续费米子系统建立了Lieb-Robinson界（LRB），适用于具有受控局域粒子数与能量矩的初态，从而实现热力学稳定性；该结果突破了将局域性工具推广至连续系统的主要技术障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服量子多体物理中希尔伯特空间维数随自由度指数增长的困难，将离散系统中成熟的局域性工具（如Lieb-Robinson界）拓展至真实连续系统。

Method: 通过构造适用于一维连续费米子系统的传播子估计与算符局部化技术，结合对初态局域粒子数和能量矩的控制，严格证明了Lieb-Robinson型对易子衰减界。

Result: 首次在标准一维连续费米子模型中建立了Lieb-Robinson界，其适用初态满足热力学稳定性条件（即局域粒子数与能量矩有界），且传播速度为有限常数。

Conclusion: 该工作打通了将基于局域性的解析与数值方法（如MPS、light-cone动力学、准绝热演化等）系统应用于连续量子场论与冷原子实验建模的关键路径，标志着连续多体系统严格局域性理论的重大突破。

Abstract: The central difficulty in quantum many-body physics is that the Hilbert space dimension grows exponentially with the total degrees of freedom. A key feature to circumvent this problem is locality, which manifests dynamically through an approximate causal structure in the form of a Lieb-Robinson bound (LRB). LRBs have been proved for quantum spin systems and certain lattice boson systems, but so far never for realistic continuum systems. We present the first LRB that applies to a standard system of one-dimensional continuum fermions. It applies to initial states with controlled moments of local particle number and energy and is thus thermodynamically stable. Our result breaks the main technical barrier that has existed for using the vast locality toolbox on continuum systems.

</details>


### [172] [Projective symmetries and differential recursions for the large-$N$ Gross--Witten--Wadia and one-dimensional QCD matrix models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11670)
*S. Voloshyn*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文比较了固定电荷平面Gross--Witten--Wadia模型与无源一维QCD在大N极限及N_f/N固定下的结构；发现二者自由能导数均满足代数方程（GWW为四次，QCD为六次），并借助Toeplitz行列式、S_4对称性、微分递推关系、Narayana三角形与平面二部树等数学结构建立统一射影描述；多味标度极限下六次方程退化为四次，保留横向射影数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示固定电荷GWW模型与1D QCD在大N和固定N_f/N极限下的深层代数与对称性联系，统一其可积结构与组合解释。

Method: 利用解析延拓下的resolvent与单割条件导出自由能导数的代数方程；结合有限N Toeplitz行列式验证QCD分支选择；引入齐次参数三元组的带号置换实现S_4作用；建立基于六次方程的微分递推与PDE重构方案；分析Narayana三角形边界与坍缩截面的组合意义。

Result: GWW与1D QCD共享S_4射影结构；QCD自由能导数满足六次代数方程，导出二阶PDE与跨两行的小电荷递推；Narayana三角形构成其系数金字塔的自然边界；坍缩截面对应带标记的平面二部树，具精确对称积公式；多味极限下六次退化为四次，且QCD除差三元组在此边界发生共点，而极限四次方程满足完整标准GWW三阶ODE系统。

Conclusion: 两类模型虽物理起源不同，但在代数几何（六次vs四次曲线）、微分结构（二阶PDE vs 三阶ODE）、组合表示（Narayana三角形与平面二部树）及对称性（S_4射影作用）层面存在深刻统一；该统一由固定N_f/N大N极限与电荷约束共同决定。

Abstract: We compare the fixed-charge planar Gross--Witten--Wadia (GWW) model with source-free one-dimensional QCD at large $N$ and $N_f/N$ fixed. In both models the resolvent and one-cut condition give an algebraic equation for a derivative of the free energy: a quartic for GWW and a sextic for QCD. Finite-$N$ Toeplitz determinants independently check the selected QCD branch. Signed permutations of homogeneous parameter triples realize $S_4$; the resulting action on the algebraic branches and operator labels gives a common projective description of the two models. For QCD, the sextic yields a differential recurrence linking small-charge rows two apart. The same second-order PDE gives a finite-width column recursion that reconstructs the coefficient pyramid from its GWW boundary. A distinct boundary, obtained from the first resummed coefficient, is the Narayana triangle. A formally collapsed slice counts decorated bipartite plane trees and admits an exact symmetric product formula. The many-flavor scaling reduces the sextic to the quartic and retains transverse projective data: the QCD divided-difference triple coalesces at this boundary, whereas the limiting quartic satisfies the full standard GWW third-order system. We distinguish algebraic degree, mixed differential order, and fixed-parameter ordinary differential order throughout.

</details>


### [173] [Transformations of Hilbert Module Reference Frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11683)
*Lucus Brady*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过函子性刻画了希尔伯特模上的经典与量子参考系，构建了等变耦合与希尔伯特模参考系的范畴，并证明该构造构成一个函子；进一步导出了固定希尔伯特模动力系统的‘酉’群胚作为参考系变换，并给出了两类具体例子：不可区分的经典二态开关与希尔伯特空间情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为统一处理经典与量子参考系的结构与对称性，需发展一种范畴论框架，尤其适用于具有模结构的动力系统（如非交换几何或量子引力背景中的参考系问题）。

Method: 采用算子代数与范畴论方法，特别是C*-代数上的希尔伯特模理论、等变映射与2-范畴结构；定义参考系为满足特定协变性的对象，并利用函子性建立其分类与变换群胚。

Result: 1) 希尔伯特模参考系被函子性地表征；2) 构造出等变耦合与参考系的范畴Cat^{eq}；3) 参考系系统构造是该范畴到Hilbert C*-模范畴的函子；4) 对固定动力系统，其参考系变换形成酉群胚G_{ref}；5) 在二态经典开关与标准希尔伯特空间中实现该群胚。

Conclusion: 希尔伯特模参考系具有天然的函子性与群胚对称性，该框架可推广至非交换时空与量子参考系叠加的研究，为量子参照系理论提供严格数学基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we provide a functorial characterization of Hilbert module classical and quantum reference frames. Using the functorial characterization, we construct a category for equivariant couplings and Hilbert module reference frames. We prove the construction of the reference frame system from the equivariant coupling data yields a functor. We also deduce a ``unitary" groupoid as frame transformations for Hilbert module reference frames of a fixed Hilbert module dynamical system. We provide some examples of these frame transformations in the case of the indistinguishable classical two state switch and in the case of Hilbert spaces.

</details>


### [174] [Certified finite-precision readout for $p$-adic kernel aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12254)
*Su Hu,Min-Soo Kim*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了非阿基米德数值分析中基于p进范数的有限精度认证框架，为振幅族读出、p进指数和、多类p进分类器及p进路径积分提供严格、精确且可计算的精度保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在p进数值计算中，传统浮点近似无法保证下游决策（如分类、极值判定）的数学严格性；需发展能在有限精度下即刻认证结果正确性的新理论工具。

Method: 运用p进赋值v(·)与绝对值|·|_p的离散性，构造基于已解析位集（resolved set）的有限精度证书；结合中心矩展开、赋值阶比较与总变差分解，建立不依赖完整数值展开的精确判定条件。

Result: 1) 范数读出：在精度v_*+1下可精确确定所有最大值点（含并列）；2) p进指数和：给出中心矩证书及显式余项界，仅用有理运算即可判定非零性；3) 多类分类器：认证阈值为v^{(2)}+1（第二高判别量赋值），无需解析胜出判别量本身；4) 应用于离散p进路径积分，证得零有效二次耦合下的输出独立性。

Conclusion: p进数值分析可实现‘早停即确证’（early-certification）范式：有限位解析即足以保障下游数学结论的绝对正确性，为p进机器学习与量子引力计算提供可验证基础。

Abstract: In non-archimedean numerical analysis, a verified $p$-adic approximation can determine an absolute value, and hence downstream probabilistic rankings and classification decisions, with mathematical exactness long before the underlying number is fully resolved. We turn this observation into rigorous finite-precision certificates for norm-based readouts of amplitude families. For a common error radius, a nonempty resolved set already fixes every exact maximizer, ties included, at the sharp integer absolute precision $v_*+1$, and conditioning on that set yields an exact total-variation identity separating unresolved $p$-adic mass from real rounding error. For aggregates of $p$-adic exponentials, we give a centered-moment certificate with an explicit remainder bound: on rational data it runs in exact rational arithmetic without individual exponential evaluations, attains its minimal bound at an active phase, and resolves every nonzero aggregate at high order; we also obtain an aggregate perturbation bound free of summand-count factors. For multiclass $p$-adic classifiers that decode by minimizing the norm of a class discriminant, we prove a necessary and sufficient certification condition and show that the sharp common precision threshold is $v^{(2)}+1$, governed by the second-largest discriminant valuation, so the winning discriminant need not itself be resolved. We apply these certificates to discrete $p$-adic path-integral actions on uniform affine chains, deriving explicit action centers and proving exact output-independence at zero effective quadratic coupling. Pseudocode and a reproducible reference implementation accompany the theory.

</details>


### [175] [On transition probabilities for the ASEP on the half-line](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12258)
*Alexandr Garbali,Cengiz Gazi,Jan de Gier,William Mead,Michael Wheeler*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究半直线上的非对称简单排斥过程（ASEP），在边界处仅插入粒子而无移除；推导并证明了描述相同粒子数初末构型间跃迁概率的两种不同多重围道积分公式，其中一种为文献已知，另一种为新证；利用坐标Bethe ansatz验证新公式的演化方程满足性与初值条件，并应用该公式求得至给定时刻粒子数守恒的概率积分表达式；技术核心为对称有理函数及其围道形变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解边界驱动非平衡统计系统中粒子数守恒动力学，特别是半无限格点上带单侧注入的ASEP模型的精确可解结构与概率演化性质。

Method: 坐标Bethe ansatz、多重围道积分表示、对称有理函数理论、复平面上的围道形变技巧。

Result: 1) 给出并严格证明了一个新的多重围道积分形式的跃迁概率公式；2) 验证其满足Kolmogorov前向方程及初始δ函数条件；3) 对末态求和得到粒子数守恒概率的显式积分表达式。

Conclusion: ASEP在半直线单边注入边界条件下的跃迁概率具有双重围道积分表示，二者等价但解析结构互补；该结构揭示了模型深层的对称函数代数与可积性，并为计算守恒量概率提供新工具。

Abstract: We study the asymmetric simple exclusion process on the half-line with particle insertion and no particle removal at the boundary. We point out that there exist two distinct multiple contour integral formulas for the transition probability between two finite particle configurations containing the same number of particles. We recall one of these formulas from the literature and prove a second, new formula by showing that it satisfies the evolution equation using a coordinate Bethe ansatz calculation and verify that the initial condition is implemented by the contour integrals. As an application we sum this formula over all final configurations and obtain an integral formula for the probability that the number of particles is conserved up to a given time. The proofs use symmetric rational functions and their properties together with contour deformations.

</details>


### [176] [An Algebraic Foundation of Rate-Product Style Quantity Calculus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12428)
*Dan Jonsson*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过引入可扩展（右）幺半群-堆的新概念，推广了文献[1]中的量纲演算，使其能够涵盖不可相乘的物理量，并同时处理（弱）速率与量的乘积。文中给出了计量学与物理学中的若干应用实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展传统量纲演算的适用范围，以容纳不可乘物理量及更一般的代数结构（如弱速率、量积），满足现代计量学与基础物理建模的需求。

Method: 引入可扩展（右）幺半群-堆（scalable (right) monoid-heap）作为量纲代数的范畴论框架，构建广义量空间及其运算结构，严格定义弱速率与量积的合成规则。

Result: 建立了兼容不可乘性、弱速率和量积的广义量纲演算体系；给出了该框架在单位一致性验证、导出量定义及物理定律形式化中的具体应用。

Conclusion: 可扩展幺半群-堆为量纲分析提供了更普适的代数基础，统一处理传统SI量制无法涵盖的非乘性物理关系，增强了物理理论与计量实践之间的数学严谨性。

Abstract: Using the new notion of a scalable (right) monoid-heap, the quantity calculus in [1] is generalized to include quantities that may not be possible to multiply as well as both (weak) rates and products of quantities. Several examples are given of applications in metrology and physics

</details>


### [177] [Real-Time Path Integral Resurgence: Which Classical Path Contributes?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12510)
*Job Feldbrugge*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用 resurgence 理论替代 Picard-Lefschetz 理论，通过高阶渐近展开、Borel-Padé 分析与 South-East 规则，系统识别实时间 Feynman 路径积分中起关键作用的复经典路径（包括量子隧穿路径），并在 Rosen-Morse 势垒模型中验证其指数精度；方法涵盖 Feynman 图展开、累积量输运递归及 Stokes 现象追踪；结果表明该框架可自然推广至受 Hamilton 约束的相对论性路径积分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Picard-Lefschetz 理论虽原则上可确定实时间路径积分中贡献显著的复经典路径（即 steepest descent 路径/ thimbles），但在无限维函数空间中计算 thimble 交截数极为困难；亟需一种实用、可计算的替代方案来判定复经典解的相关性（relevance）。

Method: 采用 resurgence 分析：1) 对实/复经典路径作用量展开高阶渐近级数（用 Feynman 图与累积量输运递归实现）；2) 利用 Borel-Padé 方法解析发散尾部，提取路径间 Stokes 相邻性（adjacency）；3) 应用 South-East rule 将相邻性转化为物理相关性（relevance）；4) 追踪终态参数变化下的普通与高阶 Stokes 现象。

Result: 在 Rosen-Morse 势垒模型中，精确识别出势垒前/后所有相关复经典路径（含主导量子隧穿的复路径）；完整刻画了随终态位置移动发生的 Stokes 线穿越与高阶 Stokes 现象；经与精确传播子比对，Borel- resummed 级数达到指数精度；并指出该方法可推广至受 Hamilton 约束的相对论性路径积分（如引力或共形场论中的约束系统）。

Conclusion: Resurgence 提供了一种切实可行、无需显式构造 thimbles 的复路径选择机制，将路径相关性问题转化为可计算的渐近分析与 Stokes 几何问题；为实时量子动力学、量子隧穿及量子引力中的路径积分提供了新的系统性计算范式。

Abstract: Which complex classical paths contribute to a real-time Feynman path integral? Picard-Lefschetz theory answers this question with the intersection numbers of the thimbles, which are notoriously hard to compute in infinite dimensions. We show that resurgence provides a practical alternative. We compute the asymptotic series of the real and complex classical paths to high order, with Feynman diagrams and with a transport recursion for the cumulants, and read off the adjacency of the classical paths from the divergent tails of the series with Borel-Padé methods. The South-East rule then turns adjacency into relevance. For a particle meeting a Rosen-Morse barrier, we identify the relevant complex classical paths in front of and behind the barrier, including the complex path that carries quantum tunnelling, follow ordinary and higher-order Stokes phenomena as the final point moves, and verify the predictions with the exact propagator. The resummed series reproduce the propagator with exponential accuracy. Finally, we outline how the expansion extends to relativistic path integrals governed by a Hamiltonian constraint.

</details>


### [178] [The finite projective classification of complex spinors via momentum maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12549)
*Bartłomiej Sikorski,Adam Sawicki*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用动量映射方法研究复旋量的射影轨道分类，覆盖奇维（1–11）不可约旋表示与偶维（2–14）半旋表示；通过Clifford–Fock模型构造临界点代表元，结合Vinberg分次李代数理论，统一推导轨道数目、射影维数及14维情形下的闭包序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对复旋量在Spin群作用下的射影轨道进行系统、几何化分类，摆脱传统基于不变量或Case-by-case枚举的方法，寻求由辛几何（动量映射）与代数结构（分次李代数）驱动的统一框架。

Method: 以动量映射μ为工具，将轨道分类转化为其平方范数‖μ‖²的临界点分析；在Clifford–Fock实现中显式构造纯基旋量之和形式的代表元，使其为‖μ‖²的临界点；利用Vinberg关于θ-群（即Z-分次半单李代数g=⊕g_i中G₀作用于g₁）的轨道理论保证完备性；通过固定Weyl腔中μ值区分轨道，并用‖μ‖²单调性刻画闭包序。

Result: 完整给出1–14维（奇维至11、偶维至14）所有具有有限多轨道的旋表示中的轨道数目（吻合经典结果：奇维1,1,1,2,3,5；偶维1,1,1,2,2,4,9），计算每条轨道的射影维数，并首次确定14维半旋表示中轨道的闭包偏序关系（由‖μ‖²严格递增判定）。

Conclusion: 动量映射的临界点方法与Vinberg分次李代数理论相结合，为旋量轨道分类提供了内蕴、统一且可推广的辛代数框架，不仅重构经典结果，还实现了新结构（如闭包序）的几何刻画。

Abstract: We describe the projective orbits of complex spinors from the point of view of the momentum map. We treat the irreducible spin modules in odd dimensions 1,3,5,7,9,11 and the half-spin modules in even dimensions 2,4,6,8,10,12,14, which are the only cases with finitely many orbits. In the Clifford--Fock model, we construct representatives as sums of pure basis spinors, and the coefficients are chosen so that the representative is a critical point of the squared norm of the momentum map. The resulting momentum values in a fixed Weyl chamber distinguish the orbits. Completeness follows from Vinberg's theory of graded Lie algebras and stabilizer actions, independently of the classical spinor classifications. We recover the classical counts of 1,1,1,2,3,5 orbits in the odd cases and 1,1,1,2,2,4,9 in the even cases, and compute the projective dimension of every orbit. In dimension fourteen we also determine the closure order of the orbits, using the fact that the minimum of the squared momentum norm increases strictly under degeneration.

</details>


### [179] [One-Particle Resonances and Eigenstate Thermalization in Periodic Free-Fermion Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12701)
*Tristan Benoist,Vojkan Jakšić,Claude-Alain Pillet,Anna Szczepanek,Cornelia Vogel*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了周期性立方晶格上最近邻自由费米子模型中粒子数本征态涨落，证明了粒子数方差的上界为NL^{d-1}，并给出了基于能量转移分解的最优估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解自由费米系统中本征态热化（ETH）行为，特别是粒子数涨落在不同尺度下的统计特性及其与系统尺寸和粒子密度的关系。

Method: 将中心化粒子数算符按单粒子能量转移分解，零转移部分用Hilbert-Schmidt范数估计，非零转移部分利用Mann定理导出的共振界进行控制。

Result: 证明粒子数方差上界为O(NL^{d-1})，相对涨落的ETH估计为O(L^{d-1}/(Nη²))；该界在所有维度下均最优，且由最大非零能隙简并度L^{d-1}所决定。

Conclusion: 自由费米子模型中粒子数本征态涨落满足严格的尺度律，其ETH行为由能谱共振结构与多体态构造共同决定，且所得界不可改进。

Abstract: We study eigenstate fluctuations of the particle number in the nearest-neighbour free-fermion model on the periodic cubic lattice $(\mathbb{Z}/L\mathbb{Z})^d$. For every fixed dimension, every particle number, every subvolume, and every normalized energy eigenstate, we prove a bound of order $NL^{d-1}$ for the particle-number variance. The corresponding eigenstate-thermalization estimate is of order $L^{d-1}/(N\eta^2)$ for fluctuations of relative size $\eta$. In particular, at positive particle density the bound decays as the inverse linear size. The proof decomposes the centered particle-number operator according to one-particle energy transfer and treats the zero- and nonzero-transfer components separately. The nonzero-transfer part is controlled by a sharp resonance bound, proved from Mann's theorem on relations among roots of unity, while the zero-transfer part is estimated by a Hilbert-Schmidt argument. These bounds are optimal in every dimension: the maximal nonzero-gap multiplicity has order $L^{d-1}$, and suitable many-body eigenstates realize matching variance lower bounds and show that the concentration bound cannot be improved in estimates uniform in the tolerance parameter.

</details>


### [180] [Global Asymptotics of a Toda Soliton Condensate with Random Scattering Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12748)
*Guido Mazzuca,Mateusz Piorkowski*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了无限Toda格点上N-孤子凝聚体在N→∞极限下的稠密随机孤子气体行为，首次将离散本征值与归一化常数均设为独立随机变量，并建立了基于概率Riemann–Hilbert问题的自平均理论；通过Deift–Zhou非线性最速下降法，导出了时空平面上五类有限亏格（含椭圆与genus-2）宏观区域的显式渐近解，其界面为非线性曲线，并确立了该孤子凝聚体的非线性色散关系及态密度决定宏观系综平均的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 突破以往孤子气体数学研究中将归一化常数设为确定性或无穷小量的限制，引入具有任意大小但满足有限矩条件的独立随机归一化常数，以刻画更真实的高密度、强涨落孤子系统。

Method: 构建概率Riemann–Hilbert问题框架以实现自平均分析；结合Deift–Zhou非线性最速下降法求解连续极限下的宏观演化；运用有限亏格反散射理论与非线性色散关系推导宏观区域划分及波形调制结构。

Result: 1) 随机Toda变量以O(N^{-δ})（0<δ<1/2）多项式速率收敛至确定性连续模型；2) 时空平面被划分为五个有限亏格区域（含调制椭圆波与genus-2波），界面为非线性曲线，误差为O(1/ln N)；3) 建立Toda孤子凝聚体的非线性色散关系，并证明宏观系综平均由孤子气态密度完全决定。

Conclusion: 随机孤子凝聚体在强涨落下仍呈现鲁棒的宏观确定性结构，其演化由非线性色散关系与态密度支配，揭示了高密度可积系统中随机性与可积性深层协同的本质。

Abstract: We study a dense $N$-soliton condensate for the infinite Toda lattice in the limit $N \to \infty$. In contrast to previous mathematical studies of soliton gases where the norming constants are assumed to be deterministic or infinitesimal, we consider initial scattering data where both the discrete eigenvalues and the norming constants are independent random variables. The norming constants $\gamma_j$ are allowed to be arbitrarily large under a mild moment condition $\mathbb{E}[\gamma_j^\nu] < \infty$ ($\nu > 1$). Our main result is divided into two parts. First, we develop a probabilistic Riemann--Hilbert framework to show that the system self-averages: despite large, independent fluctuations in the initial data, the random Toda lattice variables converge deterministically to an effective continuum model at a polynomial rate $\mathcal{O}(N^{-\delta})$ for $0 < \delta < 1/2$. Second, by performing the Deift--Zhou nonlinear steepest descent analysis of the continuum problem, we obtain the complete leading-order macroscopic description of the lattice across the entire space-time plane. The solution decomposes into five distinct finite-gap regimes, characterized explicitly by modulated elliptic and genus-2 waves with an error of order $\mathcal{O}(1/\ln N)$. In contrast to the straight characteristic rays typical of standard soliton gas limits, the interfaces separating these five macroscopic regimes in the space-time plane are genuinely non-linear curves; in particular, the boundary of the modulated genus-2 region is completely non-linear. Finally, under our assumptions, we establish the Nonlinear Dispersion Relations for the Toda soliton condensate and prove that the macroscopic ensemble averages are determined by the soliton gas density of states.

</details>


### [181] [Eigenvector Occupation Statistics and Extremes in the Rosenzweig-Porter Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12858)
*Yan V. Fyodorov,Mariya Shcherbina,Tatyana Shcherbina*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究GUE Rosenzweig-Porter模型中归一化体态本征矢的占据概率分布，通过条件化单本征对Kac-Rice律导出有限N下的投影密度，并利用Hubbard-Stratonovich变换与鞍点法得到自洽Lorentz型占据剖面；证明了广义逆参与率和振幅计数谱的渐近行为，揭示q=1/2处的相变、两端极值的Poisson点过程极限（Gumbel与指数分布），并证伪固定q下统计冻结现象。所有结论为淬火意义下、在对角无序高概规律性事件上一致成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解无序量子系统中本征态的局域化-遍历过渡机制，特别是RP模型这一介于Wigner-Dyson与Anderson极限之间的可解非遍历中间相；探究占据概率的精细统计结构（如极值分布、分形谱、冻结行为）以刻画多体量子混沌与非遍历性的本质特征。

Method: 基于归一化单本征对的Kac-Rice公式进行条件化分析；构造精确有限-N投影密度；引入2×2厄米矩阵上的Hubbard-Stratonovich积分表示；应用鞍点法提取主导渐近贡献；结合随机矩阵理论、大偏差分析与极值统计工具推导广义逆参与率及振幅谱；严格建立淬火意义下的高概规律性事件。

Result: 1) 占据场服从自洽Lorentz分布，有效共振尺寸K_N(E)∼N^{2−γ}，具局域指数涨落；2) 广义逆参与率∑p_i^q呈现q=1/2相变；3) 振幅计数谱呈双支加宽结构；4) 最大分量服从Gumbel极值分布，最小分量在尺度N^{−1−γ}下收敛于指数分布；5) 对任意固定q>0，最大分量对∑p_i^q的贡献趋于零，故无统计冻结。

Conclusion: RP模型在1<γ<2区间内展现出丰富的非遍历统计结构：其占据概率既非完全遍历（δ-函数型），亦非完全局域（单峰型），而是具有尺度依赖的Lorentz轮廓与强关联极值统计；该结果为中间相本征态的分形性与弱遍历破缺提供了严格数学刻画，并排除了传统意义上基于固定q的冻结判据的有效性。

Abstract: We study the occupation probabilities $p_i=|u_i|^2$ of a normalized bulk eigenvector in the GUE Rosenzweig-Porter model $A_N=B_N+\tau_NG_N$, with $\tau_N^2=N^{1-\gamma}$ and $1<\gamma<2$. Conditioning on an eigenvalue at a regular bulk energy $E$ through the normalized one-eigenpair Kac-Rice law, we derive an exact finite-$N$ projective density and reduce its asymptotic analysis to a Hubbard-Stratonovich integral over $2\times2$ Hermitian matrices, which we evaluate by the saddle-point method. The resulting occupation field is governed by a self-consistent Lorentzian profile with effective resonant size $K_N(E)\asymp N^{2-\gamma}$ and local exponential fluctuations. We prove the asymptotics of all generalized inverse participation sums and identify a transition at $q=1/2$. We also obtain a two-branch thickened amplitude-counting spectrum. At the two ends of the occupation field we prove marked Poisson point-process limits: the largest components have Gumbel fluctuations, whereas the smallest component has an exponential limit on the scale $N^{-1-\gamma}$. Finally, for every fixed $q>0$ the largest component contributes an asymptotically vanishing fraction of the inverse participation sum, showing that these fixed-$q$ statistics do not freeze. The statements are quenched, uniformly on a high-probability regularity event for the diagonal disorder, and hence also hold in the annealed setting.

</details>
